end

gui = script.Parent.Parent.Parent --the Shop frame
tmi = gui.TooManyItems
enabled = gui.Enabled
player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
itempurchase = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ItemBuyRequest

input = game:GetService("UserInputService")
touch = false
taps = 0
if input.TouchEnabled == true then
	touch = true
	gui.TouchNotice.Visible = true
end

function onMouseEnter()
	local desc = script.Parent:findFirstChild("ItemDesc")
	local cost = script.Parent:findFirstChild("ItemCost")
	if desc ~= nil and cost ~= nil then
		gui.Desc.ItemDesc.Text = desc.Value
		gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..cost.Value
		gui.Desc.ItemName.Text = script.Parent.Name
	end
end

function onMouseButton1Click() --clone the item into their backpack
	itemsbought = gui.ItemsBought
	if touch then
		local desc = script.Parent:findFirstChild("ItemDesc")
		local cost = script.Parent:findFirstChild("ItemCost")
		if desc ~= nil and cost ~= nil then
			gui.Desc.ItemDesc.Text = desc.Value
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..cost.Value
			gui.Desc.ItemName.Text = script.Parent.Name
		end
		taps = taps + 1
		if taps == 2 then
			taps = 0
			if enabled.Value == true then
			enabled.Value = false
			if player:DistanceFromCharacter(gui.Parent.Parent.ShopPos.Value) < 25 then
				if player:findFirstChild("Backpack") ~= nil then
					tools = player.Backpack:GetChildren()
					if #tools < 10 then
						
							if itemsbought.Value < 6 then
								local purchase = itempurchase:InvokeServer(gui.Parent.Parent.Name,script.Parent.Name)
								if purchase then
									itemsbought.Value = itemsbought.Value + 1
								else
									tmi.Text = "You do not have enough cash! You can earn some cash over time, or purchase more in the Game Pass Shop."
									tmi.Visible = true
									wait(4)
									tmi.Visible = false
								end
							else
								tmi.Text = "You are buying a lot of items - please wait and give the other customers a chance!"
								tmi.Visible = true
								wait(6)
								tmi.Visible = false
								gui.Parent.Parent:Destroy()
							end
							
						
					else
						tmi.Text = "You have too many items! Use Backspace to drop unwanted items."
						tmi.Visible = true
						wait(4)
						tmi.Visible = false
					end
				end
			else
				tmi.Text = "You have walked too far away from the shopkeeper!"
				tmi.Visible = true
				wait(4)
				tmi.Visible = false
				if player:DistanceFromCharacter(gui.Parent.Parent.ShopPos.Value) >= 25 then
					gui.Parent.Parent:Destroy()
				end
			end
			wait(1)
			enabled.Value = true
		end
		end
		wait(.5)
		if taps > 0 then
			taps = taps - 1
		end
	else
		if enabled.Value == true then
			enabled.Value = false
			if player:DistanceFromCharacter(gui.Parent.Parent.ShopPos.Value) < 25 then
				if player:findFirstChild("Backpack") ~= nil then
					tools = player.Backpack:GetChildren()
					if #tools < 10 then
						
							if itemsbought.Value < 6 then
								local purchase = itempurchase:InvokeServer(gui.Parent.Parent.Name,script.Parent.Name)
								if purchase then
									itemsbought.Value = itemsbought.Value + 1
								else
									tmi.Text = "You do not have enough cash! You can earn some cash over time, or purchase more in the Game Pass Shop."
									tmi.Visible = true
									wait(4)
									tmi.Visible = false
								end
							else
								tmi.Text = "You are buying a lot of items - please wait and give the other customers a chance!"
								tmi.Visible = true
								wait(6)
								tmi.Visible = false
								gui.Parent.Parent:Destroy()
							end
							
						
					else
						tmi.Text = "You have too many items! Use Backspace to drop unwanted items."
						tmi.Visible = true
						wait(4)
						tmi.Visible = false
					end
				end
			else
				tmi.Text = "You have walked too far away from the shopkeeper!"
				tmi.Visible = true
				wait(4)
				tmi.Visible = false
				if player:DistanceFromCharacter(gui.Parent.Parent.ShopPos.Value) >= 25 then
					gui.Parent.Parent:Destroy()
				end
			end
			wait(1)
			enabled.Value = true
		end

	end
end

function slideIn(gui)
	gui:TweenPosition(gui.Position + UDim2.new(1,0,0,0),"Out", "Quad", .6)
end

coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
	while mainframe ~= nil do
		for _,bg in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
			if bg.Name == "BGFrame" then
				local basecolor = {(100 + (bg.Position.Y.Scale * 60))/255,(160 + (bg.Position.Y.Scale * 60))/255,1} --with possible offset of 13
				local newcolor = Color3.new(bg.BackgroundColor3.r + math.random(-1,1)/255,bg.BackgroundColor3.g + math.random(-1,1)/255,1)
				if newcolor.r > basecolor[1] - 15/255 and newcolor.r < basecolor[1] + 15/255 and newcolor.g > basecolor[2] - 15/255 and newcolor.g < basecolor[2] + 15/255 then
					bg.BackgroundColor3 = newcolor
				end
			end
		end
		wait(1/30)
	end
end))

coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
	local versiondata = game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Version")
	local description = versiondata:WaitForChild("Desc")
	if description.Value ~= "" then
		frame.Version.Text = versiondata.Value..": "..description.Value
	else
		frame.Version.Text = versiondata.Value
	end
end))

=== chunk=419 type=PROP offset=44447 runSha256=34f02eccaa6cab7236fbd33c561db2614c830de9930369a81157db481ae06dad ===
--[[
	// FileName: ControlScript.lua
	// Version 1.1
	// Written by: jmargh and jeditkacheff
	// Description: Manages in game controls for both touch and keyboard/mouse devices.
	
	// This script will be inserted into PlayerScripts under each player by default. If you want to
	// create your own custom controls or modify these controls, you must place a script with this
	// name, ControlScript, under StarterPlayer -> PlayerScripts.
	
	// Required Modules:
		ClickToMove
		DPad
		KeyboardMovement
		Thumbpad
		Thumbstick
		TouchJump
		MasterControl
		VehicleController
--]]

--[[ Services ]]--
local ContextActionService = game:GetService('ContextActionService')
local Players = game:GetService('Players')
local UserInputService = game:GetService('UserInputService')
-- Settings and GameSettings are read only
local Settings = UserSettings()
local GameSettings = Settings.GameSettings

-- Issue with play solo? (F6)
while not UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled and not UserInputService.TouchEnabled and not UserInputService.GamepadEnabled do
	wait()
end

--[[ Script Variables ]]--
while not Players.LocalPlayer do
	wait()
end

local lastInputType = nil
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild('PlayerGui')
local IsTouchDevice = UserInputService.TouchEnabled
local UserMovementMode = IsTouchDevice and GameSettings.TouchMovementMode or GameSettings.ComputerMovementMode
local DevMovementMode = IsTouchDevice and LocalPlayer.DevTouchMovementMode or LocalPlayer.DevComputerMovementMode
local IsUserChoice = (IsTouchDevice and DevMovementMode == Enum.DevTouchMovementMode.UserChoice) or (DevMovementMode == Enum.DevComputerMovementMode.UserChoice)
local TouchGui = nil
local TouchControlFrame = nil
local IsModalEnabled = UserInputService.ModalEnabled
local BindableEvent_OnFailStateChanged = nil
local isJumpEnabled = false

local ControlState = {}
ControlState.Current = nil
function ControlState:SwitchTo(newControl)
	if ControlState.Current == newControl then return end
	
	if ControlState.Current then
		ControlState.Current:Disable()
	end
	
	ControlState.Current = newControl
	
	if ControlState.Current then
		ControlState.Current:Enable()
	end
end

--[[ Modules ]]--
local ClickToMoveTouchControls = nil
local ControlModules = {}

local MasterControl = require(script:WaitForChild('MasterControl'))

local ThumbstickModule = require(script.MasterControl:WaitForChild('Thumbstick'))
local ThumbpadModule = require(script.MasterControl:WaitForChild('Thumbpad'))
local DPadModule = require(script.MasterControl:WaitForChild('DPad'))
local DefaultModule = ControlModules.Thumbstick
local TouchJumpModule = require(script.MasterControl:WaitForChild('TouchJump'))

local keyboardModule = require(script.MasterControl:WaitForChild('KeyboardMovement'))
ControlModules.Gamepad = require(script.MasterControl:WaitForChild('Gamepad'))

function getTouchModule()
	local module = nil
	if not IsUserChoice then
		if DevMovementMode == Enum.DevTouchMovementMode.Thumbstick then
			module = ThumbstickModule
			isJumpEnabled = true
		elseif DevMovementMode == Enum.DevTouchMovementMode.Thumbpad then
			module = ThumbpadModule
			isJumpEnabled = true
		elseif DevMovementMode == Enum.DevTouchMovementMode.DPad then
			module = DPadModule
			isJumpEnabled = false
		elseif DevMovementMode == Enum.DevTouchMovementMode.ClickToMove then
			-- Managed by CameraScript
			module = nil
		elseif DevMovementMode == Enum.DevTouchMovementMode.Scriptable then
			module = nil
		end
	else
		if UserMovementMode == Enum.TouchMovementMode.Default or UserMovementMode == Enum.TouchMovementMode.Thumbstick then
			module = ThumbstickModule
			isJumpEnabled = true
		elseif UserMovementMode == Enum.TouchMovementMode.Thumbpad then
			module = ThumbpadModule
			isJumpEnabled = true
		elseif UserMovementMode == Enum.TouchMovementMode.DPad then
			module = DPadModule
			isJumpEnabled = false
		elseif UserMovementMode == Enum.TouchMovementMode.ClickToMove then
			-- Managed by CameraScript
			module = nil
		end
	end
	
	return module
end

function setJumpModule(isEnabled)
	if not isEnabled then
		TouchJumpModule:Disable()
	elseif ControlState.Current == ControlModules.Touch then
		TouchJumpModule:Enable()
	end
end

function setClickToMove()
	if DevMovementMode == Enum.DevTouchMovementMode.ClickToMove or DevMovementMode == Enum.DevComputerMovementMode.ClickToMove or
		UserMovementMode == Enum.ComputerMovementMode.ClickToMove or UserMovementMode == Enum.TouchMovementMode.ClickToMove then
		--
		if lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			ClickToMoveTouchControls = ControlState.Current
		end
	elseif ClickToMoveTouchControls then
		ClickToMoveTouchControls:Disable()
		ClickToMoveTouchControls = nil
	end
end

ControlModules.Touch = {}
ControlModules.Touch.Current = nil
ControlModules.Touch.LocalPlayerChangedCon = nil
ControlModules.Touch.GameSettingsChangedCon = nil

function ControlModules.Touch:RefreshControlStyle()
	if ControlModules.Touch.Current then
		ControlModules.Touch.Current:Disable()
	end
	setJumpModule(false)
	TouchJumpModule:Disable()
	ControlModules.Touch:Enable()
end
function ControlModules.Touch:DisconnectEvents()
	if ControlModules.Touch.LocalPlayerChangedCon then
		ControlModules.Touch.LocalPlayerChangedCon:disconnect()
		ControlModules.Touch.LocalPlayerChangedCon = nil
	end
	if ControlModules.Touch.GameSettingsChangedCon then
		ControlModules.Touch.GameSettingsChangedCon:disconnect()
		ControlModules.Touch.GameSettingsChangedCon = nil
	end
end
function ControlModules.Touch:Enable()
	DevMovementMode = LocalPlayer.DevTouchMovementMode
	IsUserChoice = DevMovementMode == Enum.DevTouchMovementMode.UserChoice
	if IsUserChoice then
		UserMovementMode = GameSettings.TouchMovementMode
	end
		
	local newModuleToEnable = getTouchModule()
	if newModuleToEnable then
		setClickToMove()
		setJumpModule(isJumpEnabled)
		
		newModuleToEnable:Enable()
		ControlModules.Touch.Current = newModuleToEnable
				
		if isJumpEnabled then TouchJumpModule:Enable() end
	end
	
	-- This being within the above if statement was causing issues with ClickToMove, which isn't a module within this script.
	ControlModules.Touch:DisconnectEvents()
	ControlModules.Touch.LocalPlayerChangedCon = LocalPlayer.Changed:connect(function(property)
		if property == 'DevTouchMovementMode' then
			ControlModules.Touch:RefreshControlStyle()
		end
	end)
	
	ControlModules.Touch.GameSettingsChangedCon = GameSettings.Changed:connect(function(property)
		if property == 'TouchMovementMode' then
			ControlModules.Touch:RefreshControlStyle()
		end
	end)
end
function ControlModules.Touch:Disable()
	ControlModules.Touch:DisconnectEvents()
	
	local newModuleToDisable = getTouchModule()
	
	if newModuleToDisable == ThumbstickModule or
		newModuleToDisable == DPadModule or
		newModuleToDisable == ThumbpadModule then
			newModuleToDisable:Disable()
			setJumpModule(false)
			TouchJumpModule:Disable()
	end
end

local function getKeyboardModule()
	-- NOTE: Click to move still uses keyboard. Leaving cases in case this ever changes.
	local whichModule = nil
	if not IsUserChoice then
		if DevMovementMode == Enum.DevComputerMovementMode.KeyboardMouse then
			whichModule = keyboardModule
		elseif DevMovementMode == Enum.DevComputerMovementMode.ClickToMove then
			-- Managed by CameraScript
			whichModule = keyboardModule
		end 
	else
		if UserMovementMode == Enum.ComputerMovementMode.KeyboardMouse or UserMovementMode == Enum.ComputerMovementMode.Default then
			whichModule = keyboardModule
		elseif UserMovementMode == Enum.ComputerMovementMode.ClickToMove then
			-- Managed by CameraScript
			whichModule = keyboardModule
		end
	end

	return whichModule
end

ControlModules.Keyboard = {}
function ControlModules.Keyboard:RefreshControlStyle()
	ControlModules.Keyboard:Disable()
	ControlModules.Keyboard:Enable()
end
function ControlModules.Keyboard:Enable()
	DevMovementMode = LocalPlayer.DevComputerMovementMode
	IsUserChoice = DevMovementMode == Enum.DevComputerMovementMode.UserChoice
	if IsUserChoice then
		UserMovementMode = GameSettings.ComputerMovementMode
	end
		
	local newModuleToEnable = getKeyboardModule()
	if newModuleToEnable then
		newModuleToEnable:Enable()
	end
	
	ControlModules.Keyboard:DisconnectEvents()
	ControlModules.Keyboard.LocalPlayerChangedCon = LocalPlayer.Changed:connect(function(property)
		if property == 'DevComputerMovementMode' then
			ControlModules.Keyboard:RefreshControlStyle()
		end
	end)
	
	ControlModules.Keyboard.GameSettingsChangedCon = GameSettings.Changed:connect(function(property)
		if property == 'ComputerMovementMode' then
			ControlModules.Keyboard:RefreshControlStyle()
		end
	end)
end
function ControlModules.Keyboard:DisconnectEvents()
	if ControlModules.Keyboard.LocalPlayerChangedCon then
		ControlModules.Keyboard.LocalPlayerChangedCon:disconnect()
		ControlModules.Keyboard.LocalPlayerChangedCon = nil
	end
	if ControlModules.Keyboard.GameSettingsChangedCon then
		ControlModules.Keyboard.GameSettingsChangedCon:disconnect()
		ControlModules.Keyboard.GameSettingsChangedCon = nil
	end
end
function ControlModules.Keyboard:Disable()
	ControlModules.Keyboard:DisconnectEvents()
	local newModuleToDisable = getKeyboardModule()
	if newModuleToDisable then
		newModuleToDisable:Disable()
	end
end

if IsTouchDevice then
	BindableEvent_OnFailStateChanged = script.Parent:WaitForChild('OnClickToMoveFailStateChange')
end

-- not used, but needs to be required
local VehicleController = require(script.MasterControl:WaitForChild('VehicleController'))


--[[ Initialization/Setup ]]--
local function createTouchGuiContainer()
	if TouchGui then TouchGui:Destroy() end
	
	-- Container for all touch device guis
	TouchGui = Instance.new('ScreenGui')
	TouchGui.Name = "TouchGui"
	TouchGui.Parent = PlayerGui
	
	TouchControlFrame = Instance.new('Frame')
	TouchControlFrame.Name = "TouchControlFrame"
	TouchControlFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	TouchControlFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	TouchControlFrame.Parent = TouchGui
	
	ThumbstickModule:Create(TouchControlFrame)
	DPadModule:Create(TouchControlFrame)
	ThumbpadModule:Create(TouchControlFrame)
	TouchJumpModule:Create(TouchControlFrame)
end

--[[ Settings Changed Connections ]]--
LocalPlayer.Changed:connect(function(property)
	if lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and property == 'DevTouchMovementMode' then
		ControlState:SwitchTo(ControlModules.Touch)
	elseif UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled and property == 'DevComputerMovementMode' then
		ControlState:SwitchTo(ControlModules.Keyboard)
	end
end)

GameSettings.Changed:connect(function(property)
	if not IsUserChoice then return end
	if property == 'TouchMovementMode' or property == 'ComputerMovementMode' then
		UserMovementMode = GameSettings[property]
		if property == 'TouchMovementMode' then
			ControlState:SwitchTo(ControlModules.Touch)
		elseif property == 'ComputerMovementMode' then
			ControlState:SwitchTo(ControlModules.Keyboard)
		end
	end
end)

--[[ Touch Events ]]--
-- On touch devices we need to recreate the guis on character load.
local lastControlState = nil
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:connect(function(character)
	if ControlState.Current then -- only do this if it wasn't done through CharacterRemoving
		lastControlState = ControlState.Current
		ControlState:SwitchTo(nil)
	end
	
	if UserInputService.TouchEnabled then
		createTouchGuiContainer()
	end
	
	if ControlState.Current == nil then
		ControlState:SwitchTo(lastControlState)
	end
end)

LocalPlayer.CharacterRemoving:connect(function()
	lastControlState = ControlState.Current
	ControlState:SwitchTo(nil)
end)
	
UserInputService.Changed:connect(function(property)
	if property == 'ModalEnabled' then
		IsModalEnabled = UserInputService.ModalEnabled

		if lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			if ControlState.Current == ControlModules.Touch and IsModalEnabled then
				ControlState:SwitchTo(nil)
			elseif ControlState.Current == nil and not IsModalEnabled then
				ControlState:SwitchTo(ControlModules.Touch)
			end
		end
	end
end)

if BindableEvent_OnFailStateChanged then
	BindableEvent_OnFailStateChanged.Event:connect(function(isOn)
		if lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch and ClickToMoveTouchControls then
			if isOn then
				ControlState:SwitchTo(ClickToMoveTouchControls)
			else
				ControlState:SwitchTo(nil)
			end
		end
	end)
end

local switchToInputType = function(newLastInputType)
	lastInputType = newLastInputType
	
	if lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				ControlState:SwitchTo(ControlModules.Touch)
	elseif lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard or
			lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or
			lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton2 or
			lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton3 or
			lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseWheel or
			lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement  then
				ControlState:SwitchTo(ControlModules.Keyboard)
	elseif lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 or
			lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad2 or
			lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad3 or
			lastInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad4 then
				ControlState:SwitchTo(ControlModules.Gamepad)
	end
end

if IsTouchDevice then
	createTouchGuiContainer()
end

MasterControl:Init()

UserInputService.GamepadDisconnected:connect(function(gamepadEnum)
	local connectedGamepads = UserInputService:GetConnectedGamepads()
	if #connectedGamepads > 0 then return end
	
	if UserInputService.KeyboardEnabled then
		ControlState:SwitchTo(ControlModules.Keyboard)
	elseif IsTouchDevice then
		ControlState:SwitchTo(ControlModules.Touch)
	end
end)

UserInputService.GamepadConnected:connect(function(gamepadEnum)
	ControlState:SwitchTo(ControlModules.Gamepad)
end)

switchToInputType(UserInputService:GetLastInputType())
UserInputService.LastInputTypeChanged:connect(switchToInputType)

--// wonuf -- Probably really messy, but it works..so :D

script.Name = "core"

local Player = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local Mouse = Player:GetMouse()

--// Settings

local SplashSound = 142431247

local rFunction = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.fishUpdate

if not game.Workspace:findFirstChild("FishRodBin") then
	Bin = Instance.new("Backpack", game.Workspace)
	Bin.Name = "FishRodBin"
else
	Bin = game.Workspace.FishRodBin
end

local Tool = script.Parent
local WireStart = Tool:WaitForChild("Tip")

--// Initialisation

local Part, Wire, Catching, Enabled = nil, nil, false, false

--// Functions

function DrawFromPoints(a, b)
	if Wire then
		Wire:Destroy()
	end
	Wire = Instance.new('Part',Bin)
	Wire.CanCollide = false
	Wire.Anchored = true
	Wire.BrickColor = BrickColor.new('Light stone grey')
	Wire.FormFactor = Enum.FormFactor.Custom
	Wire.Size = Vector3.new(.4, 0, .2)
	
	local Mesh = Instance.new('CylinderMesh', Wire)
	Mesh.Scale = Vector3.new(1, 1, 0.3)
	
	Wire.Size = Vector3.new(1,(a - b).magnitude, 0.3)
	Wire.CFrame = CFrame.new(a, b) * CFrame.new(0, 0, -(a - b).magnitude / 2) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90),0,0)
	
	game.Debris:AddItem(Wire, 60)
end

function throw()
	if Mouse.Hit and Mouse.Target and Mouse.Target.Name == "Water" then
		DrawFromPoints(WireStart.Position, Mouse.Hit.p)
		local P = Instance.new("Part")
		P.Name = Player.Name.."Part"
		P.Anchored = true
		P.Shape = "Ball"
		P.Size = Vector3.new(1,1,1)
		P.BrickColor = BrickColor.Red()
		P.CanCollide = false
		P.Transparency = 0
		P.TopSurface = "Smooth"
		P.BottomSurface = "Smooth"
		P.CFrame = CFrame.new(Mouse.Hit.p + Vector3.new(0,.2,0))
		P.Parent = Bin
		Part = P
		game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(Part,60)
	end
end

function bob()
	if Enabled then
		Catching = true
		local s = Instance.new("Sparkles", Part)
		game.Debris:AddItem(s, 1)
		if not Player.PlayerGui:findFirstChild("BobSound") then
			local x = Instance.new("Sound", Player.PlayerGui)
			x.Name = "BobSound"
			x.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..SplashSound
			x:play()
			game.Debris:AddItem(x, 2)
		end
		delay(1, function() if Catching and Enabled and Part and Wire then 
			Enabled, Catching = false, false Part:Destroy() Wire:Destroy() wait(1)
			end 
		end)
	end
end

function vowelCheck(fish)
	local vowels = {"a","e","i","o","u"}
	for i,v in pairs(vowels) do
		if string.sub(string.lower(fish),1,1) == v then
			return true
		end
	end
end

function catch()
	local fish,price = rFunction:InvokeServer(Player.userId)
	local gui = Instance.new("ScreenGui",game.Players.LocalPlayer.PlayerGui)
	game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(gui,3)
	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	label.Position = UDim2.new(.2,0,1,0)
	label.Size = UDim2.new(.6,0,0,50)
	if vowelCheck(fish) then
		label.Text = "You caught an "..fish.."! (+$"..price..")"
	else
		label.Text = "You caught a "..fish.."! (+$"..price..")"
	end
	label.TextColor3 = Color3.new(0,214/255,1)
	label.TextStrokeTransparency = .3
	label.Font = "SourceSansBold"
	label.FontSize = "Size48"
	label.Parent = gui
	label:TweenPosition(UDim2.new(.2,0,.25,0),"Out", "Quad", .3)
	wait(2.4)
	label:TweenPosition(UDim2.new(.2,0,0,-60),"In", "Quad", .3)
end

--// Main

Tool.Equipped:connect(function(Mouser)
	Mouser.Button1Down:connect(function()
		if not Catching then
			if not Enabled and not Catching then
				if Mouse.Hit and Mouse.Target and Mouse.Target.Name == "Water" then
					Enabled = true
					catchtimer = 0
					timetocatch = math.random(5,15)
					throw()
				end
			elseif Enabled then
				Part:Destroy()
				Wire:Destroy()
				Enabled = false
			end
		else
			if Enabled and Catching then
				Part:Destroy()
				Wire:Destroy()
				Catching, Enabled = false, false
				local catchco = coroutine.create(catch)
				coroutine.resume(catchco)
			end
		end
	end)
end)

Tool.Unequipped:connect(function()
	if Part then
		Part:Destroy()
	end
	if Wire then
		Wire:Destroy()
	end
	Catching, Enabled = false, false
end)

game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:connect(function()
	if Enabled and Part and Wire then
		local a, b = WireStart.Position, Part.Position
		Wire.Size = Vector3.new(1,(a - b).magnitude, 0.3)
		Wire.CFrame = CFrame.new(a, b) * CFrame.new(0, 0, -(a - b).magnitude / 2) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(90), 0, 0)
		if (Player.Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart").Position - Part.Position).Magnitude > 30 then
			Part:Destroy()
			Wire:Destroy()
			Catching, Enabled = false, false
		end
	end
end)

function changeTimer()
	while true do
		wait(1)
		if Enabled then
			catchtimer = catchtimer + 1
			if catchtimer == timetocatch then
				bob()
			end
		end
	end
end

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local gui = plr:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

local enabled = true
local currentdialog --keeps track of which NPC is being talked to
local selectedoption --keeps track of where the player is in any given dialog
local startingoption

repeat wait() until plr.Character and plr.Character.Parent ~= nil

local human = plr.Character:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local hrp = plr.Character:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")

function closeDialog()
	local dgui = gui:FindFirstChild("ACTIVE_Dialog")
	if dgui then
		dgui.Frame:TweenPosition(dgui.Frame.Position + UDim2.new(0,0,1,0),"InOut", "Quad", .5, true)
		game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(dgui,.5)
	end
end

function hideDialogOptions(all)
	local dgui = gui:FindFirstChild("ACTIVE_Dialog")
	if dgui then
		for i=1,5 do
			if selectedoption == nil or (selectedoption.Name ~= tostring(i) or all) then --display only selected option when clicked
				dgui.Frame[tostring(i)].Visible = false
			end
		end
	end
end

function displayNPCText(text)
	local dgui = gui:FindFirstChild("ACTIVE_Dialog")
	if dgui then
		local field = dgui.Frame.DialogText
		for i=1,#text do
			wait(1/30)
			if dgui then
				field.Text = string.sub(text,1,i)
			end
		end
		wait(1.3)
	end
end


function fillDialogChoiceBubble(text,scriptobject,guiobject) --fill the GUI objects with the appropriate text/DialogAdvancer script - guiobject is the frame containing the choice
	local oldscr = guiobject.SelectionButton:FindFirstChild("DialogAdvancer")
	if oldscr then
		oldscr:Destroy()
	end
	if scriptobject == nil then
		scriptobject = script.DialogAdvancer
	end
	local scr = scriptobject:clone()
	scr.Name = "DialogAdvancer"
	scr.Parent = guiobject.SelectionButton
	scr.Disabled = false
	guiobject.DialogText.Text = text
	guiobject.Visible = true
end


function loadDialogOptions() --go by selectedoption - load GUI objects
	local dgui = gui:FindFirstChild("ACTIVE_Dialog")
	if dgui then
		for i=1,5 do
			local choice = selectedoption:FindFirstChild(i)
			local guiobject = dgui.Frame:FindFirstChild(i)
			if choice then --dialog option available
				local scriptobject = choice:FindFirstChild("DialogAdvancer")
				fillDialogChoiceBubble(choice.UserDialog.Value,scriptobject,guiobject)
				if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") and guiobject.Name == "1" then
					if guiobject and guiobject.Visible then
						game:GetService("GuiService").SelectedObject = guiobject.SelectionButton
					end
				end
			else --make it the goodbye option
				if i == 1 then --nothing left to say, close the dialog
					closeDialog()
				end
				fillDialogChoiceBubble("Goodbye!",script["DialogAdvancer_Goodbye"],guiobject)
				break
			end
		end
	end
end



game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.DialogChoiceSelected.Event:connect(function(num,reset) --the number of the choice that they made - we can figure the rest out with this script's variables
	if num == "Goodbye" then
		selectedoption = nil
		hideDialogOptions()
		wait(.3)
		closeDialog()
		return
	end
	local newoption = selectedoption:FindFirstChild(num) --the option they just clicked
	selectedoption = newoption --prepare to load new choices + text
	hideDialogOptions()
	wait(1)
	displayNPCText(selectedoption.ResponseDialog.Value)
	if reset then
		selectedoption = startingoption
	end
	hideDialogOptions(true)
	loadDialogOptions()
end)

	if enabled and hit and hit.Name:match("DIALOG_") then
		local npcname = string.sub(hit.Name,8)
		local dialog = game.ReplicatedStorage.Dialog:FindFirstChild(npcname)

				if v.Name:match("ACTIVE_") then
					activeguis = activeguis + 1
				end
			end
			if activeguis == 0 then --load dialog
				
				local dgui = gui.LoadableGuis.Dialog:clone()
				dgui.Name = "ACTIVE_Dialog"
				dgui.Frame.ShopPos.Value = hit.Position
				dgui.Frame.Visible = true
				dgui.Parent = gui
				dgui.Frame:TweenPosition(dgui.Frame.Position + UDim2.new(0,0,-1,0),"InOut", "Quad", .5, true)
				currentdialog = dialog --so it can be loaded easily
				selectedoption = dialog
				startingoption = dialog
				wait(.5)
				displayNPCText(dialog.Value) --load initial dialog
				loadDialogOptions() --start loading the choices
				
			else
				game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalNotification:Fire("Please close the currently active window before talking to this person.")
				enabled = false
				wait(2)
				enabled = true
			end
			
		end
	end
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LoadLocalGui.Event:connect(function(guiname, newname,itemcategory,objectreference)
	local gui = plrgui.LoadableGuis:FindFirstChild(guiname)
	if gui then
		gui = gui:clone()
		if newname ~= nil then
			gui.Name = newname
		end
		if itemcategory then
			local category = Instance.new("StringValue")
			category.Name = "ItemCategory"
			category.Value = itemcategory
			category.Parent = gui
		end
		if objectreference then
			local ov = Instance.new("ObjectValue")
			ov.Name = "SelectedObject"
			ov.Value = objectreference
			ov.Parent = gui
		end
		gui.Name = "ACTIVE_"..gui.Name
		gui.Parent = plrgui
		if gui:FindFirstChild("LoadScript") then
			gui.LoadScript.Disabled = false
		end
		if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") then
			coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function() require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.XboxFunctions).SetGamepadSelection(gui) end))
		end
		
	end
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RequestToLoadGui.Event:connect(function(guiname,newname,itemcategory,objectreference)
	local activeguis = 0
	for i,v in pairs(plrgui:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name:match("ACTIVE_") then
			activeguis = activeguis + 1
		end
	end
	if activeguis == 0 then
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LoadLocalGui:Fire(guiname,newname,itemcategory,objectreference)
	else
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalNotification:Fire("Please close the currently active window before opening a new one.")
	end
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.OpenGuiFromServer.OnClientEvent:connect(function(guiname,newname,itemcategory,objectreference)
	game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RequestToLoadGui:Fire(guiname,newname,itemcategory,objectreference)
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.WaitToLoadGui.Event:connect(function(guiname,newname,itemcategory,objectreference) --wait for currently active GUI to disappear, then load the queued GUI
	
	local expectedgui = {guiname,newname}
	queuedgui = expectedgui --if these two end up differing, just stop the loop
	
	local function checkForActiveGuis()
		for i,v in pairs(plrgui:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name:match("ACTIVE_") then
				return true
			end
		end
		return false
	end
	
	repeat wait(1/30) until checkForActiveGuis() == false or expectedgui ~= queuedgui
	
	if expectedgui ~= queuedgui then return end
	
	game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RequestToLoadGui:Fire(queuedgui[1],queuedgui[2],itemcategory,objectreference)
	
end)

-- Display the Fire and Smoke GUI for Unrestricted DJ

function FireSmokeGUICheck(nextsong)
	local activegui = plrgui:FindFirstChild("FireSmokeGUI")
	if activegui then
		activegui:Destroy()
	end

	if nextsong and nextsong.PlayerName.Value == plr.Name then
		--it's this player's turn to have the GUI
		local newgui = plrgui.LoadableGuis:FindFirstChild("FireSmokeGUI"):clone()
		newgui.Parent = plrgui
		newgui.Frame.Visible = true
	end

end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NextSongInQueue.OnClientEvent:connect(function(nextsong)
	if game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalGamePassCheck:Invoke("UnrestrictedDJ") == true then
		FireSmokeGUICheck(nextsong)
	end
end)

		local fl = house:FindFirstChild("Floor")
		if fl then
			local mag = (fl.Position - hrp.Position).magnitude
			if mag < 150 then
				return true
			else
				return false
			end
		end
	end
end

function DetailSettingChanged(val)
	if val == 3 then
		
		if not workspace:FindFirstChild("!HighDetail") then
			local building = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.GetServerSideBuilding:InvokeServer("!HighDetail")
			if not workspace:FindFirstChild("!HighDetail") then
				local cl = building:clone()
				building:Destroy()
				cl.Parent = workspace
			end
		end
		
		if not workspace:FindFirstChild("!MaxDetail") then
			local building2 = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.GetServerSideBuilding:InvokeServer("!MaxDetail")
			if not workspace:FindFirstChild("!MaxDetail") then
				local cl = building2:clone()
				building2:Destroy()
				cl.Parent = workspace
			end
		end
		
		for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.UnrenderedApartments:GetChildren()) do
			v.Parent = workspace
		end
		
		ChangePlaceholderApartments(false)
		
	elseif val == 2 then
		
		if not workspace:FindFirstChild("!HighDetail") then
			local building = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.GetServerSideBuilding:InvokeServer("!HighDetail")
			if not workspace:FindFirstChild("!HighDetail") then
				local cl = building:clone()
				building:Destroy()
				cl.Parent = workspace
			end
		end
		
		if workspace:FindFirstChild("!MaxDetail") then
			workspace["!MaxDetail"]:Destroy()
		end
		
		for _,v in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == "Apartment" or v.Name:match("apartment_") then
				v.Parent = game.ReplicatedStorage.UnrenderedApartments
			end
		end
		
		ChangePlaceholderApartments(true)
		
	else
		
		if workspace:FindFirstChild("!MaxDetail") then
			workspace["!MaxDetail"]:Destroy()
		end
		
		if workspace:FindFirstChild("!HighDetail") then
			workspace["!HighDetail"]:Destroy()
		end
		
		for _,v in pairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == "Apartment" or v.Name:match("apartment_") then
				v.Parent = game.ReplicatedStorage.UnrenderedApartments
			end
		end
		
		ChangePlaceholderApartments(true)
		
	end
end

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local gui = plr:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
local mouse = plr:GetMouse()
local container = gui:WaitForChild("ContextActionsGui")

if plr.Character == nil or plr.Character.Parent == nil then
	plr.CharacterAdded:wait()
end

local char = plr.Character
local hrp = char:WaitForChild("HumanoidRootPart")
local playerstatus = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId):WaitForChild("SpecialStatus")

local CurrentObject = nil
local actions = {}

local hover_icon = "rbxassetid://231195429"
local normal_icon = "rbxasset://textures/ArrowFarCursor.png"
local select_sound = "rbxassetid://231188062"

--[[

on RenderStepped: 

IF NO TOOL EQUIPPED:


IF NO CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- get mouse Hit. if the object's ContextActionsModelName is the same as the CurrentObjectHoveringOver, change nothing.
- if ContextActionsModelName is different (or there is currently a CurrentObjectHoveringOver, and a new ContextActionsModelName is not found), then remove the "Interact with" overlay if there is one and set mouse icon to White. if there is a ContextActionsModelName in the new object, make that the overlay and set mouse icon to Green.

- every frame of this, update any "Interact with" overlay's position


IF CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- if mouse is outside of context menu's range, remove context menu




otherwise, IF TOOL EQUIPPED: 
- remove any overlays, do not change mouse icon







on MouseButton1Down:
IF NO CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- if mouse is over object, generate the object's action menu. set mouse icon to White

IF CONTEXT MENU OPEN:
- if mouse is not over context menu, remove context menu

each action button has its own Clicked connection which will run the action's module and then remove the context menu

]]

function isOffScreen(point,bx,by) --bx and by are part of AbsoluteSize
	if point.x < 0 or point.x > bx or point.y < 0 or point.y > by then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end

function checkOffScreenCorners(element) --corners are like quadrants
	local pos = element.Position
	local size = element.Size
	local c1 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset + size.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset)
	local c2 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset)
	local c3 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset + size.Y.Offset)
	local c4 = Vector2.new(pos.X.Offset + size.X.Offset, pos.Y.Offset + size.Y.Offset)
	local bx = container.AbsoluteSize.x
	local by = container.AbsoluteSize.y
	return isOffScreen(c1,bx,by),isOffScreen(c2,bx,by), isOffScreen(c3,bx,by),isOffScreen(c4,bx,by)
end

function handsAreFree()
	if plr.Character then
		for i,v in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Tool") or v:IsA("HopperBin") then
				return false
			end
		end
		for i,v in pairs(gui:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name:match("ACTIVE_") then
				return false
			end
		end
		return true
	end
end

function getAvailableActions(obj)
	local getactions = {}
	local objname = obj:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName")
	if objname then
		objname = objname.Value
		local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.ContextActions:FindFirstChild(objname)
		if objdata then
			
			for i,v in pairs(objdata:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("StringValue") then
					table.insert(getactions,v)
				end
			end
			
			local owner = obj:FindFirstChild("Owner")
			if owner then
				if owner.Value == plr.Name then
					for i,v in pairs(objdata.Owner:GetChildren()) do
						if v:IsA("StringValue") then
							table.insert(getactions,v)
						end
					end
					for i,v in pairs(objdata.Owner.SpecialStatus:GetChildren()) do
						if playerstatus:FindFirstChild(v.Name) then --if this status name is inside the player's special statuses
							for i,x in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do --add any actions that are under that category
								table.insert(getactions,x)
							end
						end
					end
				else
					if owner.Value == "" then
						for i,v in pairs(objdata.NoOwner:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("StringValue") then
								table.insert(getactions,v)
							end
						end
					else
						for i,v in pairs(objdata.OtherOwner:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("StringValue") then
								table.insert(getactions,v)
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end

			for i,v in pairs(objdata.SpecialStatus:GetChildren()) do
				if playerstatus:FindFirstChild(v.Name) then 
					for i,x in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
						table.insert(getactions,x)
					end
				end
			end
			
			for i,v in pairs(objdata.NotSpecialStatus:GetChildren()) do
				if not playerstatus:FindFirstChild(v.Name) then 
					for i,x in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
						table.insert(getactions,x)
					end
				end
			end
			
			
		end
	end
	actions = getactions
end

gui:WaitForChild("ActionMenuSelection").Event:connect(function(num)
	if CurrentObject ~= nil then
		local objname = CurrentObject:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName")
		if objname then
			objname = objname.Value
			local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.ContextActions:FindFirstChild(objname)
			if objdata then
				local action = objdata:FindFirstChild(num,true)
				if action then
					local module = require(action.ModuleScript)
					module.RunAction(plr,CurrentObject) --run the action now!
				end
			end
		end
	end
end)

local RMBdown = false

function WithinValidDistance(hit,target)
	local model = target.Parent
	local maxdistance = 32
	if model:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsDistance") then
		maxdistance = model.ContextActionsDistance.Value
	end
	if char and hrp and (hrp.Position - hit.p).magnitude < maxdistance then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end

function MouseOverActiveFrame(x,y)
	local isover = false
	local function recurse(instance)
		for _,v in pairs(instance:GetChildren()) do
			if (v:IsA("Frame") or v:IsA("ScrollingFrame")) and v.Visible == true then
				if (x > v.AbsolutePosition.x and x < v.AbsolutePosition.x + v.AbsoluteSize.x and y > v.AbsolutePosition.y and y < v.AbsolutePosition.y + v.AbsoluteSize.y) then
					isover = true
					break
				else
					recurse(v)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	if gui:FindFirstChild("MainMenu") then
		recurse(gui.MainMenu)
	end
	if not isover then
		if gui:FindFirstChild("PhoneGUI") and gui.PhoneGUI.Frame.Visible == true then
			recurse(gui.PhoneGUI)
		end
	else
		return true
	end
	if not isover then
		if gui:FindFirstChild("HouseEditMode") and gui.HouseEditMode.CenterFrame.Visible == true then
			recurse(gui.HouseEditMode.CenterFrame)
		end
	else
		return true
	end
	if not isover then
		return false
	else
		return true
	end
end

game:GetService("RunService").RenderStepped:connect(function()
	local actionmenu = container:FindFirstChild("ActionMenu")
	local overlay = container:FindFirstChild("InteractOverlay")
	if handsAreFree() then
		if not actionmenu then --manage interact overlay if there's no action menu
			local targetmodel
			if mouse.Target then
				targetmodel = (mouse.Target.Parent:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName") or mouse.Target.Parent:IsA("Accoutrement") and mouse.Target.Parent.Parent:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName"))
			end
			if targetmodel then
				targetmodel = targetmodel.Parent
			end
			if mouse.Target and targetmodel and not RMBdown and WithinValidDistance(mouse.Hit,mouse.Target) and not MouseOverActiveFrame(mouse.X,mouse.Y) then
				if targetmodel ~= CurrentObject then --if object is valid and it is not the same one being hovered over already, get actions
					getAvailableActions(targetmodel)
					if #actions > 0 then
						CurrentObject = targetmodel
						if overlay then
							overlay:Destroy()
						end
						mouse.Icon = hover_icon
						overlay = container.BaseInteractOverlay:clone()
						overlay.Visible = true
						overlay.Name = "InteractOverlay"
						if #actions == 1 then
							overlay.Text = actions[1].Value
						else
							local modelname = targetmodel.ContextActionsModelName.Value
							if modelname == "Player" then
								modelname = targetmodel.Name
							end
							overlay.Text = "Interact with " ..modelname
						end
						overlay.Parent = container
						overlay.Visible = true
						overlay.Size = UDim2.new(0,overlay.TextBounds.X + 15,0,16)
					end
				end
			else --remove overlay if not hovering over valid object - either no CAMN, too far away, hovering over active GUI frame, or RMB is down
				mouse.Icon = normal_icon
				CurrentObject = nil
				if overlay then
					overlay:Destroy()
				end
			end
			if overlay then
				overlay.Position = UDim2.new(0,mouse.X,0,mouse.Y - overlay.Size.Y.Offset)
				local c1,c2,c3,c4 = checkOffScreenCorners(overlay)
				if c4 == true and c1 == true then
					overlay.Position = overlay.Position - UDim2.new(0,overlay.Size.X.Offset,0,0)
				end
				if c1 == true and c2 == true then
					overlay.Position = overlay.Position + UDim2.new(0,0,0,overlay.Size.Y.Offset)
				end
			end
		else
			local x = mouse.X
			local y = mouse.Y
			if x < actionmenu.Position.X.Offset - 40 or x > actionmenu.Position.X.Offset + actionmenu.Size.X.Offset + 40 or y < actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset - 40 or y > actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset + actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset + 40 then
				mouse.Icon = normal_icon
				actionmenu:Destroy()
				CurrentObject = nil
			end
		end
	else
		if actionmenu then
			actionmenu:Destroy()
		end
		if overlay then
			overlay:Destroy()
		end
		CurrentObject = nil
	end
end)

mouse.Button1Down:connect(function()
	if handsAreFree() then
		local actionmenu = container:FindFirstChild("ActionMenu")
		if not actionmenu then 
			
			if CurrentObject ~= nil and #actions > 0 then --create an action menu for the object, then set mouse icon
				local sound = Instance.new("Sound",gui)
				sound.SoundId = select_sound
				sound:Play()
				sound.TimePosition = .05
				game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,.5)
				if #actions >= 2 then
					mouse.Icon = normal_icon
					local overlay = container:FindFirstChild("InteractOverlay")
					if overlay then
						overlay:Destroy()
					end
					actionmenu = container.BaseActionMenu:clone()
					actionmenu.Name = "ActionMenu"
					actionmenu.Parent = container
					actionmenu.Visible = true
					local maxwidth = 0
					for i,v in pairs(actions) do
						local button = actionmenu.BaseSelector:clone()
						button.Name = v.Name
						button.Text = " "..v.Value
						button.Parent = actionmenu
						button.Visible = true
						button.Position = UDim2.new(0,0,0, (#actionmenu:GetChildren() - 2) * 20)
						if button.TextBounds.X > maxwidth then
							maxwidth = button.TextBounds.X
						end
						
						if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") and i == 1 then
							game:GetService("GuiService").SelectedObject = button
						end
					end
					actionmenu.Size = UDim2.new(0,maxwidth + 3,0,(#actionmenu:GetChildren() - 1) * 20)
					actionmenu.Position = UDim2.new(0,mouse.X,0,mouse.Y)
					local c1,c2,c3,c4 = checkOffScreenCorners(actionmenu)
					if c1 == true and c3 == true and c4 == true then
						actionmenu.Position = actionmenu.Position - UDim2.new(0,actionmenu.Size.X.Offset,0,actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset)
					elseif c3 == true and c4 == true then
						actionmenu.Position = actionmenu.Position - UDim2.new(0,0,0,actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset)
					elseif c1 == true and c4 == true then
						actionmenu.Position = actionmenu.Position - UDim2.new(0,actionmenu.Size.X.Offset,0,0)
					end
				else
					gui.ActionMenuSelection:Fire(actions[1].Name) --only one action
				end
			end
			
		else --action menu already open - is mouse in range of it?
			local x = mouse.X
			local y = mouse.Y
			if x < actionmenu.Position.X.Offset or x > actionmenu.Position.X.Offset + actionmenu.Size.X.Offset or y < actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset or y > actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset + actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset then
				actionmenu:Destroy()
				CurrentObject = nil
			end
		end
	end
end)

mouse.Button2Down:connect(function() --remove on right click outside of menu
	RMBdown = true
	if handsAreFree() then
		local actionmenu = container:FindFirstChild("ActionMenu")
		if actionmenu then 
			local x = mouse.X
			local y = mouse.Y
			if x < actionmenu.Position.X.Offset or x > actionmenu.Position.X.Offset + actionmenu.Size.X.Offset or y < actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset or y > actionmenu.Position.Y.Offset + actionmenu.Size.Y.Offset then
				actionmenu:Destroy()
				CurrentObject = nil
			end
		end
	end
end)

mouse.Button2Up:connect(function()
	RMBdown = false
end)

--Made by Luckymaxer

ScreenGui = script.Parent
Frame = ScreenGui:WaitForChild("Frame")

Player = ScreenGui.Parent

SkateboardValue = ScreenGui:WaitForChild("SkateboardValue")

function InvokeServer(RemoteFunction, Mode, Value)
	local ServerReturn = nil
	pcall(function()
		ServerReturn = RemoteFunction:InvokeServer(Mode, Value)
	end)
	return ServerReturn
end

function DoSkateboard(action)
	local Skateboard = SkateboardValue.Value
		if Skateboard then
			local SkateboardPlatform = Skateboard:FindFirstChild("SkateboardPlatform")
			if SkateboardPlatform then
				local RemoteControl = SkateboardPlatform:FindFirstChild("RemoteControl")
				if RemoteControl then
					local ServerControl = RemoteControl:FindFirstChild("ServerControl")
					if ServerControl then
						if action == "PickUp" then
							Spawn(function()
								InvokeServer(ServerControl, "KeyPress", {Key = "Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl", Down = true})
								wait(0.001)
								InvokeServer(ServerControl, "KeyPress", {Key = "Enum.KeyCode.P", Down = true})
							end)
						elseif action == "Dismount" then
							InvokeServer(ServerControl, "KeyPress", {Key = "Backspace", Down = true})
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
end

for i, v in pairs(Frame:GetChildren()) do
	if v:IsA("GuiButton") then
		v.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
			DoSkateboard(v.Name)
		end)
	end
end

if game.Players.LocalPlayer:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") then
	local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
	local GuiService = game:GetService("GuiService")
	
	UserInputService.InputBegan:connect(function(input,gameprocessed)
		if ScreenGui and ScreenGui.Parent ~= nil and GuiService.SelectedObject == nil and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 then
			if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.DPadUp then
				DoSkateboard("Dismount")
			elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.DPadDown then
				DoSkateboard("PickUp")
			end
		end
	end)
end

--house product id: 183
--house painting license: 208
--furniture shop name: ACTIVE_FurnitureShop

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local tools = {"Move","Paint","Remove"}

if plr.Character == nil or plr.Character.Parent == nil then
	plr.CharacterAdded:wait()
end

local hum = char:WaitForChild("Humanoid")
local backpack = plr:WaitForChild("Backpack")

local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId)

local cf = script.Parent:WaitForChild("CenterFrame")
local frame = cf:WaitForChild("Frame")

local enabled = frame:WaitForChild("Enabled")
local selectedtool = frame:WaitForChild("SelectedTool")

local prompts = frame:WaitForChild("GamepadToolPrompts")

local moveinc = plr:FindFirstChild("HouseMoveIncrement") --1, 2, 4, 8, 16
if moveinc == nil then
	moveinc = Instance.new("IntValue",plr)
	moveinc.Name = "HouseMoveIncrement"
	moveinc.Value = 1
end

local rotateinc = plr:FindFirstChild("HouseRotateIncrement") --45, 15
if rotateinc == nil then
	rotateinc = Instance.new("IntValue",plr)
	rotateinc.Name = "HouseRotateIncrement"
	rotateinc.Value = 45
end

local paintcolor = plr:FindFirstChild("HousePaintColor") --send this value with REs
if paintcolor == nil then
	paintcolor = Instance.new("BrickColorValue",plr)
	paintcolor.Name = "HousePaintColor"
	paintcolor.Value = BrickColor.new("Dark green")
end





function setFeedback(type,text,value) --types: Save, Exit, Dialog (no buttons), NormalButton, Cash
	local gui = frame.FeedbackDialog
	gui.WindowType.Value = type
	if text == nil then
		text = ""
	end
	gui.Reason.Text = text
	for _,v in pairs(gui:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("Frame") then
			v.Visible = false
		end
	end
	if type == "Save" or type == "Exit" then
		gui.OptionButtons.Visible = true
	--elseif type == "Dialog" then	
	elseif type == "NormalButton" then
		gui[type].Visible = true
	elseif type == "Cash" then

		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			if v.Name ~= "30" then
				local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - value
				if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
					bestprod = v
				end
				if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
					highestprod = v
				end
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		gui.Reason.Text = "You need more Cash in order to save all the items that you've added to your house. Get more Cash & save the house?"
		gui.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		gui.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		gui.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		gui.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		
	end
	gui.Visible = true
end

function removeTools()
	for _,v in pairs(tools) do
		local t = backpack:FindFirstChild(v)
		if t then
			t:Destroy()
		end
		local tool = char:FindFirstChild(v)
		if tool then
			hum:UnequipTools()
			wait(1/30)
			if tool then
				tool:Destroy()
			end
		end
		
		if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") then
			for _,v in pairs(prompts:GetChildren()) do
				v.Visible = false
			end
		end
	end
end

data:WaitForChild("cash").Changed:connect(function(val)
	local gui = frame:WaitForChild("FeedbackDialog")
	if gui.Visible == true and gui:WaitForChild("WindowType").Value == "Cash" then
		local diff = data:WaitForChild("FurnitureCost").Value - data.cash.Value
		if diff > 0 then --can't afford
			
		else
			setFeedback("Save","Save your house?")
			selectedtool.Value = ""
			removeTools()
		end
	end
end)

function checkForNoGui()
	for _,v in pairs(plr.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name:match("ACTIVE_") then
			return false
		end
	end
	return true
end

frame.SelectTool.Event:connect(function(toolname)
	if enabled.Value == true then
		if toolname == selectedtool.Value then
			selectedtool.Value = ""
			enabled.Value = false
			removeTools()
			enabled.Value = true
			return
		else
			selectedtool.Value = toolname
		end

		enabled.Value = false
		removeTools()
		enabled.Value = true
		local tool = game.ReplicatedStorage.HouseEditingTools:FindFirstChild(toolname)
		if tool then
			local cl = tool:clone()
			cl.Parent = backpack
			hum:EquipTool(cl)
		end
		if plr:FindFirstChild("GamepadMode") then
			game:GetService("GuiService").SelectedObject = nil
			for _,v in pairs(prompts:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name == toolname then
					v.Visible = true
				else
					v.Visible = false
				end
				prompts.Visible = true
			end
		end

frame.ExitHouse.Event:connect(function(confirmed)
	if checkForNoGui() and (enabled.Value == true or confirmed) then
		if confirmed then --exit house
			selectedtool.Value = ""
			removeTools()
			local house = workspace["!home_"..plr.Name]
			local request = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ReloadHouse:InvokeServer(house.HousePlacement.Value)
			if request == true or request == nil then
				cf.Visible = false
				enabled.Value = true
			else
				setFeedback("NormalButton","Error: Please wait a few seconds, then try again.")
			end
		else --display dialog
			setFeedback("Exit","Exit edit mode? Any unsaved progress on your house will be lost.")
			selectedtool.Value = ""
			removeTools()
		end
	end
end)

frame.SaveHouse.Event:connect(function(confirmed)
	if checkForNoGui() and (enabled.Value == true or confirmed) then
		if confirmed then
			selectedtool.Value = ""
			removeTools()
			setFeedback("Dialog","Saving your house...")
			local request = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.HouseSave:InvokeServer()
			if request == true then
				setFeedback("NormalButton","Save successful!")
			elseif request == false then
				setFeedback("NormalButton","Error: Please wait a few seconds, then try again.")
			else
				setFeedback("NormalButton","Error: Failed to save house. Please try again.")
			end
		else
			local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
			local diff = data.FurnitureCost.Value - data.cash.Value
			if diff > 0 then --can't afford
				setFeedback("Cash","",diff)
			else
				setFeedback("Save","Save your house?")
			end
			selectedtool.Value = ""
			removeTools()
		end
	end
end)

	local menu
	for i,v in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("Frame") and v.Name == "menu_"..button.Name then
			menu = v
		end
	end
	
	if menu then
		
		if menu.Visible == true then
			menu.Visible = false
			button.BackgroundColor3 = defaultcolor
			return
		end
		
		menu.Visible = true
		
		for i,v in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("GuiButton") then
				if v ~= button and v.Name ~= "ShopButton" and v.Name ~= "MobileAd" and v.Name ~= "ToggleVisibility" then --make other buttons def.
					v.BackgroundColor3 = defaultcolor
				elseif v == button then --make the selected button green
					v.BackgroundColor3 = selectcolor
				end
			elseif v:IsA("Frame") then
				if v.Name:match("menu_") then
					if v.Name ~= "menu_"..button.Name then --make frame invis
						v.Visible = false
					end
				end
			end
		end
		
	end
end)

frame:WaitForChild("CloseMenus").Event:connect(function()
	
	for i,v in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if v:IsA("GuiButton") then
			if v.Name ~= "ShopButton" and v.Name ~= "MobileAd" and v.Name ~= "ToggleVisibility" then --make other buttons def.
				v.BackgroundColor3 = defaultcolor
			end
		elseif v:IsA("Frame") then
			if v.Name:match("menu_") then
				v.Visible = false
			end
		end
	end
	
end)

local shopname = script.Parent.Name

	shopname = string.sub(shopname,8)
end

local gui = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")
local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local enabled = gui:WaitForChild("Enabled")

gui:WaitForChild("Header").Text = shopname

local module = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.InterfaceModule)
module.loadShopItems(gui,shopname)
module.loadShopTheme(script.Parent,shopname)
module.openWindow(gui)

local torso
if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
	torso = char.HumanoidRootPart
	gui.ShopPos.Value = char.HumanoidRootPart.Position

function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "cash" then
		
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			if v.Name ~= "30" then
				local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
				if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
					bestprod = v
				end
				if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
					highestprod = v
				end
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"
		failure.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end


gui.BuyRequest.Event:connect(function(button)
	if enabled.Value == true then
		enabled.Value = false
		local oldbg = button.BackgroundColor3
		local oldborder = button.BorderColor3
		button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.6,.6,.6)
		button.BorderColor3 = Color3.new(.1,.1,.1)
		local result,reason = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PurchaseTemporaryItem:InvokeServer(shop.Name, gui.SelectedItem.Value)
		if result == true then
			local sound = Instance.new("Sound",plr.PlayerGui)
			sound.Name = "ShopBuySuccess"
			sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..game.ReplicatedStorage.AssetIDs[sound.Name].Value
			sound:Play()
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,3)
		else
			displayFailureWindow(result,reason)
		end
		button.BackgroundColor3 = oldbg
		button.BorderColor3 = oldborder
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

gui.LoadMenu.Event:connect(function(button) --selected button, get item data
	local data = shop:FindFirstChild(button.Name)
	gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..data.ItemCost.Value
	gui.Desc.ItemName.Text = button.Name
	gui.Desc.ItemDesc.Text = data.ItemDesc.Value
	gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = true
	for i,v in pairs(gui.Items:GetChildren()) do
		v.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
	end
	button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
	gui.SelectedItem.Value = button.Name
end)

function createButton(obj,hassong)
	local button = frame.BaseButton:clone()
	local num = #frame:GetChildren() - 1
	button.Name = obj.Name
	button.ItemID.Value = obj.ItemID.Value
	button.Position = button.Position + UDim2.new(0,0,0,36*num)
	button.TextLabel.Text = string.sub(obj.Name,7)
	if hassong == true then
		button.TextLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(.2,1,.2)
	end
	button.Visible = true
	button.Parent = frame
	frame.CanvasSize = frame.CanvasSize + UDim2.new(0,0,0,36)
end

shopframe.LoadMenu.Event:connect(function(category,search)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects
	if data then
		clearAllButtons()
		if category ~= "Search" then
			shopframe.LoadItemInfo:Fire(nil)
		end
		local count = 0
		for _,obj in pairs(objdata:GetChildren()) do
			if obj.Name:match("music_") and ((search ~= nil and string.sub(string.lower(obj.Name),7):match(string.lower(search))) or (category ~= "Search" and obj.Genre.Value == category)) and obj.ItemCost.Value < 1000000 and not obj:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") then
				local hassong = false
				if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(obj.ItemID.Value) then
					hassong = true
				end
				count = count + 1
				createButton(obj,hassong)
			end
		end
		local warning = shopframe:WaitForChild("ItemsBG"):WaitForChild("WarningLabel")
		if count > 0 then
			warning.Visible = false
		else
			if search ~= nil then
				warning.Text = "There are no CDs which match that search. (Can't find it here? Unrestricted DJ Powers let you play any song!)"
			end
			warning.Visible = true
		end
	end
end)

	if button ~= nil then
		local id = button.ItemID.Value
		shopframe.SelectedID.Value = id
		shopframe.Desc.ItemName.Text = button.TextLabel.Text
		local item = getItemDataFromID(id)
		shopframe.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..item.ItemCost.Value
		shopframe.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = true
		shopframe.Desc.PreviewButton.Visible = true
	else
		shopframe.SelectedID.Value = 0
		shopframe.Desc.ItemName.Text = "Click on a song to get more info"
		shopframe.Desc.ItemCost.Text = ""
		shopframe.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = false
		shopframe.Desc.PreviewButton.Visible = false
	end
end)

	if item then
		if script.Parent:FindFirstChild("MusicPreview") then
			script.Parent.MusicPreview:Stop()
			script.Parent.MusicPreview:Destroy()
		end

			return
		end
		local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
		sound.Name = "MusicPreview"
		sound.Volume = .56 - item.Modifier.Value
		sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..item.AssetID.Value
		sound.Parent = script.Parent
		sound:Play()

		wait(40)
		if sound then
			local vol = sound.Volume
			for i=1,20 do
				if sound then
					sound.Volume = sound.Volume - vol/20
					wait(.1)
				end
			end
			if sound then
				sound:Stop()
				sound:Destroy()
			end
		end
	end
end)

			end

		button.BorderColor3 = oldborder

	end
end)

	hoverimg.Visible = true
	gui.Desc.ItemDesc.Text = desc
	gui.Desc.ItemName.Text = itemname

			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: "..baseprice.." R$"
			gui.Desc.DiscountSlash.Visible = true
			gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = asset.PriceInRobux.." R$"
			gui.Desc.Savings.Text = "You save: "..baseprice - asset.PriceInRobux.." R$!"
			
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextTransparency = .5
		else
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: "..asset.PriceInRobux.." R$"
			gui.Desc.DiscountSlash.Visible = false
			gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = ""
			gui.Desc.Savings.Text = ""
			
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextStrokeTransparency = .7
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextTransparency = 0
		end
	else
		gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "(failed to load price)"
	end
end

function onMouseLeave()
	hoverimg.Visible = false
end

	local selection = gui:WaitForChild("GamepadSelection")
	selection.Changed:connect(function(obj)
		if obj == script.Parent then
			onMouseEnter()
		else
			onMouseLeave()
		end
	end)
end

marketplace.PromptPurchaseFinished:connect(function(plrpurchased,purchaseId,isPurchased)
	if plrpurchased == plr and isPurchased then
		local successdesc
		
		for name,desc in pairs(assets) do
			if game.ReplicatedStorage.GamePasses[name].Value == purchaseId then
				successdesc = desc
			end
		end
		
		if successdesc == nil then
			for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Value == purchaseId then
					successdesc = "You have successfully purchased +"..v.Name.." RHS Cash. You can use RHS Cash to purchase a variety of special items throughout the game. Enjoy!"
				end
			end
		end
		
		if successdesc == nil then
			successdesc = ""
		end
		
		gui.PurchaseSuccess.Desc.Text = successdesc
		gui.PurchaseSuccess.Visible = true
	end
end)

			wait(1/30)
			if gamepadselection.Value ~= guiservice.SelectedObject then
				gamepadselection.Value = guiservice.SelectedObject
			end
		end
	end))

--remember, pages are 1, 4, 7, and 10 (+2)

--player's data --> Outfits --> outfits named 1-12

--last few things: check the SafeToExit things, then we're good to go??

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local frame = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")
local outfitslots = frame:WaitForChild("OutfitSlots")
local outfitpages = frame:WaitForChild("OutfitPages")
local inputs = frame:WaitForChild("OutfitInputs")
local enabled = frame:WaitForChild("Enabled")

local marketplace = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")

local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId)

local canpurchase = true --stop multi-purchases

local module = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.InterfaceModule)
module.openWindow(script.Parent.Frame)

function getItemDataFromID(id)
	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.ItemID.Value == id then
			return v
		end
	end
end


function displayWindow(windowname,reason) --reason is optional and only for the errorwarning
	enabled.Value = false
	local window = frame:FindFirstChild(windowname)
	if window then
		if reason ~= nil then
			window.Reason.Text = reason
		end
		window.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until window.Visible == false
	end
	enabled.Value = true
end

function loadSlots(num) --1/4/7/10
	local slot = tonumber(num)
	frame.SelectedPage.Value = slot
	
	for i,v in pairs(outfitslots:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "BaseSlot" then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	
	for i=slot,slot+2 do
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(i)
		if outfit then
			local gui = outfitslots.BaseSlot:clone()
			gui.OutfitName.Text = outfit.OutfitName.Value
			if outfit.OutfitName.Value == "" then
				gui.OutfitName.Text = "Empty"
				gui.Load.Visible = false
			end
			gui.NumberLabel.Text = "#"..i
			gui.Position = gui.Position + UDim2.new((#outfitslots:GetChildren() - 1)*.33,0,0,0)
			gui.Name = i
			gui.Parent = outfitslots
			gui.Visible = true
		end
	end
	setOutfitPageColors()
end

function setOutfitPageColors()
	for _,button in pairs(outfitpages:GetChildren()) do
		button.BackgroundTransparency = .5
		if button.Name ~= "OutfitsPageBaseSlot" then --has outfit page
			button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(140/255, 221/255, 1)
			--button.ItemCost.Text = ""
		end
		if frame.SelectedPage.Value == tonumber(button.Name) then
			button.BackgroundTransparency = .2
		end
	end
end

data.Objects.ChildAdded:connect(function(child)
	if tonumber(child.Name) == outfitpages["4"].ItemID.Value or tonumber(child.Name) == outfitpages["7"].ItemID.Value or tonumber(child.Name) == outfitpages["10"].ItemID.Value then
		setOutfitPageColors()
	end
end)

frame.LoadOutfitPage.Event:connect(function(num)
	loadSlots(num)
end)

frame.ChangeSelectedSlot.Event:connect(function(num)
	frame.SelectedSlot.Value = num
end)

function checkIfSafeToExit() --for loading or closing - (if input boxes are NOT blank, or input boxes do NOT match an already saved outfit)
	return true
end

--"confirmed" - nil means display a window - true means it's okay to do the action

frame.LoadOutfit.Event:connect(function(confirmed) --load the outfit IDs stored in the SelectedSlot
	local check = checkIfSafeToExit()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		enabled.Value = false
		displayWindow("LoadWarning")
		enabled.Value = true
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		local slot = frame.SelectedSlot.Value
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
		if outfit then
			
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
					v.Enabled.Value = outfit.RemoveShirt.Value
				else
					v.InputBox.Text = outfit[v.Name].Value
					if outfit[v.Name].Value == 0 then
						v.InputBox.Text = ""
					end
				end
			end
			
			frame.WearOutfit:Fire()
			
		end
	end
end)

frame.CloseWindow.Event:connect(function(confirmed)
	local check = checkIfSafeToExit()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		displayWindow("CloseWarning")
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		module.closeWindow(script.Parent)
	end
end)

frame.WearOutfit.Event:connect(function()
	if enabled.Value == true then
		enabled.Value = false
		local tabletosend = {} --["NameOfInput"] = ID
		for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
				tabletosend[v.Name] = v.Enabled.Value
			else
				tabletosend[v.Name] = v.InputBox.Text
			end
		end
		local request,statuses = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.WearOutfit:InvokeServer(tabletosend)
		if request == true then
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				v.InputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			displayWindow("SuccessWindow","Outfit worn successfully!")
		elseif request == false then
			if statuses ~= nil then
				for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
					if tostring(statuses[v.Name]):match("invalid") or tostring(statuses[v.Name]):match("admin") then
						v.InputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,.4,.4)
					end
				end
			end
			displayWindow("ErrorWarning","You have entered at least 1 invalid asset ID. Please correct the invalid IDs.")
		else
			displayWindow("ErrorWarning","Please wait a few more moments before trying this again.")
		end
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

frame.LoadOutfitPage:Fire(1)
setOutfitPageColors()

function checkIfSafeToExit() --for loading or closing - (if input boxes are NOT blank, or input boxes do NOT match an already saved outfit)
	local inputsblank = true
	for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "RemoveShirt" and v.InputBox.Text ~= "" then
			inputsblank = false
		end
	end
	
	if inputsblank == false then
		for _,outfit in pairs(data.Outfits:GetChildren()) do
			local matching = true
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				local val = outfit[v.Name].Value
				if val == 0 then
					val = ""
				end
				if v.Name ~= "RemoveShirt" and v.InputBox.Text ~= tostring(val) then
					matching = false
				end
			end
			if matching == true then return true end
		end
		return false
	else
		return true
	end
end

function checkIfSafeToSave() --(if slot is NOT empty, or outfit in that slot is NOT the same)
	local slot = frame.SelectedSlot.Value
	local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
	if outfit then
		if outfit.OutfitName.Value == "" then return true end
		for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
				if v.Enabled.Value ~= outfit.RemoveShirt.Value then return false end
			else
				local val = tostring(outfit[v.Name].Value)
				if val == 0 then
					val = ""
				end
				if v.InputBox.Text ~= tostring(val) then return false end
			end
		end
		return true
	end
end

--"confirmed" - nil means display a window - true means it's okay to do the action

frame.LoadOutfit.Event:connect(function(confirmed) --load the outfit IDs stored in the SelectedSlot
	local check = checkIfSafeToExit()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		enabled.Value = false
		displayWindow("LoadWarning")
		enabled.Value = true
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		local slot = frame.SelectedSlot.Value
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
		if outfit then
			
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
					v.Enabled.Value = outfit.RemoveShirt.Value
				else
					v.InputBox.Text = outfit[v.Name].Value
					if outfit[v.Name].Value == 0 then
						v.InputBox.Text = ""
					end
				end
			end
			
		end
	end
end)

	local check = checkIfSafeToExit()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		displayWindow("CloseWarning")
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		module.closeWindow(script.Parent)
	end
end)

frame.SaveOutfit.Event:connect(function(confirmed)
	local check = checkIfSafeToSave()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		displayWindow("SaveWarning")
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		local slot = frame.SelectedSlot.Value
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
		if outfit then
			
			--[[for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do --hah, we gotta FE this
				if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
					 outfit.RemoveShirt.Value = v.Enabled.Value
				else
					 outfit[v.Name].Value = v.InputBox.Text
				end

				else
					tabletosend[v.Name] = v.InputBox.Text
				end
			end
			local request,statuses = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SaveOutfit:InvokeServer(tabletosend,slot)
			if request == true then
				for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do

				end
				frame.LoadOutfitPage:Fire(frame.SelectedPage.Value)
				displayWindow("SuccessWindow","Outfit saved successfully!")
			elseif request == false then
				if statuses ~= nil then
					for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
						if tostring(statuses[v.Name]):match("invalid") or tostring(statuses[v.Name]):match("admin") then
							v.InputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,.4,.4)
						end
					end
				end
				displayWindow("ErrorWarning","You have entered at least 1 invalid asset ID. Please correct the invalid IDs.")
			else
				displayWindow("ErrorWarning","To avoid straining the server, please wait a few moments to try this again.")
			end
			
		end
	end
end)

local shopname = script.Parent.Name
if shopname:match("ACTIVE_") then
	shopname = string.sub(shopname,8)
end
local category = script.Parent:WaitForChild("ItemCategory") --the category of perma-item that should be loaded (pets, gear, etc.)

local shop = {} --table referencing all the objects that belong to the category

for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
	if v.Name:match(category.Value.."_") then
		table.insert(shop,v)
	end
end


local currenttime = os.time()
local sale_end_time = 1482444000
local oldcostname = "BoomboxOldCost" --for showing discounts for certain items in future?


local gui = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")
local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local enabled = gui:WaitForChild("Enabled")

local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId)

gui:WaitForChild("Header").Text = shopname

local details = game.ReplicatedStorage.PermaShopDetails:FindFirstChild(shopname)
if details then
	gui:WaitForChild("SubHeader").Text = details.SubHeader.Value
end

--now load all the buttons
local xv = 0
local yv = 0

for i,v in pairs(shop) do
	if not v:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") and not (v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and v.ReleaseTime.Value > game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value and game.PlaceId == 9689581) and not (v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") and v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value < game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value) then
		local button = gui.Items.BaseButton:clone() --make new button for each item
		button.Parent = gui.Items
		button.Name = v.Name
		button.ItemID.Value = v.ItemID.Value
		button.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id="..v.DecalID.Value
		button.Position = UDim2.new(0, xv*75, 0, yv*75)
		if v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
			local bird = script.TwitterBird:clone()
			bird.Parent = button
			bird.Visible = true
		end
		local oldcost = v:FindFirstChild(oldcostname)
		if oldcost and currenttime < sale_end_time and not data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) then
			local percent = math.ceil(-(v.ItemCost.Value - oldcost.Value) / oldcost.Value * 100)
			if percent == 34 then
				percent = 33
			end
			local oc = Instance.new("IntValue")
			oc.Name = "LocalOldCost"
			oc.Value = oldcost.Value
			oc.Parent = button
			button.DiscountPercent.Text = percent.."%"
			button.DiscountBanner.Visible = true
		end
		if v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*21 and not v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") then
			local newb = script.NewBanner:clone()
			newb.Parent = button
			newb.Visible = true
		elseif v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") then
			local b = script.LimitedBanner:clone()
			b.Parent = button
			b.Visible = true
			local offsaletime = Instance.new("IntValue")
			offsaletime.Name = "OffSaleTime"
			offsaletime.Value = v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value
			offsaletime.Parent = button
		end
		xv = xv + 1
		if xv == 4 then
			xv = 0
			yv = yv + 1
		end
		button.Visible = true
	end
end

gui.Items.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,(yv + 1) * 75)


local module = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.InterfaceModule)
module.openWindow(gui)
