local torso
if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
	torso = char.HumanoidRootPart
	gui.ShopPos.Value = char.HumanoidRootPart.Position
end


function setButtonColors()
	for _,button in pairs(gui.Items:GetChildren()) do
		if button.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(button.ItemID.Value) then --has object
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.5,1,.5)
			else
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			if gui.SelectedItem.Value == button.ItemID.Value then
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
			end
		end
	end
end


function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "cash" then
		
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			if v.Name ~= "30" then
				local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
				if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
					bestprod = v
				end
				if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
					highestprod = v
				end
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"
		failure.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end


gui.BuyRequest.Event:connect(function(button)
	if enabled.Value == true then
		enabled.Value = false
		local oldbg = button.BackgroundColor3
		local oldborder = button.BorderColor3
		button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.6,.6,.6)
		button.BorderColor3 = Color3.new(.1,.1,.1)
		local result,reason = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PurchasePermanentItem:InvokeServer( gui.SelectedItem.Value) --the item ID
		if result == true then
			local sound = Instance.new("Sound",plr.PlayerGui)
			sound.Name = "ShopBuySuccess"
			sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..game.ReplicatedStorage.AssetIDs[sound.Name].Value
			sound:Play()
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,3)
		else
			displayFailureWindow(result,reason)
		end
		button.BackgroundColor3 = oldbg
		button.BorderColor3 = oldborder
		enabled.Value = true
	end
end)

gui.LoadMenu.Event:connect(function(button) --selected button, get item data
	local itemdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:FindFirstChild(button.Name)
	local itemname = string.sub(button.Name,#category.Value + 2)
	gui.Desc.ItemName.Text = itemname
	gui.Desc.ItemDesc.Text = itemdata.ItemDesc.Value
	gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = ""
	gui.Desc.DiscountSlash.Visible = false
	gui.Desc.Savings.Text = ""
	gui.Desc.LimitedTimeNotice.Text = ""
	
	gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextStrokeTransparency = .7
	gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextTransparency = 0
	
	if not itemdata:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(itemdata.ItemID.Value) then
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "You own this item"
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = false
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = ""
		else
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..itemdata.ItemCost.Value
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = true
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = ""
			if button:FindFirstChild("LocalOldCost") then
				gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = "Price: $"..button.LocalOldCost.Value
				gui.Desc.DiscountSlash.Visible = true
				gui.Desc.ItemCostDiscounted.Text = "$"..itemdata.ItemCost.Value
				gui.Desc.Savings.Text = "You save: $"..button.LocalOldCost.Value - itemdata.ItemCost.Value.."!"
				
				gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
				gui.Desc.ItemCost.TextTransparency = .5
			end
			if button:FindFirstChild("OffSaleTime") then
				local timeleft = button.OffSaleTime.Value - game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value
				local days = math.floor(timeleft / 86400)
				local timeleftstr = (days >= 2 and (days.." days")) or (days == 1 and "1 day") or "less than 1 day"
				gui.Desc.LimitedTimeNotice.Text = "Off-sale in: "..timeleftstr
			end
		end
	else
		if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(itemdata.ItemID.Value) then
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = "You've unlocked this item from being an RHS Fan Club member!"
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = ""
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = false
		else
			gui.Desc.TwitterNotice.Text = "Join the RHS Fan Club to unlock this item! (Link in the game description)"
			gui.Desc.ItemCost.Text = ""
			gui.Desc.BuyButton.Visible = false
		end
	end
	
	for i,v in pairs(gui.Items:GetChildren()) do
		v.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
	end
	button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
	gui.SelectedItem.Value = button.ItemID.Value
	setButtonColors()
end)

local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId)

function getAvailableItemCount(id)
	local count = 0
	for _,v in pairs(data.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if tonumber(v.Name) == id then
			count = count + 1
		end
	end
	local furnbought = data.FurnitureCost:FindFirstChild(id)
	if furnbought then
		count = count + furnbought.PendingSales.Value + furnbought.Value --items in Objects - sold items - added items (or + removed items)
	end
	return count
end

--now load all the buttons

	if enabled.Value == true then
		local xv = 0
		local yv = 0

		savedcategory.Value = categoryname

			if v.Name ~= "BaseButton" then

			end
		end

				button.Name = v.Name
				button.ItemID.Value = v.ItemID.Value
				button.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/Thumbs/Asset.ashx?width=75&height=75&assetId=".. v.ModelID.Value
				button.Position = UDim2.new(0, xv*75, 0, yv*75)
				if v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") then
					local bird = script.TwitterBird:clone()
					bird.Parent = button
					bird.Visible = true
				end
				if v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*21 then
					local newb = script.NewBanner:clone()
					newb.Parent = button
					newb.Visible = true
				end
				xv = xv + 1
				if xv == 4 then
					xv = 0
					yv = yv + 1
				end
				button.Visible = true
			end
		end
		
		gui.Items.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,(yv + 1) * 75)
		gui.LoadItemInfo:Fire()
		setButtonColors()

			if v.Name == categoryname then
				v.BackgroundTransparency = 0
			else
				v.BackgroundTransparency = .5
			end
		end
	end
end

function setButtonColors()
	for _,button in pairs(gui.Items:GetChildren()) do
		if button.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			if getAvailableItemCount(button.ItemID.Value) > 0 then --has object
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.5,1,.5)
			else
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			if gui.SelectedItem.Value == button.ItemID.Value then
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
			end
		end
	end
end


function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.Header.Text = "Oops!"
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.OptionButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "sell" then --reason is the obj
		local failure = gui.FailureWindow
		failure.Header.Text = "Sell Item? (70%)"
		failure.Reason.Text = "Sell this item for $"..math.ceil(reason.ItemCost.Value * .7).."? This will be deducted from your final expenses."
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.OptionButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
		for _,v in pairs(gui.Items:GetChildren()) do
			if v.ItemID.Value == gui.SelectedItem.Value then
				gui.LoadItemInfo:Fire(v)
				break
			end
		end
	end
end

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local mp = game:GetService("MarketplaceService")

local gui = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")
local shirtid = gui:WaitForChild("ShirtID")
local pantsid = gui:WaitForChild("PantsID")

local module = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.InterfaceModule)
module.openWindow(script.Parent.Frame)

wait(.09)

--print(game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):GetProductInfo(581285249).Creator.CreatorTargetId)

function getClothingId(valueobj)
	if valueobj.Value ~= 0 then
		local asset
		local origid = valueobj.Value
		pcall(function()
			asset = mp:GetProductInfo(origid)
		end)
		if asset then
			local creator = asset.Creator.CreatorTargetId
			local count = 0
			local success
			local clothingasset
			local groups
			repeat
				pcall(function() clothingasset = mp:GetProductInfo(origid+(count+1)) end)
				if clothingasset and (clothingasset.AssetTypeId == 11 or clothingasset.AssetTypeId == 12) then
					if clothingasset.Creator.CreatorTargetId == creator then
						--surefire hit
						success = true
					elseif clothingasset.Creator.CreatorType == "Group" then
						if groups == nil then
							pcall(function()
								groups = game:GetService("GroupService"):GetGroupsAsync(creator)
							end)
						end
						if groups then
							for _,entry in pairs(groups) do
								if entry["Id"] == clothingasset.Creator.CreatorTargetId then
									success = true
									break
								end
							end
						end
					else
						clothingasset = nil
					end
				else
					clothingasset = nil
				end
				count = count + 1
			until success == true or count > 25
			if success == true then
				valueobj.Value = origid + count
			else
				print("failed to load clothing asset")
			end
		end
	end
end

getClothingId(shirtid)
getClothingId(pantsid)

--http://www.roblox.com/Thumbs/Asset.ashx?width=110&height=110&assetId=187347200

	gui.TwoItemsContainer.Pants.ImageButton.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/Thumbs/Asset.ashx?width=110&height=110&assetId="..pantsid.Value

else
	for _,v in pairs(gui:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name:match("ID") and v:IsA("IntValue") and v.Value ~= 0 then
			local container = gui.OneItem
			container.Name = string.sub(v.Name,1,5)
			container.ImageButton.Image = "http://www.roblox.com/Thumbs/Asset.ashx?width=110&height=110&assetId="..v.Value
			container.Visible = true
		end
	end

	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do

			return v
		end
	end

function displayWindow(windowname,reason) --reason is optional and only for the errorwarning
	enabled.Value = false
	local window = frame:FindFirstChild(windowname)
	if window then
		if reason ~= nil then
			window.Reason.Text = reason
		end
		window.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until window.Visible == false
	end
	enabled.Value = true
end

function loadSlots(num) --1/4/7/10
	local slot = tonumber(num)
	frame.SelectedPage.Value = slot
	
	for i,v in pairs(outfitslots:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "BaseSlot" then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	
	for i=slot,slot+2 do
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(i)
		if outfit then
			local gui = outfitslots.BaseSlot:clone()
			gui.OutfitName.Text = outfit.OutfitName.Value
			gui.NumberLabel.Text = "#"..i
			gui.Position = gui.Position + UDim2.new((#outfitslots:GetChildren() - 1)*.33,0,0,0)
			gui.Name = i
			gui.Parent = outfitslots
			gui.Visible = true
		end
	end
	setOutfitPageColors()
end

function setOutfitPageColors()
	for _,button in pairs(outfitpages:GetChildren()) do
		button.BackgroundTransparency = .5
		if button.Name ~= "OutfitsPageBaseSlot" then --has outfit page
			button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(140/255, 221/255, 1)
			--button.ItemCost.Text = ""
		end
		if frame.SelectedPage.Value == tonumber(button.Name) then
			button.BackgroundTransparency = .2
		end
	end
end

function checkIfSafeToExit() --for loading or closing - (if input boxes are NOT blank, or input boxes do NOT match an already saved outfit)
	local inputsblank = true
	for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "RemoveShirt" and v.InputBox.Text ~= "" then
			inputsblank = false
		end
	end
	
	if inputsblank == false then
		for _,outfit in pairs(data.Outfits:GetChildren()) do
			local matching = true
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				local val = outfit[v.Name].Value
				if val == 0 then
					val = ""
				end
				if v.Name ~= "RemoveShirt" and v.InputBox.Text ~= tostring(val) then
					matching = false
				end
			end
			if matching == true then return true end
		end
		return false
	else
		return true
	end
end

function checkIfSafeToSave() --(if slot is NOT empty, or outfit in that slot is NOT the same)
	local slot = frame.SelectedSlot.Value
	local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
	if outfit then
		if outfit.OutfitName.Value == "" then return true end
		for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
				if v.Enabled.Value ~= outfit.RemoveShirt.Value then return false end
			else
				local val = tostring(outfit[v.Name].Value)
				if val == 0 then
					val = ""
				end
				if v.InputBox.Text ~= tostring(val) then return false end
			end
		end
		return true
	end
end

--"confirmed" - nil means display a window - true means it's okay to do the action

frame.LoadOutfit.Event:connect(function(confirmed) --load the outfit IDs stored in the SelectedSlot
	local check = checkIfSafeToExit()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		enabled.Value = false
		displayWindow("LoadWarning")
		enabled.Value = true
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		local slot = frame.SelectedSlot.Value
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
		if outfit then
			
			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
					v.Enabled.Value = outfit.RemoveShirt.Value
				else
					v.InputBox.Text = outfit[v.Name].Value
					if outfit[v.Name].Value == 0 then
						v.InputBox.Text = ""
					end
				end
			end
			
		end
	end
end)

frame.CloseWindow.Event:connect(function(confirmed)
	local check = checkIfSafeToExit()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		displayWindow("CloseWarning")
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		module.closeWindow(script.Parent)
	end
end)

	local check = checkIfSafeToSave()
	if not confirmed and enabled.Value == true and check == false then
		displayWindow("SaveWarning")
	elseif confirmed == true or check == true then
		local slot = frame.SelectedSlot.Value
		local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(slot)
		if outfit then

				if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
					 outfit.RemoveShirt.Value = v.Enabled.Value
				else
					 outfit[v.Name].Value = v.InputBox.Text
				end

			for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Name == "RemoveShirt" then
					tabletosend[v.Name] = v.Enabled.Value
				else
					tabletosend[v.Name] = v.InputBox.Text
				end
			end
			local request,statuses = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SaveOutfit:InvokeServer(tabletosend,slot)
			if request == true then
				for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do

				end
				frame.LoadOutfitPage:Fire(frame.SelectedPage.Value)
				displayWindow("SuccessWindow","Outfit saved successfully!")
			elseif request == false then

					for _,v in pairs(inputs:GetChildren()) do

							v.InputBox.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,.4,.4)
						end
					end
				end
				displayWindow("ErrorWarning","You have entered at least 1 invalid asset ID. Please correct the invalid IDs.")
			else
				displayWindow("ErrorWarning","To avoid straining the server, please wait a few moments to try this again.")
			end
			
		end
	end
end)

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local stats = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(plr.userId):WaitForChild("LoyaltyStats")
local rs = game:GetService("RunService")
local frame = script.Parent:WaitForChild("Frame")

--empty, yellow, purple
local colors = {Color3.new(74/255,58/255,0), Color3.new(1,234/255,0), Color3.new(1,44/255,220/255)}

function commanumber(num)
	local num = tostring(num)
	local i = 0
	local finalstring = ""
	for n in string.gmatch(num,"%d") do
		finalstring = finalstring..n
		i = i + 1
		if (#num - i) % 3 == 0 and i ~= #num then
			finalstring = finalstring..","
		end
	end
	return finalstring
end

function SetBarOverheadText()
	frame:WaitForChild("ConsecDaysCount").Text = stats.Spins.Value == 0 and "Visit 5 days in a row = free RHS prize spins!" or stats.Spins.Value > 0 and "Congrats! You earned "..stats.Spins.Value.." free prize spin"..(stats.Spins.Value == 1 and "" or "s").."!"
end

--set up the text based on data
frame:WaitForChild("LPReward").Text = "+"..commanumber(stats.LPReward.Value).." LP"
frame:WaitForChild("LPDayBonus").Text = "(500+"..(stats.ConsecDays.Value > 5 and 5 or stats.ConsecDays.Value) * 100 ..": "..stats.ConsecDays.Value.." Day Bonus)"
local pre_lp = stats.LP.Value - stats.LPReward.Value
frame:WaitForChild("TotalLP").Text = commanumber(pre_lp)
SetBarOverheadText()

local startingbars = stats.ConsecDays.Value - 1
if startingbars >= 5 then
	for i=1,5 do
		frame:WaitForChild("ConsecDaysBar"):WaitForChild(i).BackgroundColor3 = colors[2]
	end
end
for i=1, (startingbars % 5) do
	frame:WaitForChild("ConsecDaysBar"):WaitForChild(i).BackgroundColor3 = startingbars > 5 and colors[3] or colors[2]
end

module.openWindow(script.Parent.Frame,true)

function TextTransparencyFade(element, totalframes, fadein)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		local i = 0
		local transincrement = (1 - element.TextTransparency) / totalframes
		local strokeincrement = (1 - element.TextStrokeTransparency) / totalframes
		local mult = 1
		if fadein then
			mult = -1
			element.TextTransparency = 1
			element.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
		end
		element.Visible = true
		local render = rs.RenderStepped:connect(function()
			if i < totalframes then
				element.TextTransparency = element.TextTransparency + transincrement * mult
				element.TextStrokeTransparency = element.TextStrokeTransparency + strokeincrement * mult
				i = i + 1
			end
		end)
		repeat wait() until i >= totalframes
		if fadein == false then
			element.Visible = false
			element.TextTransparency = 1 - (transincrement * totalframes)
			element.TextStrokeTransparency = 1 - (strokeincrement * totalframes)
		end
		render:disconnect()
	end),element, totalframes, fadein)
end

function ImageFade(element, totalframes, fadein)
	coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
		local i = 0
		local increment = 1 / totalframes
		local mult = 1
		if fadein then
			mult = -1
			element.ImageTransparency = 1
		else
			element.ImageTransparency = 0
		end
		element.Visible = true
		local render = rs.RenderStepped:connect(function()
			if i < totalframes then
				element.ImageTransparency = element.ImageTransparency + increment * mult
				i = i + 1
			end
		end)
		repeat wait() until i >= totalframes
		if fadein == false then
			element.Visible = false
		end
		render:disconnect()
	end),element, totalframes, fadein)
end

function PlaySound(id,timelength,volume,pitch)
	local sound = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
	sound.Name = "LoyaltySound"
	sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..id
	sound.Volume = volume or 0.5
	sound.Pitch = pitch or 1
	sound:Play()
	game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,timelength or 3)
end

--[[format of spin prize table:
	{  {"ItemName" = "Generic Item Name", "ItemID" = 210, "ImageID" = "rbxassetid://12345678", "PrizeTier" = 1, "IsCash" = false}, [...]  }
	if IsCash, then ItemID is the amount

local spinnercolors = {Color3.new(122/255, 1, 122/255), Color3.new(20/255, 235/255, 20/255), Color3.new(1, 48/255, 7/255), Color3.new(1, 226/255, 0)}

if stats.Spins.Value > 0 then
	
	local prizetable
	
	function ShowSpinPrize(tbl, index, winner)
		local item = tbl[index]
		frame.SpinnerFrame.BackgroundColor3 = spinnercolors[item["PrizeTier"]]
		frame.SpinnerFrame.ItemName.Text = item["ItemName"]
		frame.SpinnerFrame.ItemPic.Image = item["ImageID"]
		PlaySound(203785584,.5,.30,2)
		if winner then --final prize
			PlaySound(131322514,2,.5,1.5)
			if item["PrizeTier"] == 4 then
				PlaySound(345563471,10)
			else
				PlaySound(140413818)
			end
			if item["IsCash"] == true then
				PlaySound(131886985)
			end
			--play star animations?
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PrizeWheelStopped:FireServer()
			SetBarOverheadText()
			frame.SpinnerFrame.Header.Visible = true
			frame.ShopButton.Visible = true
			frame.CloseButton.Visible = true
		else
			frame.SpinnerFrame.Header.Visible = false
		end
	end
	
	frame:TweenSizeAndPosition(UDim2.new(0,360,0,210), UDim2.new(0.5, -180,0.5, -110),...,..., .25,true)
	wait(.25)
	frame.OpenSpinner.Visible = true
	frame.OpenSpinner.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
		TextTransparencyFade(frame.RHSLPLabel, 20, false)
		TextTransparencyFade(frame.TotalLP, 20, false)
		frame.OpenSpinner.Visible = false
		PlaySound(345593426,nil,.8)
		frame:TweenSizeAndPosition(UDim2.new(0,360,0,240), UDim2.new(0.5, -180,0.5, -125),nil,nil, .4,true)
		wait(.32)
		
		local spinning = false
		local cyclerate = 12
		local winningindex
		
		local function CycleSpinner()
			local recentlyusedtable = {}
			local function DupeCheck(i)
				for _,v in pairs(recentlyusedtable) do
					if v == i then
						return true
					end
				end
				return false
			end
			while spinning do
				local indextodisplay
				local winner = false
				if winningindex ~= nil and DupeCheck(winningindex) == false then
					indextodisplay = winningindex
					winner = true
				else
					repeat
						local n = math.random(1,#prizetable)
						if DupeCheck(n) == false then
							indextodisplay = n
						end
					until indextodisplay ~= nil
				end
				table.insert(recentlyusedtable,indextodisplay)
				if #recentlyusedtable > 5 then
					table.remove(recentlyusedtable,1)
				end
				
				ShowSpinPrize(prizetable,indextodisplay,winner)
				
				if winner then
					spinning = false
					if stats.Spins.Value > 0 then
						frame.RespinButton.Visible = true
					end
					return
				end
				
				wait(1/cyclerate)
			end
		end
		
		local function RunSpin()
			winningindex = nil
			frame.RespinButton.Visible = false
			frame.ShopButton.Visible = false
			frame.CloseButton.Visible = false
			cyclerate = 12
			prizetable = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FetchSpinPrizeTable:InvokeServer()
			frame.SpinnerFrame.Visible = true
			frame.StopSpinner.Visible = true
			spinning = true
			spawn(CycleSpinner)
			
			local stopcon
			stopcon = frame.StopSpinner.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
				stopcon:disconnect()
				frame.StopSpinner.Visible = false
				PlaySound(345593426,nil,.8)
				wait(math.random(3,6)/10)
				for i=1,10 do
					wait(.6 + math.random(1,10)/100 - cyclerate / 40)
					cyclerate = cyclerate - 1
				end
				wait(math.random(1,25)/10)
				winningindex = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FetchWinningSpinPrize:InvokeServer()
				local respincon
				respincon = frame.RespinButton.MouseButton1Click:connect(function()
					if stats.Spins.Value > 0 and spinning == false then
						respincon:disconnect()
						PlaySound(345593426,nil,.8)
						RunSpin()
					end
				end)
			end)
		end
		
		RunSpin()
	end)
end

function commanumber(num)
	local num = tostring(num)
	local i = 0
	local finalstring = ""
	for n in string.gmatch(num,"%d") do
		finalstring = finalstring..n
		i = i + 1
		if (#num - i) % 3 == 0 and i ~= #num then
			finalstring = finalstring..","
		end
	end
	return finalstring
end

function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = shopframe.FailureWindow
		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "cash" then
		
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
			if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
				bestprod = v
			end
			if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
				highestprod = v
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		local failure = shopframe.FailureWindow
		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"
		failure.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end

	for _,button in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if button.Name ~= "BaseButton" then

			else
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			if button.ItemID.Value == 288 then
				button.ItemCost.Text = ""
			end
			if plr.SelectedCar.Value == button.ItemID.Value then
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
			end
		end
	end
end

function setNavigationButtonColors()
	local canvaswidth = frame.CanvasSize.X.Offset
	local canvaspos = frame.CanvasPosition.X
	local framewidth = frame.AbsoluteSize.X
	
	local isat
	
	if canvaspos == 0 then
		isat = 0
	elseif canvaspos >= canvaswidth - framewidth then
		isat = 1
	end
	
	if isat == 0 then
		backbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(107, 120, 245)
		backbutton.ImageTransparency = .7
	else
		backbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 244, 120)
		backbutton.ImageTransparency = 0
	end
	
	if isat == 1 then
		forwardbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(107, 120, 245)
		forwardbutton.ImageTransparency = .7
	else
		forwardbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 244, 120)
		forwardbutton.ImageTransparency = 0
	end
end

shopframe.LoadMenu.Event:connect(function() --create all buttons
	--first, clear any existing buttons
	shopframe:WaitForChild("BuyButton").Visible = false
	shopframe:WaitForChild("ItemDesc").Text = ""
	for i,v in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			v:Destroy()
		end
	end
	frame.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0,0,0,0)
	
	local objectstable = {}
	
	local objdata = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects
	for _,v in pairs(objdata:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name:match("vehic_") and not (v:FindFirstChild("OldSchoolCar")) and not (v:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") and data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) == nil) and not ((v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v.ReleaseTime.Value < 0) and game.PlaceId == 9689581) and not (v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") and v.TimeToGoOffSale.Value < game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value and data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) == nil) then
			table.insert(objectstable,v)
		end
	end
	
	local function sortfunc(v1, v2)
		if (v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24) or (v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24) then
			if v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 and (v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") == nil or game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value > 86400*24) then
				return true
			elseif v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 and (v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") == nil or game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value > 86400*24) then
				return false
			elseif v1:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v1.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 and v2:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v2.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24 then
				if v1.ReleaseTime.Value > v2.ReleaseTime.Value then
					return true
				elseif v1.ReleaseTime.Value < v2.ReleaseTime.Value then
					return false
				else
					if v1.ItemID.Value > v2.ItemID.Value then
						return true
					else
						return false
					end
				end
			end
		end
		
		if v1.ItemCost.Value < v2.ItemCost.Value then
			return true
		else
			return false
		end
	end
	
	table.sort(objectstable,sortfunc)
	
	for _,v in pairs(objectstable) do
		local button = frame.BaseButton:clone()
		button.Image = "rbxassetid://"..v.DecalID.Value
		button.ItemID.Value = v.ItemID.Value
		if not data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) then --show cost
			button.ItemCost.Text = "$"..v.ItemCost.Value
		end
		button.Position = UDim2.new(0,(#frame:GetChildren() - 1) * 100,0,0)
		button.Name = string.sub(v.Name,7)
		button.Parent = frame
		if (v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value - v.ReleaseTime.Value < 86400*24) or v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") then
			if v:FindFirstChild("TimeToGoOffSale") then
				button.LimitedBanner.Visible = true

function getItemDataFromID(id)
	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do

			return v
		end

function displayFailureWindow(result,reason) --reason == amount of cash needed
	if result == false then
		
		local failure = shopframe.FailureWindow
		failure.ProductId.Value = 0
		failure.Reason.Text = reason
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = true
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = false
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	elseif result == "cash" then
		
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
		local cash = data.cash.Value
		local products = game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()
		local bestprod
		local highestprod
		for i,v in pairs(products) do
			local diff = tonumber(v.Name) - reason
			if diff >= 0 and (bestprod == nil or tonumber(bestprod.Name) > tonumber(v.Name)) then
				bestprod = v
			end
			if highestprod == nil or tonumber(v.Name) > tonumber(highestprod.Name) then
				highestprod = v
			end
		end
		if bestprod == nil then
			bestprod = highestprod
		end
		
		local failure = shopframe.FailureWindow
		failure.Reason.Text = "You don't have enough Cash to buy this item. Would you like to get more Cash?"
		failure.CashAmount.Value = tonumber(bestprod.Name)
		failure.ProductId.Value = bestprod.Value
		failure.BuyCashButtons.GetCash.Text = "Get +"..bestprod.Name.." Cash!"
		failure.NormalButton.Visible = false
		failure.BuyCashButtons.Visible = true
		failure.Visible = true
		repeat wait() until failure.Visible == false
		
	end
end


function setButtonColors()
	for _,button in pairs(frame:GetChildren()) do
		if button.Name ~= "BaseButton" then
			if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(button.ItemID.Value) then --has object
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(.5,1,.5)
				button.ItemCost.Text = ""
			else
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,1)
			end
			if plr.SelectedPaint.Value == button.ItemID.Value then
				button.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,1,.25)
			end
		end
	end
end

function setNavigationButtonColors()
	local canvaswidth = frame.CanvasSize.X.Offset
	local canvaspos = frame.CanvasPosition.X
	local framewidth = frame.AbsoluteSize.X
	
	local isat
	
	if canvaspos == 0 then
		isat = 0
	elseif canvaspos >= canvaswidth - framewidth then
		isat = 1
	end
	
	if isat == 0 then
		backbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(107, 120, 245)
		backbutton.ImageTransparency = .7
	else
		backbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 244, 120)
		backbutton.ImageTransparency = 0
	end
	
	if isat == 1 then
		forwardbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(107, 120, 245)
		forwardbutton.ImageTransparency = .7
	else
		forwardbutton.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(128, 244, 120)
		forwardbutton.ImageTransparency = 0
	end
end

local jointime = data:WaitForChild("FirstLoginTime").Value
local currenttimeobj = game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime
local timelabel = frame:WaitForChild("LimitedTimeNotice")

function GetTimeString(timeleft)
	if timeleft < 0 then
		return "Ends now!"
	end
	
	local d, h, m, s 
	
	d = math.floor(timeleft / 86400)
	if d == 0 then
		d = ""
	else
		d = d..":"
	end
	
	h = math.floor((timeleft / 3600) % 24)
	if h < 10 then
		if h == 0 then
			if d == "" then
				h = ""
			else
				h = "00:"
			end
		else
			if d == "" then
				h = h..":"
			else
				h = "0"..h..":"
			end
		end
	else
		h = h..":"
	end
	
	m = math.floor((timeleft / 60) % 60)
	if m < 10 then
		if m == 0 then
			if h == "" then
				m = "0:"
			else
				m = "00:"
			end
		else
			if h == "" then
				m = m..":"
			else
				m = "0"..m..":"
			end
		end
	else
		m = m..":"
	end
	
	s = timeleft % 60
	if s < 10 then
		s = "0"..s
	end
	
	return d..h..m..s
end

function SetTimeString()
	local timeleft = (jointime + 86400 * 7) - currenttimeobj.Value
	if timeleft <= 0 then
		timelabel.Text = "Offer about to expire!"
	else
		timelabel.Text = "Offer ends in "..GetTimeString(timeleft)
	end
end

SetTimeString()

currenttimeobj.Changed:connect(function()
	SetTimeString()
end)

module.openWindow(script.Parent.Frame)

end)

local day = game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("Day")

local songholder = game.ReplicatedStorage.SongQueueItems

local songs = {} --the sets in the order that they should play based on the rng

local rng = game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("RNG").Value
local weekdayspassed = game.ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("WeekdaysPassed")
local musicsets = { --4 tables of each music set (1-3 weekdays, 4 weekends) (overnight to evening)
	{503064767,503065215,503066771,503063705},
	{503064767,503064607,503066799,503063839},
	{503064767,503065423,503066871,503063867},

	songs[i] = musicsets[(rng + i * neg) % 3 + 1]
end

local fading = false
local location
--Outside, Club Red, Madi's Music

local avg_loudness = 0
local highest_loudness = 0

local shifting = false --whether the volume is currently being shifted

local defaultid = "rbxassetid://143080540" --the default club music

local current_club_song
local current_queue_item

local current_boombox_sound
local boombox_player

local preloaded_next_song = false

local loudnesses = {}


function fadeSong(sound)
	fading = true
	shifting = true
	local basevol = sound.Volume
	for i=1,20 do
		if sound then
			sound.Volume = sound.Volume - basevol / 20
			wait(.14)
		end
	end
	if sound then
		sound:Destroy()
	end
	shifting = false
	fading = false
end

local firsttime
if script.Parent:FindFirstChild("ACTIVE_IntroGUI") then
	firsttime = true
end

--Shift the volume of the sound according to what the current highest loudness is
--(If starting is true, volume can be shifted up)

function ShiftVolume(sound, starting)
	if sound == nil or shifting == true or sound.SoundId == defaultid then return end
	shifting = true
	local target_volume
	
	if highest_loudness <= 200 then
		target_volume = .9
	elseif highest_loudness <= 300 then
		target_volume = .8 + (300 - highest_loudness) / 1000
	elseif highest_loudness <= 350 then
		target_volume = .7 + (350 - highest_loudness) / 500
	elseif highest_loudness <= 400 then
		target_volume = .6 + (400 - highest_loudness) / 500
	elseif highest_loudness <= 450 then
		target_volume = .5 + (450 - highest_loudness) / 500
	elseif highest_loudness <= 500 then
		target_volume = .5
	elseif highest_loudness <= 675 then
		target_volume = .15 + (675 - highest_loudness) / 500
	elseif highest_loudness > 675 then
		target_volume = .1
	end
	
	if starting ~= true and sound.Volume <= target_volume then shifting = false return end
	
	local vol = sound.Volume
	for i=1,7 do
		if sound then
			sound.Volume = sound.Volume - ((vol - target_volume) / 7)
			wait(1/32)
		end
	end
	
	if target_volume == .1 then --too loud
		if sound and sound.Name == "ClubMusic" then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FlagSong:FireServer()
		elseif sound and sound.Name == "BGM" then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.FlagBoomboxSong:FireServer(boombox_player)
		end
	end
	
	shifting = false
end

function createBackgroundMusic(id,isboombox,musicset,segment)
	if not script.Parent:FindFirstChild("BGM") then
		if script.Parent:FindFirstChild("ClubMusic") and not fading then
			fadeSong(script.Parent.ClubMusic)
		end
		current_club_song = nil
		if firsttime and not isboombox then
			--preload the BGM upon first joining game
			local s = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
			s.SoundId = id
			s.Volume = .001
			s:Play()
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(s,.1)
			wait(4)
			firsttime = false
		end
		local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
		sound.SoundId = id
		sound.Volume = (isboombox and 0) or 10 / 7
		sound.Looped = true
		sound.SoundGroup = game.Soundscape.BackgroundMusic
		sound.Name = "BGM"
		sound.Parent = script.Parent
		local i = 0
		while (sound and sound.IsLoaded == false) or i == 90 do
			wait(1/30)
			i = i + 1
		end
		if isboombox and isboombox.TimePosition > 3.5 then
			sound.TimePosition = isboombox.TimePosition
		end
		if isboombox then
			current_boombox_sound = sound
			highest_loudness = 0
			avg_loudness = 0
			loudnesses = {}
		else
			current_boombox_sound = nil
		end
		sound:Play()
		if not isboombox then
			--preload the next song
			local nextsongid
			local t = game.Lighting:GetMinutesAfterMidnight()
			if day.Value >= 6 and t >= 1050 and t < 1380 then
				musicset = 4
				segment = 1
			else
				segment = segment + 1
				if segment > 4 then
					segment = 1
					musicset = musicset + 1
					if musicset > 4 then
						musicset = 1
					end
				end
			end
			nextsongid = songs[musicset][segment]
			if nextsongid then
				local sound = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
				sound.Volume = .001
				sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..nextsongid
				sound:Play()
				game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,.1)
			end
		end
		if isboombox and vol.Value == 0 then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.LocalNotification:Fire("Your Background Music volume is set to 0%, so you will not hear the Boombox.")
		end
		if isboombox then
			shifting = true
			for i = 1,10 do
				if sound then
					sound.Volume = sound.Volume + 0.06 -- go to 0.6 volume
					wait(1/32)
				end
			end
			shifting = false
			
			ShiftVolume(sound, true)
		end
	end
end


-- Create Club Red DJ music, getting the ID from the queue 

function createClubMusic() 
	if not script.Parent:FindFirstChild("ClubMusic") then
		current_boombox_sound = nil
		loudnesses = {}
		avg_loudness = 0
		highest_loudness = 0
		
		local queueitem
		local nextsong
		preloaded_next_song = false
		for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.SongQueueItems:GetChildren()) do
			if v.QueuePosition.Value == 1 then
				queueitem = v
			elseif v.QueuePosition.Value == 2 then
				nextsong = v 
			end
		end
		
		local starttime
		if queueitem then
			current_queue_item = queueitem
			starttime = game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.GetTimeOfSong:InvokeServer()
			if starttime < 1.5 then starttime = 0 end
		end
		
		local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
		sound.Name = "ClubMusic"
		if queueitem then
			sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..queueitem.SongID.Value
		else
			sound.SoundId = defaultid
		end
		sound.Volume = 0
		sound.TimePosition = starttime
		sound.SoundGroup = game.Soundscape.ClubMusic
		sound.Looped = true
		sound.Parent = script.Parent
		current_club_song = sound
		while (sound and sound.IsLoaded == false) do
			wait(1/30)
		end
		if not sound then return end
		sound:Play()
		
		if nextsong then
			-- Preload the next song 
			local temp = Instance.new("Sound", script.Parent)
			temp.Volume = 0.001
			temp.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..nextsong.SongID.Value
			temp:Play()
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(temp, .1)
			preloaded_next_song = true
		end
		
		shifting = true
		for i = 1,10 do
			if sound then
				sound.Volume = sound.Volume + 0.06 -- go to 0.6 volume
				wait(1/32)
			end
		end
		shifting = false
		
		ShiftVolume(sound, true)
	end
end



function getBackgroundMusic() --get BG music based on time/day
	local day = game.ReplicatedStorage.Day.Value
	local wp = weekdayspassed.Value
	
	local t = game.Lighting:GetMinutesAfterMidnight()
	if t >= 1380 then --get next day's music (late night)
		day = day + 1
		if day == 8 then
			day = 1
		end
	end
	
	local musicset
	if day == 1 or day == 7 then
		musicset = 4
	else
		musicset = (weekdayspassed.Value - 1) % 3 + 1
	end
	
	local segment
	if t >= 1380 or t < 300 then
		segment = 1
	elseif t >= 300 and t < 660 then
		segment = 2
	elseif t >= 660 and t < 1050 then
		segment = 3
	elseif t >= 1050 and t < 1380 then
		segment = 4
	end
	
	local id = "rbxassetid://"..songs[musicset][segment]
	return id,musicset,segment
end

function isInClub(pos)
	if require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.PositionIsInsideArea)(pos,"Club Red") then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end

function isInSchool(pos) --and during class hours
	local day = game.ReplicatedStorage.Day.Value
	local t = game.Lighting:GetMinutesAfterMidnight()
	if require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.PositionIsInsideArea)(pos,"School") and day > 1 and day < 7 and t >= 420 and t < 900 then
		return true
	else
		return false
	end
end


game.Soundscape.ClubMusic.Volume = clubvol.Value / 10
game.Soundscape.BackgroundMusic.Volume = vol.Value / 10

vol.Changed:connect(function(val)
	game.Soundscape.BackgroundMusic.Volume = vol.Value / 10
end)

clubvol.Changed:connect(function(val)
	game.Soundscape.ClubMusic.Volume = clubvol.Value / 10
end)

-- Constantly check for club sound volume

coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
	while true do
		wait(1/12)
		local song = current_club_song or current_boombox_sound
		if (song and song.PlaybackLoudness >= -1) then
			table.insert(loudnesses, song.PlaybackLoudness)
			if #loudnesses > 10 then
				table.remove(loudnesses, 1)
			end
			local avg = 0
			for _,v in pairs(loudnesses) do
				avg = avg + v 
			end
			avg = avg / #loudnesses
			avg_loudness = avg 
			--print("avg_loudness: ".. avg_loudness .." - highest_loudness: ".. highest_loudness .. "num loudnesses: ".. #loudnesses)
			if #loudnesses >= 8 and highest_loudness < avg then
				--print("highest loudness exceeded, shifting")
				highest_loudness = avg 
				ShiftVolume(song)
			end
		end
	end
end))



while true do --main function at the bottom, constantly checking
	wait(.25)
	local char = plr.Character
	if char and char.Parent ~= nil and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
		local torso = char.HumanoidRootPart
		local newlocation = "Outside"
		if isInClub(torso.Position) then

		end
		
		if newlocation and (newlocation ~= location or newlocation == "Outside") then
			location = newlocation
			if location == "Outside" then
				
				local newsong,musicset,segment = getBackgroundMusic() --returns soundid
				local isboombox
				
				if boomboxsetting.Value == true and not isInSchool(torso.Position) then
					for _,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
						if v.Character and v.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
							local boombox = v.Character:FindFirstChild("Boombox") or v.Character:FindFirstChild("Golden Boombox")
							if boombox and (torso.Position - v.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position).magnitude < 40 then
								local boomsound = boombox:FindFirstChild("BoomboxSound")
								if boomsound then
									local id = boomsound.SoundId
									if id ~= "" then
										isboombox = boomsound
										newsong = id
										break
									end
								end
							end
						end
					end
				end
				
				local bgm = script.Parent:FindFirstChild("BGM")
				if bgm and bgm.SoundId == newsong then
					--no change
				elseif bgm and bgm.SoundId ~= newsong then
					fadeSong(bgm)
					createBackgroundMusic(newsong,isboombox,musicset,segment)
					if isboombox then
						boombox_player = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(isboombox.Parent.Parent)
					end
				else
					createBackgroundMusic(newsong,isboombox,musicset,segment)
				end
			
			elseif location == "Club Red" then
				
				local bgm = script.Parent:FindFirstChild("BGM")
				if bgm then
					fadeSong(bgm)
				end
				createClubMusic()
				
			end
			
		elseif newlocation == "Club Red" then
			-- do checks on the current song playing - whether it's time to switch, or preload new song 
			local queueitem
			local nextsong
			for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.SongQueueItems:GetChildren()) do
				if v.QueuePosition.Value == 1 then
					queueitem = v
				elseif v.QueuePosition.Value == 2 then
					nextsong = v 
				end
			end
			
			if queueitem ~= current_queue_item then --song change
				current_queue_item = queueitem
				if script.Parent:FindFirstChild("ClubMusic") then
					fadeSong(script.Parent.ClubMusic)
				end
				createClubMusic()
			end
			
			if nextsong and not preloaded_next_song then --preload 
				local sound = Instance.new("Sound",script.Parent)
				sound.Volume = .001
				sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://"..nextsong.SongID.Value
				sound:Play()
				game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound,.1)
				preloaded_next_song = true
			end
		end
		
	end
end

else

function GetTimeString(timeleft)
	if timeleft < 0 then
		return "Ends now!"
	end
	
	local d, h, m, s 
	
	d = math.floor(timeleft / 86400)
	if d == 0 then
		d = ""
	else
		d = d..":"
	end
	
	h = math.floor((timeleft / 3600) % 24)
	if h < 10 then
		if h == 0 then
			if d == "" then
				h = ""
			else
				h = "00:"
			end
		else
			if d == "" then
				h = h..":"
			else
				h = "0"..h..":"
			end
		end
	else
		h = h..":"
	end
	
	m = math.floor((timeleft / 60) % 60)
	if m < 10 then
		if m == 0 then
			if h == "" then
				m = "0:"
			else
				m = "00:"
			end
		else
			if h == "" then
				m = m..":"
			else
				m = "0"..m..":"
			end
		end
	else
		m = m..":"
	end
	
	s = timeleft % 60
	if s < 10 then
		s = "0"..s
	end
	
	return d..h..m..s
end

function SetTimeString()
	local timeleft = (jointime + 86400 * 7) - currenttimeobj.Value
	timelabel.Text = GetTimeString(timeleft)
	if timeleft < 86400 then
		timelabel.TextColor3 = Color3.new(.85,0,0)
		timelabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	end
end

SetTimeString()

currenttimeobj.Changed:connect(function()
	SetTimeString()
end)

		local f = Instance.new("Folder",plr)

end)

function Teleport(destinationpart)
	local tpgui = Instance.new("ScreenGui",script.Parent)
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")
	frame.Size = UDim2.new(2,0,2,0)
	frame.Position = UDim2.new(-.5,0,-.5,0)
	frame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	frame.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0,0,0)
	frame.Parent = tpgui
	
	for i=1,7 do
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = frame.BackgroundTransparency - .15
		wait(1/35)
	end
	
	if destinationpart.Parent.Name == "WarpPipeMadi" and destinationpart.Parent.Parent.Parent ~= workspace then
		--load the island into the workspace
		destinationpart.Parent.Parent.Parent = workspace
	end
	
	hrp.CFrame = destinationpart.CFrame + destinationpart.CFrame.lookVector * 10
	hrp.Velocity = Vector3.new(0,0,0)
	hrp.Anchored = true
	
	wait(1.6)
	
	hrp.Anchored = false
		
	for i=1,7 do
		frame.BackgroundTransparency = frame.BackgroundTransparency + .15
		wait(1/35)
	end
	
	tpgui:Destroy()
end

--Made by Luckymaxer

Board = script.Parent
Model = Board.Parent

MaxSpeed = 40

Players = game:GetService("Players")
ContentProvider = game:GetService("ContentProvider")
UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

ActiveAnimations = {}

RemoteControl = Board:WaitForChild("RemoteControl")
Animations = Board:WaitForChild("Animations")

ServerControl = RemoteControl:WaitForChild("ServerControl")
ClientControl = RemoteControl:WaitForChild("ClientControl")

Animations = {
	BoardKick = Animations:WaitForChild("BoardKick"),
	CoastingPose = Animations:WaitForChild("CoastingPose"),
	LeftTurn = Animations:WaitForChild("LeftTurn"),
	RightTurn = Animations:WaitForChild("RightTurn"),
	Ollie = Animations:WaitForChild("Ollie")
}

Sounds = {
	BoardDrop = Board:WaitForChild("BoardDrop"),
	BoardOllie = Board:WaitForChild("BoardOllie"),
	BoardStop = Board:WaitForChild("BoardStop"),
	CruiseLoop = Board:WaitForChild("CruiseLoop")
}

LastAction = nil
LastTime = nil
JumpEnabled = true

SkateboardEquipped = false

--ClientControl.OnClientInvoke = (function(player, Mode, Value)
--end)

function InvokeServer(Mode, Value)
	local ServerReturn = nil
	pcall(function()
		ServerReturn = ServerControl:InvokeServer(Mode, Value)
	end)
	return ServerReturn
end

function SetAnimation(Mode, Value)
	if Mode == "Play" and Value and SkateboardEquipped and CheckIfAlive() then
		for i, v in pairs(ActiveAnimations) do
			if v.Animation == Value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop()
				table.remove(ActiveAnimations, i)
			end
		end
		local AnimationTrack = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Value.Animation)
		table.insert(ActiveAnimations, {Animation = Value.Animation, AnimationTrack = AnimationTrack})
		AnimationTrack:Play(Value.FadeTime, Value.Weight, Value.Speed)
	elseif Mode == "Stop" and Value then
		for i, v in pairs(ActiveAnimations) do
			if v.Animation == Value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop(Value.FadeTime)
				table.remove(ActiveAnimations, i)
			end
		end
	end
end

function DidAction(Action)
	LastAction = Action
	LastTime = time()
end

function AxisChanged(Axis)
	
	Board.Steer = Controller.Steer
	Board.Throttle = Controller.Throttle
	
	if Board.Steer == -1 then --Left turn
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		DidAction("Left")
	elseif Board.Steer == 1 then --Right turn
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		DidAction("Right")
	elseif Board.Steer == 0 then --Forward/Backward
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		if (LastAction == "Go" and time() - LastTime < 0.1) then
		end
		DidAction("Go")
		if not Sounds.CruiseLoop.IsPlaying then
			Sounds.CruiseLoop:Play()
		end
	end
end

function EnterAirState(Boolean)
	if Boolean then
		Gyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(100, 0, 100)
		Stabilizer.maxTorque = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		JumpEnabled = false
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
		Sounds.BoardOllie:Play()
	else
		Gyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
		Stabilizer.maxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
		JumpEnabled = true
		Sounds.BoardDrop:Play()
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
	end
end

function EnterPushingState()
	SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
end

function EnterCoastingState()
	if not Sounds.CruiseLoop.IsPlaying then
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Play()
	end
end

function EnterStoppingState()
	Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
	BreakModifyAndPlay()
end

function MoveStateChanged(NewState, OldState)
	if not JumpEnabled then
		EnterAirState(false)
	end
	if NewState == Enum.MoveState.AirFree then
		EnterAirState(true)
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Pushing then
		EnterPushingState()
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Coasting then
		EnterCoastingState()
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Stopping then
		EnterStoppingState()
	end
end

function MakeOllieThrust()
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	OllieThrust = Instance.new("BodyThrust")
	OllieThrust.Name = "OllieThrust"
	OllieThrust.force = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	OllieThrust.location = Vector3.new(0, 0, -2)
	OllieThrust.Parent = Board
	Stabilizer = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
	Stabilizer.Name = "Stabilizer"
	Stabilizer.maxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
	Stabilizer.Parent = Board
end

function MakeGyro()
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	Gyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
	Gyro.Name = "SkateboardGyro"
	Gyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	Gyro.P = 50
	Gyro.D = 50
	Gyro.Parent = Torso
end

function ButtonChanged(Button,Mobile)
	local ButtonState = Controller:GetButton(Button)
	if not ButtonState and not Mobile then
		return
	end
	if Button == Enum.Button.Dismount then
		Board.ControllingHumanoid.Jump = ButtonState
	elseif Button == Enum.Button.Jump then
		if JumpEnabled and math.abs(Board.Velocity.Y) < 5 then
			JumpEnabled = false
			SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
			SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
			SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.RIghtTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
			SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.Ollie, FadeTime = 0, Weight = 1, Speed = 4})
			Board.StickyWheels = false
			Board:ApplySpecificImpulse(Vector3.new(0, 45, 0))
			OllieThrust.force = Vector3.new(0, 1200, 0)
			DidAction("Jump")
			Delay(0.1, function()
				if OllieThrust then
					OllieThrust.force = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
				end
			end)
		else
			Board.StickyWheels = true
		end
	end
end

function CruiseModify()
	local Velocity = Board.Velocity.magnitude
	local LowPitch = 0.85
	local TopPitch = 1.1
	local LowV = 0.1
	local TopV = 1
	if Velocity < 1 then
		Sounds.CruiseLoop.Volume = 0
		return
	end
	Sounds.CruiseLoop.Volume = math.min((((TopV - LowV) * (Velocity / MaxSpeed)) + LowV), 1)
	Sounds.CruiseLoop.Pitch = (((TopPitch - LowPitch) * (Velocity / MaxSpeed)) + LowPitch)
end

function BreakModifyAndPlay()
	local v = Board.Velocity.magnitude
	local Slowest = 10
	local LowV = 0.05
	local TopV = 0.8
	if (v < Slowest) then
		return
	end
	Sounds.BoardStop.Volume = math.min((((TopV - LowV) * (v / (MaxSpeed - Slowest))) + LowV), 1)
	Sounds.BoardStop:Play()
end

function CheckIfAlive()
	return (((Player and Player.Parent and Humanoid and Humanoid.Parent and Humanoid.Health > 0) and true) or false)
end

function Equipped(humanoid, controller)
	Controller = controller
	Humanoid = humanoid
	Character = Humanoid.Parent
	Player = Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Character)
	PlayerGui = Player:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui")
	Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	for i, v in pairs({KeyDownConnection, KeyUpConnection}) do
		if v then
			v:disconnect()
		end
	end

LightSpeedEffectGui = script:WaitForChild("LightSpeedEffectGui")

RemoteControl = Board:WaitForChild("RemoteControl")
Animations = Board:WaitForChild("Animations")

ServerControl = RemoteControl:WaitForChild("ServerControl")
ClientControl = RemoteControl:WaitForChild("ClientControl")

Animations = {
	BoardKick = Animations:WaitForChild("BoardKick"),
	CoastingPose = Animations:WaitForChild("CoastingPose"),
	LeftTurn = Animations:WaitForChild("LeftTurn"),
	RightTurn = Animations:WaitForChild("RightTurn"),
	Ollie = Animations:WaitForChild("Ollie")
}

	BoardDrop = Board:WaitForChild("BoardDrop"),
	BoardOllie = Board:WaitForChild("BoardOllie"),

function InvokeServer(Mode, Value)
	local ServerReturn = nil
	pcall(function()
		ServerReturn = ServerControl:InvokeServer(Mode, Value)
	end)
	return ServerReturn
end

function SetAnimation(Mode, Value)
	if Mode == "Play" and Value and SkateboardEquipped and CheckIfAlive() then
		for i, v in pairs(ActiveAnimations) do
			if v.Animation == Value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop()
				table.remove(ActiveAnimations, i)
			end
		end
		local AnimationTrack = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Value.Animation)
		table.insert(ActiveAnimations, {Animation = Value.Animation, AnimationTrack = AnimationTrack})
		AnimationTrack:Play(Value.FadeTime, Value.Weight, Value.Speed)
	elseif Mode == "Stop" and Value then
		for i, v in pairs(ActiveAnimations) do
			if v.Animation == Value.Animation then
				v.AnimationTrack:Stop(Value.FadeTime)
				table.remove(ActiveAnimations, i)
			end
		end
	end
end

function UpdateGui()
	while ReloadPercent < 100 do
		wait(ReloadTime / 100)
		Gui.Bar.Fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1,0,0)
		ReloadPercent = math.min(100, (ReloadPercent + 1))
		Gui.Bar.NameLabel.Text = "Speed Boost"
		Gui.Bar.PercentLabel.Text = (tostring(ReloadPercent) .. "%")
		Gui.Bar.Fill.Size = UDim2.new((-1 * ReloadPercent / 100), 0, 0, 6)
	end
	Gui.Bar.Fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
	Gui.Bar.NameLabel.Text = "Speed Boost"
	Gui.Bar.PercentLabel.Text = "100%"
	Gui.Bar.Fill.Size = UDim2.new(-1, 0, 0, 6)
end

function DrainGui()
	while ReloadPercent > 0 do
		wait(Duration / 100)
		Gui.Bar.Fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(0, 1, 0)
		ReloadPercent = math.max(0, (ReloadPercent - 1))
		Gui.Bar.NameLabel.Text = "Speed Boost"
		Gui.Bar.PercentLabel.Text = (tostring(ReloadPercent) .. "%")
		Gui.Bar.Fill.Size = UDim2.new((-1 * ReloadPercent / 100), 0, 0, 6)
	end
end

function ShowBoostGui()
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	
	local EffectGui = LightSpeedEffectGui:Clone()
	local Whirl = EffectGui.Whirl
	local Background = Whirl.Background
	local Text = Whirl.TextLabel
	
	for i, v in pairs({Whirl, Background, Text}) do
		if v:IsA("TextLabel") then
			v.TextColor3 = DisplayColor
		elseif v:IsA("ImageLabel") then
			v.ImageColor3 = DisplayColor
		end
	end
	
	Debris:AddItem(EffectGui, 5)
	EffectGui.Parent = PlayerGui	
	
	if PlayerGui and PlayerGui.Parent then
		EffectGui.Parent = PlayerGui
	end
	
	for i = 1, 2.5, 0.02 do
		Text.TextTransparency = (Text.TextTransparency + 0.05)
		Text.TextStrokeTransparency = Text.TextTransparency
		Whirl.Size = UDim2.new(i, 1, i, 1)
		Whirl.Position = UDim2.new((0.5 - i / 2), 0, (0.5 - i / 2), 0)
		wait(0.04)
	end
	
	if EffectGui and EffectGui.Parent then
		EffectGui:Destroy()
	end
end

function GetAllConnectedParts(Object)
	local Parts = {}
	local function GetConnectedParts(Object)
		for i, v in pairs(Object:GetConnectedParts()) do
			local Ignore = false
			for ii, vv in pairs(Parts) do
				if v == vv then
					Ignore = true
				end
			end
			if not Ignore then
				table.insert(Parts, v)
				GetConnectedParts(v)
			end
		end
	end
	GetConnectedParts(Object)
	return Parts
end

function ThrustUpdater()
	while SkateboardEquipped do
		RotationForce.angularvelocity = Vector3.new(0, TurnSpeed, 0)
		
		local Direction = Board.CFrame.lookVector
		Direction = Vector3.new(Direction.X, 0, Direction.Z).unit
		ThrustForce.velocity = (Direction * CurrentSpeed)

		if math.abs(TurnSpeed) > AlphaDampening then
			TurnSpeed = (TurnSpeed - (AlphaDampening * (math.abs(TurnSpeed) / TurnSpeed)))
		else 
			TurnSpeed = 0
		end
		local LeanAmount = (-TurnSpeed * (math.pi / 6) / 0.8)
		TurnGyro.cframe = CFrame.Angles((LeanAmount * Direction.X), 0, (LeanAmount * Direction.Z))
		
		--Compare CurrentSpeed (speed supposed to be traveling at) to actual velocity.
		local BoardVelocity = Vector3.new(Board.Velocity.X, 0, Board.Velocity.Z).magnitude
		if CurrentSpeed > 5 and (BoardVelocity + 6) < CurrentSpeed then
			CurrentSpeed = math.max((CurrentSpeed - 7),0)
		end
		
		wait(WaitTime)
	end
end

function BoostSpeed(Speed)
	EnterPushingState()
	MaxSpeed = (MaxSpeed + Speed)
	CurrentSpeed = MaxSpeed
	Spawn(function()
		ShowBoostGui()
	end)
	DrainGui()
	MaxSpeed = BaseMaxSpeed
	UpdateGui()
	JumpEnabled = true
end


function DidAction(Action)
	LastAction = Action
	LastTime = time()
end

function AxisChanged(Axis)
	print("AxisChanged:",Axis)
	Board.Steer = Controller.Steer
	Board.Throttle = Controller.Throttle
	
	CurrentSteer = Board.Steer
	
	if CurrentSteer == -1 then --Left turn
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		DidAction("Left")
		while Board.Steer == -1 do
			TurnSpeed = math.min(TurnMax, (TurnSpeed + TurnAlpha))
			wait(WaitTime)
		end
	elseif CurrentSteer == 1 then --Right turn
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		DidAction("Right")
		while Board.Steer == 1 do
			TurnSpeed = math.max((-1 * TurnMax), (TurnSpeed - TurnAlpha))
			wait(WaitTime)
		end
	elseif CurrentSteer == 0 then --Forward/Backward
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.LeftTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.Ollie})
		SetAnimation("Stop", {Animation = Animations.RightTurn, FadeTime = 0.5})
		if (LastAction == "Go" and time() - LastTime < 0.1) then
		end
		DidAction("Go")
		if not Sounds.CruiseLoop.IsPlaying then
			Sounds.CruiseLoop:Play()
		end
	end
end

function ThrottleChanged(axis)
	print("ThrottleChanged:",axis)
	if Controller.Throttle ~= CurrentThrottle then
		CurrentThrottle = Controller.Throttle
		if CurrentThrottle > 0 then
			while CurrentThrottle > 0 do
				CurrentSpeed = math.min(MaxSpeed, (CurrentSpeed + (Acceleration * (WaitTime))))
				wait(WaitTime)
			end
		end
		if CurrentThrottle < 0 then
			while CurrentThrottle < 0 do
				CurrentSpeed = math.max((-1 * MaxSpeed), (CurrentSpeed - (Deceleration * (WaitTime))))
				wait(WaitTime)
			end
		end
	end
end

function EnterAirState(Boolean)
	if Boolean then
		JumpEnabled = false
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
		Sounds.BoardOllie:Play()
	else
		JumpEnabled = true
		Sounds.BoardDrop:Play()
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
	end
end

function EnterPushingState()
	SetAnimation("Play", {Animation = Animations.BoardKick})
end

function EnterCoastingState()
	if not Sounds.CruiseLoop.IsPlaying then
		Sounds.CruiseLoop:Play()
	end
end

function EnterStoppingState()
	Sounds.CruiseLoop:Stop()
	BreakModifyAndPlay()
end

function MoveStateChanged(NewState, OldState)
	if not JumpEnabled then
		EnterAirState(false)
	end
	if NewState == Enum.MoveState.AirFree then
		EnterAirState(true)
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Pushing then
--		EnterPushingState()
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Coasting then
		EnterCoastingState()
	elseif NewState == Enum.MoveState.Stopping then
		EnterStoppingState()
	end
end

function SetUpControl()
	if not CheckIfAlive() then
		return
	end
	RotationForce = Instance.new("BodyAngularVelocity")
	RotationForce.maxTorque = Vector3.new(0, math.huge, 0)
	RotationForce.angularvelocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	RotationForce.Parent = Torso

	ThrustForce = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
	ThrustForce.maxForce = Vector3.new(30000, 0, 30000)
	ThrustForce.maxForce = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
	ThrustForce.velocity = Vector3.new(0, 0, 0)
	ThrustForce.P = 800
	ThrustForce.Parent = Board
	
	TurnGyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
	TurnGyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(math.huge, 0, math.huge)
	TurnGyro.Parent = Torso
	
	local Mass = 0
	for i, v in pairs(GetAllConnectedParts(Board)) do
		Mass = (Mass + v:GetMass() * EqualizingForce * Gravity)
	end
	
	AntiGrav = Instance.new("BodyForce")
	AntiGrav.force = Vector3.new(0, (Mass / 2), 0)
	AntiGrav.Parent = Board
	
end

function ButtonChanged(Button)
	local ButtonState = Controller:GetButton(Button)
	if not ButtonState then
		return
	end
	if Button == Enum.Button.Dismount then
		Board.ControllingHumanoid.Jump = ButtonState
	elseif Button == Enum.Button.Jump then
		if JumpEnabled and ReloadPercent == 100 then
			JumpEnabled = false
			BoostSpeed(20)
		end
	end
end

function CruiseModify()
	local Velocity = Board.Velocity.magnitude
	local LowPitch = 0.85
	local TopPitch = 1.1
	local LowV = 0.1
	local TopV = 1
	if Velocity < 1 then
		Sounds.CruiseLoop.Volume = 0
		return
	end
	Sounds.CruiseLoop.Volume = math.min((((TopV - LowV) * (Velocity / TopSpeed)) + LowV), 1)
	Sounds.CruiseLoop.Pitch = (((TopPitch - LowPitch) * (Velocity / MaxSpeed)) + LowPitch)
end

function BreakModifyAndPlay()
	local v = Board.Velocity.magnitude
	local Slowest = 10
	local LowV = 0.05
	local TopV = 0.8
	if (v < Slowest) then
		return
	end
	Sounds.BoardStop.Volume = math.min((((TopV - LowV) * (v / (MaxSpeed - Slowest))) + LowV), 1)
	Sounds.BoardStop:Play()
end

function CheckIfAlive()
	return (((Player and Player.Parent and Humanoid and Humanoid.Parent and Humanoid.Health > 0 and Head and Head.Parent and Torso and Torso.Parent) and true) or false)
end

local plr = game.Players.LocalPlayer
local mouse = plr:GetMouse()
local enabled = true
local playermouse

local range = script.Parent:WaitForChild("TeleportRange").Value

local cd = 2

local localeffect = plr.PlayerGui:WaitForChild("TeleportEffect")

function standUp(char)
	if char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
		char.Humanoid.Jump = true
	end
end

function checkIfStanding(char)
	if char and char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
		if char.Humanoid.SeatPart == nil then
			return true
		else
			return false
		end
	end
end

function enable()
	if playermouse then
		playermouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunCursor.png"
	end
	enabled = true
end

function disable()
	if playermouse then
		playermouse.Icon = "rbxasset://textures\\GunWaitCursor.png"
	end
	enabled = false
end

function activate(targetpos,mag)
	local char = plr.Character
	if char and enabled then
		local torso = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local hum = char:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if torso and hum and hum.Health > 0 then
			disable()
			
			local origin = torso.Position
			local ray1 = Ray.new(origin, (targetpos - origin).unit * mag)
			
			local ignorelist = {}
	
			for i,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
				if v.Character then
					table.insert(ignorelist,v.Character)
				end
			end
			
			local hit,destination
			local collision = false
			
			hit,destination = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray1,ignorelist)
			if hit then
				destination = destination - ray1.Unit.Direction * 1.5
				collision = true
			end
			
			if collision then
				local neworigin = targetpos + Vector3.new(0,17,0)
				local ray2 = Ray.new(neworigin, ((neworigin - Vector3.new(0,17,0)) - neworigin).unit * 16)
				local hit2,destination2
				hit2,destination2 = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray2, ignorelist)
				if not hit2 then
					destination = targetpos
				end
			end
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PlayerHasTeleported:FireServer(origin,destination,script.Parent)
			
			localeffect:Fire(origin,destination,script.Parent)
			if checkIfStanding(char) == false then
				standUp(char)
			end
			wait(.15)
			if torso and checkIfStanding(char) == true then
				torso.CFrame = CFrame.new(destination)
			end
			
			wait(cd)
			
			enable()
			
		end
	end
end


script.Parent.Equipped:connect(function(mouse)
	playermouse = mouse
	equipped = true
	mouse.Button1Down:connect(function()
		if enabled and equipped then
			local char = plr.Character
			if char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
				
				local mag = (char.HumanoidRootPart.Position - (mouse.Hit.p + Vector3.new(0,3,0))).magnitude
				if mag > range then
					mag = range
				end
				local ray = Ray.new(char.HumanoidRootPart.Position, (mouse.Hit.p + Vector3.new(0,3,0) - char.HumanoidRootPart.Position).unit * mag)
				local target = char.HumanoidRootPart.Position + (mouse.Hit.p + Vector3.new(0,3,0) - char.HumanoidRootPart.Position).unit * mag

				activate(target,mag)
			end
			
		end
	end)
end)

script.Parent.Unequipped:connect(function()
	playermouse = nil
	equipped = false
end)

		lastposition = part.Position
	end

local selectionBox

local selectedButtonTable = {}
local oldButton = nil
function onMouseLeave(hoverSelection)
	if oldButton ~= nil then
		local notSelected = true
		local selectionText = ""
		for key, value in pairs(selectedButtonTable) do
			if oldButton == value then
				notSelected = false
			else
				selectionText = value.BackgroundColor.Name
			end
		end
		if notSelected then
			hoverSelection.Text = selectionText
			oldButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
		end
	end
	oldButton = nil
end

function onMouseEnter(hoverSelection, guiButton)
	onMouseLeave(hoverSelection)
	hoverSelection.Text = guiButton.BackgroundColor.Name
	if guiButton ~= selectedButton then
		guiButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.White()
		oldButton = guiButton
	end
end

function onMouseUp(colorHolder, paletteFrame, guiButton)
	if selectedButtonTable[colorHolder] ~= nil then
		selectedButtonTable[colorHolder].Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Black()
	end
	guiButton.Parent.BackgroundColor = BrickColor.Yellow()
	colorHolder.BackgroundColor = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	selectionBox.Color = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	selectionLasso.Color = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	Tool.Value.Value = guiButton.BackgroundColor
	selectedButtonTable[colorHolder] = guiButton
	
	onMouseLeavePalette(paletteFrame)
	
	print("Selected Color: " .. guiButton.BackgroundColor.Name)
end

function onShowColorDialog(paletteFrame)
	paletteFrame.Visible = true
end

	unsetSelectionBox()
	selectionBox.Adornee = part.Parent
	selectionLasso.Part = part.Parent.PrimaryPart
end
function unsetSelectionBox() 
	selectionBox.Adornee = nil
	selectionLasso.Part = nil
end

function buildGui(root, mouse)

			if sideBar and primaryColor and sideBar.Parent ~= nil and paletteFrame and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Gamepad1 and not gameprocessed then
				if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonX then
					if paletteFrame.Visible then
						paletteFrame.Visible = false
					else
						paletteFrame.Visible = true
						local toselect = primaryColor
						for _,v in pairs(paletteFrame:GetChildren()) do
							if v:IsA("Frame") and v:FindFirstChild("TextButton") then
								if v.TextButton.BackgroundColor3 == primaryColor.BackgroundColor then
									toselect = v
								end
							end
						end
						GuiService.SelectedObject = toselect
					end
				elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonB or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.ButtonSelect then
					if paletteFrame.Visible then
						paletteFrame.Visible = false
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	
	mainFrame.MouseLeave:connect(function() onMouseLeave(hoverSelection) end)

end

function canSelectCanvas(part)
	if part ~= nil then
		return (part.Position - Tool.Parent.Head.Position).Magnitude < 16
	end
end

function Paint(canvas,position)
	
	if canSelectCanvas(canvas) then
		--primaryColor.BackgroundColor
		module.PlacePaint(game.Players.LocalPlayer, canvas, position, mode.Value, script.Parent.Value.Value)
		
	end
end

local mouseDown = false
function on3dButton1Down(mouse) 
	mouseDown = true
	while mouseDown do
		local canvas = mouse.Target
		local pos = mouse.Hit.p
		if canvas and canvas.Name == "!Canvas" then
			if lastposition == nil or (lastposition - pos).magnitude > .033 then
				Paint(canvas,pos)
			end
		else
			mouseDown = false
		end
		wait(1/30)
	end
	
end

function on3dButton1Up(mouse) 
	mouseDown = false
	lastposition = nil
end

	end
end

--imports
local RbxUtility = LoadLibrary('RbxUtility')
local Create = RbxUtility.Create

--main vars
local Tool = script.Parent
local Equipped = false
local Flying = false

--character stuff
local Character, Player, Humanoid, Torso;

--anim tracks
local GliderHold, GliderFly, GliderBomb;


---========================== main code ==========================--

local FlyingGyro;
local FlyingForce;

local DesiredFlightCFrame;

local Gui;

local MobileCooldown = false --can't start again immediately after jumping
local UnequipCooldown = false
local AboutToFly = false
local IsMobile = false

local function BeginFlight()
	if Flying or MobileCooldown or not Equipped or AboutToFly then return end
	Humanoid.Jump = true
	AboutToFly = true
	wait(0.2)
	AboutToFly = false
	if Flying or not Equipped then return end
	Flying = true
	UnequipCooldown = true
	if not IsMobile then
		game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = Tool.Handle
		game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = 'Track'
	end
	Humanoid.Sit = true
	FlyingGyro = Create'BodyGyro'{
		Parent = Torso;
		maxTorque = Vector3.new(0,0,0);
		cframe = Torso.CFrame;
	}
	FlyingForce = Create'BodyForce'{
		Parent = Torso;
		force = Vector3.new(0,0,0);
	}
	Spawn(function()
		for i = 0, 1, 0.1 do
			FlyingGyro.maxTorque = Vector3.new(i*10000,i*10000,i*10000)
		end
	end)
	GliderFly:Play(0.3)
	DesiredFlightCFrame = (Torso.CFrame - Torso.CFrame.p)*CFrame.Angles(0, 0, 0.01)
	Gui.Frame.Message.Text = --Fix implemented by Luckymaxer
		"Click and Hold to direct yourself.\n"..
		"Jump to stop gliding."
	wait(1)
	UnequipCooldown = false
end

local function EndFlight(forceremove)
	if UnequipCooldown == false or forceremove then
		Flying = false
		MobileCooldown = true
		if not IsMobile then
			game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject = Character
			game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType = 'Custom'
		end
		GliderFly:Stop(0.3)
		Humanoid.Sit = false
		FlyingGyro:Destroy()
		FlyingForce:Destroy()
		--Torso.Velocity = Vector3.new(0,0,0)
		Torso.RotVelocity = Vector3.new(0,0,0)
		Torso.CFrame = CFrame.new(Torso.Position)
		Gui.Frame.Message.Text = "Click to Glide."
		wait(1)
		MobileCooldown = false
	end
end


local function GetMassOf(model)
	if model:IsA('BasePart') then
		return model:GetMass()
	else
		local mass = 0
		for _, ch in pairs(model:GetChildren()) do
			mass = mass + GetMassOf(ch)
		end
		return mass
	end
end


local LastBombAt = tick()
local RELOAD_TIME = 0.8
local function DropBomb()
	if not Flying then return end
	if tick() - LastBombAt > RELOAD_TIME then
		LastBombAt = tick()
		--
		GliderBomb:Play()
		--
		local bomb = Create'Part'{
			Size = Vector3.new(1,2,1);
			TopSurface = 'Smooth';
			BottomSurface = 'Smooth';
			Archivable = false;
			Position = (Character['Left Arm'].CFrame*CFrame.new(0,-1,0)).p - Vector3.new(0,2,0);
			--
			Create'SpecialMesh'{
				MeshId = 'http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=90793198';
				TextureId = 'http://www.roblox.com/asset/?id=90793278';
			};
			--
			Create'Sound'{
				Name = 'ExplodeSound';
				SoundId = 'rbxasset://sounds//Rocket shot.wav';
			};
		}
		--
		local sc = Tool.MineScript:Clone()
		sc.Disabled = false
		sc.creator.Value = Player
		sc.Parent = bomb
		--
		bomb.Parent = game.Workspace
	end
end

local function Slerp(t, a, b)
	local om = math.acos(math.min(1, a:Dot(b)))
	if om < 0.01 then
		return (1-t)*a +
		          t *b
	else
		return (math.sin((1-t)*om)/math.sin(om))*a +
		       (math.sin(   t *om)/math.sin(om))*b
	end
end

Tool.Handle.Touched:connect(function(part)
	if Equipped and not part:IsDescendantOf(Character) and Flying then
		EndFlight()
	end
end)

Tool.Equipped:connect(function(mouse)
	Character = Tool.Parent
	Player = game.Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(Character)
	Humanoid = Character.Humanoid
	Torso = Character:FindFirstChild("Torso") or Character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")

	if Humanoid.RigType == Enum.HumanoidRigType.R15 then
		GliderFly = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Tool.ANIM_FlyR15)
	else
		GliderFly =  Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Tool.ANIM_Fly )
	end
	GliderBomb = Humanoid:LoadAnimation(Tool.ANIM_Bomb)
	--
	GliderHold:Play()
	--
	local MouseDown = false
	local KeyState
	mouse.Button1Down:connect(function()
		MouseDown = true
		if not Flying then
			BeginFlight()
		end
	end)
	mouse.Button1Up:connect(function()
		MouseDown = false
	end)
	mouse.KeyDown:connect(function(key)
		if key == ' ' then
			if Flying then
				EndFlight()
			end
		elseif key:byte() == 48 or key:byte() == 45 or key:byte() == 50 or 
		       key:byte() == 52 or key:byte() == 13 or key:lower() == 'q' or key:lower() == 'e' 
		then
			--DropBomb()
		end
	end)
	local Emulation = Player:FindFirstChild("KeyboardEmulation")
	if Emulation then
		IsMobile = true
		oldmouse = mouse
		for _,v in pairs(Emulation:GetChildren()) do
			v.Changed:connect(function(val)
				if Equipped and oldmouse == mouse then
					if v.Name == " " and val == true then
						if Flying then
							EndFlight()
						end
					elseif v.Name == "MouseIsDown" then
						if val == true then
							MouseDown = true
							if not Flying then
								BeginFlight()
							end
						else
							MouseDown = false
						end
					end
				end
				--InvokeServer("KeyPress", {Key = v.Name, Down = val})
			end)
		end
	end
	--
	Gui = Create'ScreenGui'{
		Parent = Player.PlayerGui;
		Create'Frame'{
			Name = 'Frame';
			Style = 'RobloxRound';
			Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -125, 1, -170);
			Size = UDim2.new(0, 250, 0, 80);
			Create'TextLabel'{
				Name = 'Message';
				TextWrapped = true;
				BackgroundTransparency = 1;
				Font = 'Arial';
				FontSize = 'Size14';
				TextColor3 = Color3.new(0.9, 0.9, 0.9);
				Text = "Click to Glide";
				Size = UDim2.new(1,0,1,0);
			};
		};
	}
	--
	local LastTime = tick()
	while true do
		local Now = tick()
		local dt = Now - LastTime
		LastTime = Now
		--
		if Flying then
			--make me fly
			local moveDir = game.Workspace.CurrentCamera.CoordinateFrame*CFrame.Angles(0.6, 0, 0)
			if MouseDown then
				local theta = math.acos(DesiredFlightCFrame.lookVector:Dot(moveDir.lookVector))
				local frac = math.min(1, 1.5*dt/theta)
				local unit = Slerp(frac, DesiredFlightCFrame.lookVector, moveDir.lookVector)
				print(unit)
				DesiredFlightCFrame = CFrame.new(Vector3.new(0,0,0), unit)
			end
			--
			local velo = Torso.Velocity
			local veloXZ = (velo - Vector3.new(0, velo.y, 0))
			--
			local dir = DesiredFlightCFrame.lookVector
			local dirXZ = (dir - Vector3.new(0, dir.y, 0)).unit
			--
			FlyingGyro.cframe = DesiredFlightCFrame*
			                    CFrame.Angles(0, 0, Torso.RotVelocity.y/3 * (1-math.abs(dir.y)^2))*
			                    CFrame.Angles(-math.pi/2, 0, 0)
			--
			local headingForceFactor = -dir.y -- heading up => negative force
			local liftForceFactor = dir.y --hading up => positive lift
			--
			local forwardsSpeed = math.max(0, velo.magnitude)
			--
			local weight = GetMassOf(Character)*20*9.81
			--
			local dragForce = (velo.magnitude/10)^2 * weight
			local dragForceY = (velo.y/15)^2 * weight
			local dragForceXZ = (veloXZ.magnitude/35)^2 * weight
			--
			local suspendFactor = 0
			if dir.y < 0 then
				suspendFactor = (-dir.y)^2
			end
			--
			FlyingForce.force = Vector3.new(0, weight*(1.0 - suspendFactor + 0.3*liftForceFactor), 0)
			                    + (dirXZ * (weight*1.0 * math.max(0, (0.5*headingForceFactor+0.6))))
			                    - (veloXZ.unit * dragForceXZ)
			                    - (Vector3.new(0, velo.y, 0).unit * dragForceY)
			--
			--print(string.format("RotVelocity: %.1f, Velo: %.2f",
			--                    Torso.RotVelocity.y, velo.magnitude))
		end
		wait()
	end
end)

		Gui:Destroy()

	GliderBomb:Stop()

local Tool = script.Parent
local Remote = Tool:WaitForChild("Remote")
local songgui

local enabled = true

local CAS = game:GetService("ContextActionService")
local ActionName = "PenguinAttack"

local Player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
local Mouse = Player:GetMouse()

function onAction()
	Remote:FireServer("Activate", Mouse.Hit.p)
end

function onEquipped(mouse)
	--ensure unequip
	onUnequipped()
	
	--bind
	mouse.Button1Down:connect(onAction)
end

function onUnequipped()
	--unbind
	CAS:UnbindAction(ActionName)
	
	if songgui then
		songgui:Destroy()
	end
end

function playAnimation(name, ...)
	local anim = Tool:FindFirstChild(name)
	if anim then
		local human = Tool.Parent:FindFirstChild("Humanoid")
		if human then
			local track = human:LoadAnimation(anim)
			track:Play(...)
		end
	end
end

function chooseSong()
	if Player.PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("ChooseSongGui") then return end
	
	local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
	sg.Name = "ChooseSongGui"
	
	local frame = Instance.new("Frame")

			if input.Text ~= "" then

				s.Name = "BOOMBOXTESTSOUND"

				local count = 0
