local objectsready = false

function createWeld(mainpart,target)
	local weld = Instance.new("Weld") 
	weld.Part0 = mainpart
	weld.Part1 = target 
	weld.C0 = mainpart.CFrame:inverse() 
	weld.C1 = target.CFrame:inverse() 
	weld.Parent = target
	target.Anchored = false
end

function getItemIDByName(itemname)
	for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name == itemname then
			return v.ItemID.Value
		end
	end
end


function getFurnitureCount(plr)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	local house = workspace:FindFirstChild("!home_"..plr.Name)
	if data and house then
		local val = data:FindFirstChild("FurnitureCount")
		if val == nil then
			val = Instance.new("IntValue",data)
			val.Name = "FurnitureCount"
		end
		local fcount = 0
		for i,v in pairs(house:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Model") then
				fcount = fcount + 1
			end
		end
		val.Value = fcount
		return fcount
	end
end


game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ToggleHomeLock.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,home) --can be home or apartment
	if home.Name:match(plr.Name) then
		local door = home.Door
		local lock = home:FindFirstChild("LockButton",true)
		if door.CanCollide == false then
			door.CanCollide = true
			lock.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Bright red")
		else
			door.CanCollide = false
			lock.BrickColor = BrickColor.new("Bright green")
		end
	end
end)


game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ToggleRestrictLock.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,home)
	if home.Name:match(plr.Name) then
		local restrict = home.Restrictions
		if restrict.Value == true then
			restrict.Value = false
		else
			restrict.Value = true
		end
	end
end)


game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ToggleHomeRestrictedPlayer.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,home,targetname) --okay, really we're adding people to the whitelist, not restricting them
	if home.Name:match(plr.Name) then
		local restrict = home.Restrictions
		local targetplr
		for _,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
			if string.lower(targetname) == string.lower(v.Name) then
				targetplr = v
			end
		end
		if targetplr then
			if targetplr ~= plr then
				if restrict:FindFirstChild(targetplr.Name) then
					restrict[targetplr.Name]:Destroy()
					return true
				else
					local marker = Instance.new("Folder",restrict)
					marker.Name = targetplr.Name
					return true
				end
			else
				return false,"Can't whitelist yourself"
			end
		else
			return false,"Player not in server"
		end
	end
end


function getFurnitureCostMarker(stats,servobj)
	local furnbought
	local id = servobj.ItemID.Value
	if stats.FurnitureCost:FindFirstChild(id) then
		furnbought = stats.FurnitureCost:FindFirstChild(id)
	else
		furnbought = Instance.new("IntValue")
		furnbought.Value = 0
		furnbought.Name = id
		furnbought.Parent = stats.FurnitureCost
	end
	local pending
	if furnbought:FindFirstChild("PendingSales") then
		pending = furnbought.PendingSales
	else
		pending = Instance.new("IntValue")
		pending.Value = 0
		pending.Name = "PendingSales"
		pending.Parent = furnbought
	end
	return furnbought,pending
end


function getOwnedObjectCount(plr,itemid)
	local count = 0
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data then
		for _,v in pairs(data.Objects:GetChildren()) do
			if tonumber(v.Name) == itemid then
				count = count + 1
			end
		end
	end
	return count
end


coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function() --initialize all furniture models
	for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name:match("furni_") then
			coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
			local model
			repeat
				local success = pcall(function()
					model = game:GetService("InsertService"):LoadAsset(v.ModelID.Value):GetChildren()[1]
				end)
				if not success then print("Failed to load "..v.Name) wait(1) end
			until model ~= nil
			
			local pp = model.PrimaryPart
			for _,part in pairs(model:GetChildren()) do
				local weld = part:FindFirstChild("Weld")
				if weld then
					part.Anchored = true
					weld:Destroy()
				end
				if part:IsA("BasePart") then
					part.Parent = v
				end
			end
			
			v.PrimaryPart = pp
			for _,part in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
				if part:IsA("Seat") and part.Anchored == false then
					createWeld(pp,part)
				end
			end
			print("Loaded furniture "..model.Name)
			model:Destroy()
			end))
		end
	end
	local complete = true
	repeat
		wait(5)
		complete = true
		for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
			if v.Name:match("furni_") and v.PrimaryPart == nil then
				complete = false
			end
		end
	until complete == true
	objectsready = true
	print("All furniture loaded and ready")
end))

function loadHouse(plr,placement)
	if not script:FindFirstChild("load_"..plr.Name) then
		if objectsready == false then
			repeat wait(math.random()) until objectsready == true
		end
		local pid = plr.userId
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(pid)
		local passes = data:WaitForChild("GamePasses")
		local hp = passes:WaitForChild("House")
		if plr and (game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.GamePassCheck:Invoke(plr,"House") == true or game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(plr,housepassid) == true or data.Objects:FindFirstChild("183")) then
			local pd = game:GetService("DataStoreService"):GetDataStore(pid.."_data")
			
			--the table name is the	unique name, like Object_1, to load each indiv obj
			--the Name key is the name of the object in ServerStorage (to load it)		
			
			
			local housedata 
			local success = pcall(function()
				housedata = pd:GetAsync("house")
			end)
			if housedata == nil then
				housedata = {}
			end
			
			if not success then
				wait(2)
				game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"Your house failed to load for this session. ROBLOX's servers may be experiencing issues; please try again later.",true)
				return
			end

			if data:FindFirstChild("FurnitureCost") then
				data.FurnitureCost.Value = 0
				data.FurnitureCost:ClearAllChildren()
			end
			
			local house = game.ServerStorage.BaseHouse:clone()
			house.PrimaryPart = house.Floor
			
			for i,obj in pairs(housedata) do --load all furniture and house data
				
				if housedata[i]["Name"] ~= nil and storage:FindFirstChild(housedata[i]["Name"]) ~= nil then
					local model = storage:FindFirstChild(housedata[i]["Name"]):clone()
					model.Name = housedata[i]["Name"]
					model.Parent = house
					if model:IsA("Model") then
						model:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(house.Floor.CFrame + (Vector3.new(housedata[i]["CFrame"][1],housedata[i]["CFrame"][2],housedata[i]["CFrame"][3])))
						model:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(model.PrimaryPart.CFrame * CFrame.Angles(0,math.rad(housedata[i]["CFrame"][4]),0))
						model.RotValue.Value = housedata[i]["CFrame"][4]
						model.PaintColor.Value = BrickColor.new(housedata[i]["PaintColor"])
						for i,part in pairs(model:GetChildren()) do
							if part:IsA("BasePart") and (part.Name == "Paintable") then
								part.BrickColor = model.PaintColor.Value
							end
						end
					end
				end
	
			end --end the furniture loading
			
			if housedata["MainHouseData"] ~= nil then
			
				for i,v in pairs(housedata["MainHouseData"]) do
					for _,part in pairs(house:GetChildren()) do
						if part:IsA("BasePart") and part.Name == i then
							part.BrickColor = BrickColor.new(v)
						end
					end
				end
			
			end
			
			local nametag = house:FindFirstChild("NameTag",true)
			nametag.TextLabel.Text = plr.Name
			
			if workspace:FindFirstChild("!home_"..plr.Name) then
				workspace.HousePlacement[workspace["!home_"..plr.Name].HousePlacement.Value].Occupied.Value = false
				workspace["!home_"..plr.Name]:Destroy()
			end
			
			house["Lock Button"].Owner.Value = plr.Name
			house["Restrict Button"].Owner.Value = plr.Name
			
			if placement == nil then
				for i=1,20 do
					if workspace.HousePlacement[i].Occupied.Value == false then
						workspace.HousePlacement[i].Occupied.Value = true
						house.Parent = workspace
						house.Name = "!home_"..plr.Name
						house:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(workspace.HousePlacement[i].CFrame)
						local placevalue = Instance.new("IntValue")
						placevalue.Name = "HousePlacement"
						placevalue.Value = i
						placevalue.Parent = house
						break
					end
				end
			else
				workspace.HousePlacement[placement].Occupied.Value = true
				house.Parent = workspace
				house.Name = "!home_"..plr.Name
				house:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(workspace.HousePlacement[placement].CFrame)
				local placevalue = Instance.new("IntValue")
				placevalue.Name = "HousePlacement"
				placevalue.Value = placement
				placevalue.Parent = house
				if plr and plr:FindFirstChild("Backpack") and plr:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Humanoid") then
					for i,v in pairs(plr.Backpack:GetChildren()) do
						if v.Name == "Move" or v.Name == "Paint" or v.Name == "Remove" then
							v:Destroy()
						end
					end
					for i,v in pairs(plr.Character:GetChildren()) do
						if (v.Name == "Move" or v.Name == "Paint" or v.Name == "Remove") and v:IsA("Tool") then
							plr.Character.Humanoid:UnequipTools()
							wait()
							v:Destroy()
						end
					end
					--[[for i,v in pairs(plr.PlayerGui:GetChildren()) do

							v:Destroy()
						end
					end]] --can't do that
				end
			end
			
			--[[for i,v in pairs(house:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Model") then
					for i,part in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
						if part:IsA("BasePart") and not part:IsA("Seat") then
							part.Anchored = true
						end
					end
				end
			end
			for i,v in pairs(house.Floor:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Weld") then

				end
			end]]

			loadmarker.Name = "load_"..plr.Name
			loadmarker.Parent = script
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(loadmarker,12)

				workspace.HousePlacement[house.HousePlacement.Value].Occupied.Value = false

			return true
			
		end --end the entire house loading function
	else
		return false
	end
end

function RemoveFurnitureItem(plr,model) --referenced later down
	local house = workspace:FindFirstChild("!home_"..plr.Name)
	local stats = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if house and stats and model.Parent == house then
		local itemserv = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:FindFirstChild(model.Name)
		if itemserv then
			local furnbought,pending = getFurnitureCostMarker(stats,itemserv)
			furnbought.Value = furnbought.Value + 1
			model:Destroy()
			if getOwnedObjectCount(plr,itemserv.ItemID.Value) + furnbought.Value <= 0 then
				stats.FurnitureCost.Value = stats.FurnitureCost.Value - itemserv.ItemCost.Value
			end
			getFurnitureCount(plr)
			return true
		end
	end
end

					table.insert(ignoredrayparts,v)
				end
			end

					local pp = v.PrimaryPart
					local newcframe = pp.CFrame
					local ray = Ray.new(newcframe.p, ((newcframe.p + Vector3.new(0,-15,0)) - newcframe.p).unit*10)
					local findpart = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray,ignoredrayparts)
					if findpart == nil or findpart.Parent.Name ~= house.Name or not findpart.Name:match("Floor") then --put the furniture back in inventory
						RemoveFurnitureItem(plr,v)
						cleanedup = true
					end
				end
			end
			
			if cleanedup == true then
				game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"Furniture that was detected outside of your house has been placed back into your inventory.")
			end
			
			
			
			house = house:clone() --create a clone house to use for saving
			house.Name = "SAVINGHOUSE"
			house.Parent = workspace
			house:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(game.ServerStorage.BaseHouse.Floor.CFrame)
			--[[for i,v in pairs(house:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("Model") then
					for i,part in pairs(v:GetChildren()) do
						if part:IsA("BasePart") then
							part.Anchored = true
						end
					end
				end
			end
			house.Floor.Anchored = true]]
			
			
			local savefunc = pcall(function()
				local save = {}
				for _,v in pairs(house:GetChildren()) do
					if v:IsA("Model") and string.sub(v.Name,1,6) == "furni_" then
						local keyid = v.Name.."_"..math.random(-9999999,9999999)
						save[keyid] = {}
						save[keyid]["CFrame"] = {}
						local pos = v.PrimaryPart.Position - house.Floor.Position
						local rot = v.RotValue.Value
						save[keyid]["CFrame"][1] = pos.X
						save[keyid]["CFrame"][2] = pos.Y
						save[keyid]["CFrame"][3] = pos.Z
						save[keyid]["CFrame"][4] = rot
						save[keyid]["Name"] = v.Name
						save[keyid]["PaintColor"] = v.PaintColor.Value.Number

							v.temp:Destroy()
						end
					end
				end

					if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name ~= "SPECIAL" then

					end
				end

					savestr = game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(save)
				end)

					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr, "Error: Your house's save file has surpassed the maximum size (".. math.ceil((#savestr/259000)*100) .."%). You will have to remove some items first.")
					return
				end
				
				local stats = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
				if stats and stats:FindFirstChild("FurnitureCost") and stats:FindFirstChild("cash") and stats.cash.Value >= stats.FurnitureCost.Value then
				
					local pd = game:GetService("DataStoreService"):GetDataStore(plr.userId.."_data")
					pd:SetAsync("house",save)
					
					local markerstocreate = {} --names of markers (numerical IDs)
					local markerstodestroy = {} --pointers to markers
					
					--do not change ROBLOX objects within the UpdateAsync call, it could get repeated
					--FurnitureCost child's name is the item's ID
					local savesuccess = pd:UpdateAsync("maindata", function(tbl)
						--[[for _,v in pairs(stats.FurnitureCost:GetChildren()) do
							tbl[v.Name] = tbl[v.Name] + v.PendingSales.Value + v.Value
							if tbl[v.Name] < 0 then
								tbl[v.Name] = 0
							end
							stats.Objects[v.Name].Value = tbl[v.Name]
						end]]
						if tbl == nil then
							return tbl
						end
						
						markerstocreate = {}
						markerstodestroy = {}
						
						for _,v in pairs(stats.FurnitureCost:GetChildren()) do
							
							local netchange = v.PendingSales.Value + v.Value
							if netchange > 0 then --new stuff
								for _=1,netchange do
									table.insert(tbl["objects"],tonumber(v.Name))
									table.insert(markerstocreate, v.Name)
									--local marker = Instance.new("StringValue",stats.Objects)
									--marker.Name = v.Name
								end
							elseif netchange < 0 then
								for _=1,netchange*-1 do
									for i,val in pairs(tbl["objects"]) do
										if val == tonumber(v.Name) then
											table.remove(tbl["objects"],i)
											--need to make sure we get the unique objects out of the player's inv...
											--cant call findfirstchild ONLY, that makes it so only 1 gets removed
											for _,x in pairs(stats.Objects:GetChildren()) do
												if x.Name == v.Name then
													local matches
													for _,z in pairs(markerstodestroy) do
														if z == x then
															matches = true
														end
													end
													if not matches then
														--it is unique, not already being destroyed
														table.insert(markerstodestroy, x)
														break
													end
												end
											end
											break
										end
									end
								end
							end
							
						end
						
						tbl["cash"] = tbl["cash"] - stats.FurnitureCost.Value
						
						return tbl
					end)
					
					if stats and savesuccess then
						for _,v in pairs(markerstocreate) do
							local marker = Instance.new("StringValue",stats.Objects)
							marker.Name = v
						end
						for _,v in pairs(markerstodestroy) do
							v:Destroy()
						end
						
						stats.cash.Value = stats.cash.Value - stats.FurnitureCost.Value
						stats.oldcash.Value = stats.oldcash.Value - stats.FurnitureCost.Value
						
						stats.FurnitureCost.Value = 0
						for _,v in pairs(stats.FurnitureCost:GetChildren()) do
							v:Destroy()
						end
					end
					
					local savemarker = Instance.new("BoolValue")
					savemarker.Name = "save_"..plr.Name
					savemarker.Parent = script
					game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(savemarker,18)
					return true
				end
			end) --end the save function
			
			house:Destroy() --remove the cloned house
			
			--[[if plr and plr:FindFirstChild("Backpack") then
				for i,v in pairs(tools) do
					v.Enabled = true
				end
			end]]
			--house.Parent = workspace
			
			if savefunc == true then
				return true
			end
		end
	else
		return false --please wait
	end
end



game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.GetFurniture.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,objname)
	local house = workspace:FindFirstChild("!home_"..plr.Name)
	local stats = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and house and house.Floor.Editing.Value == true and stats then
		local torso = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart
		local ray = Ray.new((torso.CFrame + torso.CFrame.lookVector * 10).p, (((torso.CFrame + torso.CFrame.lookVector * 10).p + Vector3.new(0,-15,0)) - (torso.CFrame + torso.CFrame.lookVector * 10).p).unit*15)
		local ignoredparts = {}
		for i,v in pairs(house:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Model") then
				table.insert(ignoredparts,v)
			end
		end
		--check if ray is inside house
		local findpart = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray,ignoredparts)
		if findpart == nil or findpart.Parent.Name ~= house.Name or not findpart.Name:match("Floor") then
			return false,"Please stand facing some empty space inside your house, then try again."
		end
		local fcount = getFurnitureCount(plr)
		if fcount == 450 then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr, "Notice: You are approaching the item limit for your house. You have placed "..fcount.."/500 items.")
		end
		if fcount >= 500 then
			--hoarder!!
			return false,"Sorry, you cannot fit more than 1000 items in your home!"
		end
		
		
		local components = (torso.CFrame + torso.CFrame.lookVector * 10).p
		local itemserv = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:FindFirstChild(objname)
		if itemserv then
			local item = itemserv:clone()
			for _,v in pairs(item:GetChildren()) do
				if v.ClassName:match("Value") and v.Name ~= "PaintColor" and v.Name ~= "RotValue" and v.Name ~= "ItemID" then
					v:Destroy()
				end
			end
			item.Parent = house
			
			item:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(house.Floor.CFrame)
			local boxsize,boxcframe = require(game.ReplicatedStorage.Modules.GetBoundingBox)(item)
			local bottomboundary = boxcframe.Y - boxsize.Y / 2
			local yoffset = item.PrimaryPart.Position.Y - bottomboundary + house.Floor.Size.Y / 2
			local origpos = item.PrimaryPart.Position
			item:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(item.PrimaryPart.CFrame + (Vector3.new(math.floor(components.X),yoffset,math.floor(components.Z))) - Vector3.new(origpos.X,0,origpos.Z)) -- orig CFrame + (newpos - oldpos)

			local tempval = Instance.new("BoolValue")
			tempval.Name = "temp"
			tempval.Parent = item
			local furnbought,pending = getFurnitureCostMarker(stats,itemserv)
			furnbought.Value = furnbought.Value - 1
			if getOwnedObjectCount(plr,itemserv.ItemID.Value) + pending.Value + furnbought.Value < 0 then
				stats.FurnitureCost.Value = stats.FurnitureCost.Value + itemserv.ItemCost.Value
			end
			getFurnitureCount(plr)
			return true
		end
		
	end
end

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RemoveFurniture.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,model)
	RemoveFurnitureItem(plr,model)
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SellFurniture.OnServerInvoke = function(plr,objname)
	local stats = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if stats then
		local servobj = game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:FindFirstChild(objname)
		--local obj = stats.Objects:FindFirstChild(objname)
		if servobj and not servobj:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") and not servobj:FindFirstChild("NotForSale") then
			local furnbought,pending = getFurnitureCostMarker(stats,servobj)
			if getOwnedObjectCount(plr,servobj.ItemID.Value) + pending.Value + furnbought.Value > 0 then
				pending.Value = pending.Value - 1
				stats.FurnitureCost.Value = stats.FurnitureCost.Value - math.ceil(servobj.ItemCost.Value * .7)
				return true
			end
		end
	end
end





game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PaintHouseItem.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,item,color)
	if item.Parent.Name == "!home_"..plr.Name then
		if item:IsA("Model") then
			item.PaintColor.Value = color
			for i,v in pairs(item:GetChildren()) do
				if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name == "Paintable" then
					v.BrickColor = color
				end
			end
		elseif item:IsA("BasePart") then
			local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
			
			local function paintParts(part,cl)
				for i,v in pairs(part.Parent:GetChildren()) do
					if v:IsA("BasePart") and v.Name == part.Name then
						v.BrickColor = cl
					end
				end
			end
			
			local colorbanned = false
			for _,v in pairs(bannedcolors) do
				if color.Name == v then
					colorbanned = true
				end
			end
			
			local isexterior = false
			for _,v in pairs(exteriorparts) do
				if item.Name == v then
					isexterior = true
				end
			end
			
			if not isexterior then
				paintParts(item,color)
			else
				if (data and (data.Objects:FindFirstChild("208") or game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.GamePassCheck:Invoke(plr,"House"))) and colorbanned == false then
					paintParts(item,color)
				elseif colorbanned then
					paintParts(item,item.BrickColor)
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr, "You're not allowed to paint your exterior walls with neon colors!")
				else
					paintParts(item,item.BrickColor)
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.OpenGuiFromServer:FireClient(plr,"BuyPaintingLicense")
				end
			end
			
			
		end
	end
end)



game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.SendNewFurniturePosition.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,selectedmodel,newcframe,newrotval)
	if selectedmodel then
		local pp = selectedmodel.PrimaryPart
		local ray = Ray.new(newcframe.p, ((newcframe.p + Vector3.new(0,-15,0)) - newcframe.p).unit*10)
		local ignoredparts = {}
		for i,v in pairs(selectedmodel.Parent:GetChildren()) do
			if v:IsA("Model") then
				table.insert(ignoredparts,v)
			end
		end
		local findpart = workspace:FindPartOnRayWithIgnoreList(ray,ignoredparts)
		if findpart == nil or not findpart.Name:match("Floor") then --put it back in orig pos for the client
			selectedmodel:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(pp.CFrame)
			selectedmodel.RotValue.Value = selectedmodel.RotValue.Value
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"An error occurred while trying to position this piece of furniture. Please try again.")
		else --apply the new position and rotation
			selectedmodel:SetPrimaryPartCFrame(newcframe)
			selectedmodel.RotValue.Value = newrotval
		end
	end
end)

		house.Floor.Editing.Value = true
	end
end)

local groupbans = {23914734, 129280967, 86210477} --userids of people who can play RHS, but can't rank up

local datastore = game:GetService("DataStoreService"):GetDataStore("grouprankdays")
local timedata = game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData

function GetNextRankDay(plr)
	local day --the next day when the player can rank up
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data then
		if data:FindFirstChild("DayEligibleForRank") then
			day = data.DayEligibleForRank.Value
		else
			local success = pcall(function()
				day = datastore:GetAsync(plr.userId)
				if day then
					day = day + 14
				end
			end)

			if day == nil and timedata.CurrentRealLifeDay.Value < 184 then --june 9
				local num = plr.userId
				day = 154 + num % 30
				if plr.AccountAge < 14 and (day - timedata.CurrentRealLifeDay.Value) < (14 - plr.AccountAge) then
					day = timedata.CurrentRealLifeDay.Value + (14 - plr.AccountAge)
				end
			end
			if day == nil and success == true then --never ranked up before
				day = timedata.CurrentRealLifeDay.Value - plr.AccountAge + 14 --14 day acc.
			elseif day == nil and success == false then --request failed
				day = 420420
			end
			local val = Instance.new("IntValue")
			val.Name = "DayEligibleForRank"
			val.Value = day
			val.Parent = data
		end
		return day
	end
end


game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.GetGroupRankData.OnServerInvoke = function(plr) --necessary values: current rank, next rank, playtime, required playtime, next day to rank up, was request already submitted, 
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data then
		local returndata = {}
		local ranknumber = plr:GetRankInGroup(1127093)
		local rankname
		local nextrank
		if ranknumber == 0 then return end
		if ranknumber == 1 then
			rankname = "New Fan"
		end
		if ranknumber == 120 then
			returndata.CurrentRank = "Super Fan [SS+]"
			return returndata
		end
		if ranknumber == 5 then return end
		
		local highestrankdata
		for i,v in pairs(rankdata) do
			if v.RankId > ranknumber and v.Playtime <= data.totaltime.Value and (highestrankdata == nil or highestrankdata.RankId < v.RankId) then
				highestrankdata = v
			end
		end
		
		for i,v in pairs(rankdata) do
			if v.RankId == ranknumber then
				rankname = v.RankName
			end
			if v.RankId > ranknumber and v.RankId <= ranknumber + 10 then
				nextrank = v.RankName
				returndata.RequiredPlaytime = v.Playtime
			end
		end
		if nextrank == nil then return end
		if highestrankdata and highestrankdata.RankId >= ranknumber + 19 then
			nextrank = highestrankdata.RankName
			returndata.RequiredPlaytime = highestrankdata.Playtime
		end
		local nextrankday = GetNextRankDay(plr)
		--if nextrankday == 420420 then return end
		returndata.CurrentRank = rankname
		returndata.NextRank = nextrank
		returndata.Playtime = data.totaltime.Value
		returndata.NextRankUpDay = nextrankday
		returndata.RequestSubmitted = plr:FindFirstChild("RequestSubmitted") and true or false
		
		return returndata
	end
end


game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.RequestRankUp.OnServerInvoke = function(plr)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if plr.AccountAge >= 14 and plr:IsInGroup(1127093) and data and data:FindFirstChild("totaltime") then
		local playtime = data.totaltime.Value
		if playtime < rankdata[1]["Playtime"] then return end
		if plr:FindFirstChild("RequestSubmitted") then return end
		if GetNextRankDay(plr) > timedata.CurrentRealLifeDay.Value then return end
		local currentrank = plr:GetRankInGroup(1127093)
		if currentrank == 5 or game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.IsPlayerBanned:Invoke(plr) == true then
			return
		end
		for _,v in pairs(groupbans) do
			if v == plr.userId then
				return
			end
		end
		local highestrankdata
		
		for i,v in pairs(rankdata) do
			if v.RankId > currentrank and v.Playtime <= playtime and (highestrankdata == nil or highestrankdata.RankId < v.RankId) then
				highestrankdata = v
			end
		end
		
		if highestrankdata then
			local val = Instance.new("BoolValue")
			val.Name = "RequestSubmitted"
			val.Value = true
			val.Parent = plr
			local httpsuccess = pcall(function() 
				local url = "http://cindering.xyz/index.php?key=SecretWebKey&userID="..plr.userId.."&rank="..highestrankdata.RankWebId
				Http:GetAsync(url)
			end)
			if httpsuccess then
				local datasuccess = pcall(function()
					datastore:SetAsync(plr.userId, timedata.CurrentRealLifeDay.Value)
				end)
				return true
			end
		end
	end
end

function CheckFreeGamePasses(plr)
	if _G["freepasses"] and _G["admins"] then
		for _,v in pairs(_G["freepasses"]) do
			if v == plr.Name then return true end
		end
		for _,v in pairs(_G["admins"]) do
			if v == plr.Name then return true end
		end
	end
end

function AwardFanclubItems(plr)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data then
		local idstoaward = {}
		for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
			if data.Objects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) == nil and v:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") and not (v:FindFirstChild("ReleaseTime") and v.ReleaseTime.Value > game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData.CurrentTime.Value) then
				local loopcount = 1
				if v:FindFirstChild("LoopCount") then
					loopcount = v.LoopCount.Value
				end
				for i=1,loopcount do
					table.insert(idstoaward,v.ItemID.Value)
				end
			end
		end
		if #idstoaward > 0 then
			game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AwardTableOfItems:Fire(plr,idstoaward)
		end
	end
end



function GetDJCompensation(plr, datafolder, datatable)
	local compfolder = datafolder:FindFirstChild("Compensation")
	local success
	if not compfolder:FindFirstChild("djcompensation") then
		if datatable then
			datatable["Compensation"]["djcompensation"] = true
		else
			local marker = Instance.new("Folder")
			marker.Name = "djcompensation"
			marker.Parent = compfolder
			success = pcall(function()
				game:GetService("DataStoreService"):GetDataStore(plr.userId.."_data"):UpdateAsync("maindata", function(tbl)
					if tbl ~= nil then
						tbl["Compensation"]["djcompensation"] = true
					end
					return tbl
				end)
			end)
		end
		if datatable or success then
			return 3000
		end
	end
end

function GetCDCompensation(plr, datafolder, datatable)
	local compfolder = datafolder:FindFirstChild("Compensation")
	local playerobjects = datafolder:FindFirstChild("Objects")
	
	if not compfolder:FindFirstChild("CDcompensation") and not compfolder:FindFirstChild("djcompensation") and playerobjects then
		local refund = 0
		for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
			if string.sub(v.Name,1,6) == "music_" then
				if playerobjects:FindFirstChild(v.ItemID.Value) then
					refund = refund + v.ItemCost.Value
				end
			end
		end
		if refund > 0 then
			datatable["Compensation"]["CDcompensation"] = true
		end
		return refund
	end
end

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.GetDJCompensation.OnInvoke = function(plr, datafolder)
	return GetDJCompensation(plr, datafolder)
end

function RepeatGet(pd, key, successes, tries)
	local success, reason, returnvalue
	local i = 0
	repeat
		if i ~= 0 then
			wait(3)
		end
		success, reason = pcall(function()
			returnvalue = pd:GetAsync(key)
		end)
		i = i + 1
	until success or i >= (tries or 10)
	
	if successes then
		table.insert(successes,success)
		if debugmode or not success then print(reason) end
	end
	
	return returnvalue, success, reason
end

function RepeatSet(pd, key, value, successes, tries)
	local success, reason, returnvalue
	local i = 0
	repeat
		if i ~= 0 then
			wait(3)
		end
		success, reason = pcall(function()
			returnvalue = pd:SetAsync(key, value)
		end)
		i = i + 1
	until success or i >= (tries or 10)
	
	if successes then
		table.insert(successes,success)
		if debugmode or not success then print(reason) end
	end
	
	return returnvalue, success, reason
end

function RepeatUpdate(pd, key, funct, successes, tries)
	local success, reason, returnvalue
	local i = 0
	repeat
		if i ~= 0 then
			wait(3)
		end
		success, reason = pcall(function()
			returnvalue = pd:UpdateAsync(key, funct)
		end)
		i = i + 1
	until success or i >= (tries or 10)
	
	if successes then
		table.insert(successes,success)
		if debugmode or not success then print(reason) end
	end
	
	return returnvalue, success, reason
end

	--loyalty changes
	
	local function GetLoyaltyData(tbl)
		local changes = false
		local timedata = game.ReplicatedStorage.IntroGUIData
		local day = timedata.CurrentRealLifeDay.Value
		local endtime = timedata.HolidayStartTime.Value + (day * 86400)
		local timeleft = endtime - timedata.CurrentTime.Value
		local timeleftstring = math.floor(timeleft/3600).."h "..math.floor((timeleft%3600)/60).."m"
		
		local current_lp = tbl[1]
		local lp_reward = 0
		local consec_days = tbl[2]
		local current_spins = tbl[4]
		local firstlogintoday = false
		local lastdayloggedin = tbl[3]
		
		if tbl[3] ~= day then --hasn't logged in today
			firstlogintoday = true
			changes = true
			if day - tbl[3] <= 2 then --logged in yesterday or 2 days before, offer rewards for consec. logins
				tbl[3] = day
				tbl[2] = tbl[2] + 1
				consec_days = tbl[2]
				if tbl[2] >= 5 then
					lp_reward = 1000
				else
					lp_reward = 500 + tbl[2] * 100
				end
				tbl[1] = tbl[1] + lp_reward
				current_lp = tbl[1]
				if consec_days % 5 == 0 then
					tbl[4] = tbl[4] + 2
				end
				current_spins = tbl[4]
			else --didn't log in yesterday, reset counter
				tbl[2] = 1
				consec_days = 1
				lp_reward = 600
				tbl[1] = tbl[1] + lp_reward
				current_lp = tbl[1]
				tbl[3] = day
				current_spins = tbl[4]
			end
		end
		
		--create stats
		local loyaltystats = Instance.new("Folder")
		loyaltystats.Name = "LoyaltyStats"
		
		local lp = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		lp.Name = "LP"
		lp.Value = current_lp
		local oldlp = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		oldlp.Name = "OldLP"
		oldlp.Value = current_lp
		
		local spins = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		spins.Name = "Spins"
		spins.Value = current_spins
		local oldspins = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		oldspins.Name = "OldSpins"
		oldspins.Value = current_spins
		
		local lastday = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		lastday.Name = "LastDayLoggedIn"
		lastday.Value = lastdayloggedin
		
		--store data that is only used to determine what's displayed on GUI
		local rewardstat = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		rewardstat.Name = "LPReward"
		rewardstat.Value = lp_reward
		local daysstat = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		daysstat.Name = "ConsecDays"
		daysstat.Value = consec_days
		local dayendtime = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		dayendtime.Name = "EndTime"
		dayendtime.Value = endtime
		local firstloginval = Instance.new("BoolValue",loyaltystats)
		firstloginval.Name = "FirstLogin"
		firstloginval.Value = firstlogintoday
		local playerloginday = Instance.new("IntValue",loyaltystats)
		playerloginday.Name = "PlayerLoginDay"
		playerloginday.Value = day
		
		loyaltystats.Parent = stats	
		
		return tbl, changes
	end
	
	local loyaltytable, changes = GetLoyaltyData(maindata["loyaltytable"])
	maindata["loyaltytable"] = loyaltytable
	if changes then
		changesmade = true
		changestable["loyaltytable"] = true
	end
	
	
	for _,v in pairs(successes) do
		if v == false then
			safetoload = false
		end
	end
	
	
	--start creating the stats objects
	local options = maindata["options"]
	
	local color = Instance.new("Color3Value")
	color.Value = Color3.new(options["ChatColor"][1],options["ChatColor"][2],options["ChatColor"][3])
	color.Name = "chatcolor"
	color.Parent = player
	
	local volumesetting = Instance.new("IntValue")
	volumesetting.Name = "VolumeSetting"
	volumesetting.Value = options["Volume"][1]
	volumesetting.Parent = player
	
	local clubvolumesetting = Instance.new("IntValue")
	clubvolumesetting.Name = "ClubVolumeSetting"
	clubvolumesetting.Value = options["Volume"][2]
	clubvolumesetting.Parent = player
	
	local boomboxsetting = Instance.new("BoolValue")
	boomboxsetting.Name = "BoomboxSetting"
	boomboxsetting.Value = options["Volume"][3]
	boomboxsetting.Parent = player
	
	
	local detailsetting = Instance.new("IntValue")
	detailsetting.Name = "DetailSetting"
	detailsetting.Value = options["LevelOfDetail"][1]
	detailsetting.Parent = player
	
	local housessetting = Instance.new("IntValue")
	housessetting.Name = "HousesSetting"
	housessetting.Value = options["LevelOfDetail"][2]
	housessetting.Parent = player
	
	--add FirstLoginTime to the data object
	
	local flt = Instance.new("IntValue")
	flt.Name = "FirstLoginTime"
	flt.Value = maindata["FirstLoginTime"]
	flt.Parent = stats
	
	
	--handle the DJ recently played and favorites lists
	--two folders for each list: one that holds the actual current list of songs (stringvalues where Name = songID and Value is set by the client to the song's name), and another that stores boolvalues indicating whether to add/remove a song from the list when saving
	
	local songlists = Instance.new("Folder",stats)
	songlists.Name = "SongLists"
	
	local recent = Instance.new("Folder",songlists)
	recent.Name = "RecentlyPlayed"
	local recentchanges = Instance.new("Folder",songlists)
	recentchanges.Name = "RecentChanges"
	local favorites = Instance.new("Folder",songlists)
	favorites.Name = "Favorites"
	local favoritechanges = Instance.new("Folder",songlists)
	favoritechanges.Name = "FavoriteChanges"
	
	for _,v in pairs(maindata["DJRecent"]) do
		local marker = Instance.new("StringValue",recent)
		marker.Name = v 
	end
	for _,v in pairs(maindata["DJFavorites"]) do
		local marker = Instance.new("StringValue",favorites)
		marker.Name = v 
	end
	
	
	local playerobjects

	playerobjects = Instance.new("Folder")
	playerobjects.Name = "Objects"
	playerobjects.Parent = stats
	
	local pendingobjects --objects that will be saved at the autosave interval
	pendingobjects = Instance.new("Folder")
	pendingobjects.Name = "PendingObjects"
	pendingobjects.Parent = stats
	
	local itemdataloader
	pcall(function()
		itemdataloader = require(258548692)
	end)
	if itemdataloader then
		itemdataloader.loadExtraItemData(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects)
	end
	
	
	local oldcarobjects = {} --check these for the old car refund
	
	for i,v in pairs(maindata["objects"]) do --check table of player's objects
		for _,obj in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do --check server objects
			if obj.ItemID.Value == v then --if object's ID == player's item ID
				local marker = Instance.new("StringValue")
				marker.Name = v --marker's name is the ID
				marker.Parent = playerobjects
				if obj:FindFirstChild("OldSchoolCar") then
					table.insert(oldcarobjects,obj)
				end
			end
		end
	end
	
	for _,obj in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.Objects:GetChildren()) do
		if (obj.ItemCost.Value == 0 and not obj:FindFirstChild("TwitterCode") and not obj:FindFirstChild("NotForSale")) and not playerobjects:FindFirstChild(obj.ItemID.Value) then --give 'em all the free objects
			local marker = Instance.new("StringValue")
			marker.Name = obj.ItemID.Value
			marker.Parent = playerobjects
		end
	end
	
	
	local passes = Instance.new("Folder")
	passes.Name = "GamePasses"
	passes.Parent = stats
	for i,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.GamePasses:GetChildren()) do
		local marker = Instance.new("BoolValue")
		marker.Name = v.Name
		marker.Value = false
		if game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(player,v.Value) or CheckFreeGamePasses(player) == true then
			marker.Value = true
		elseif v.Name == "House" and playerobjects:FindFirstChild("183") then --Custom House product's ItemID
			marker.Value = true
		end
		marker.Parent = passes
	end
	
	
	--track number of purchases for analytics
	local numpurchases = Instance.new("IntValue")
	numpurchases.Name = "NumPurchases"
	local purchcount = 0
	for _,v in pairs(passes:GetChildren()) do
		if v.Name ~= "House" and v.Value == true then
			purchcount = purchcount + 1
		end
	end
	purchcount = purchcount + #maindata["PurchaseHistory"]
	numpurchases.Value = purchcount
	numpurchases.Parent = stats
	
	
	local outfits = Instance.new("Folder",stats)
	outfits.Name = "Outfits"
	local alloutfitdata = maindata["Outfits"]
	
	for i=1,24 do --create all of the outfits in the data
		local outfit = Instance.new("Folder",outfits)
		outfit.Name = i
		
		local outfitdata = alloutfitdata[i]
		
		for x=1,3 do
			local hat = Instance.new("IntValue",outfit)
			hat.Name = "Hat"..x
			hat.Value = outfitdata["Hat"..x]
		end
		
		local outfitname = Instance.new("StringValue",outfit)
		outfitname.Name = "OutfitName"
		outfitname.Value = outfitdata["OutfitName"]
		local shirt = Instance.new("IntValue",outfit)
		shirt.Name = "Shirt"
		shirt.Value = outfitdata["Shirt"]
		local pants = Instance.new("IntValue",outfit)
		pants.Name = "Pants"
		pants.Value = outfitdata["Pants"]
		local face = Instance.new("IntValue",outfit)
		face.Name = "Face"
		face.Value = outfitdata["Face"]
		local package = Instance.new("IntValue",outfit)
		package.Name = "Package"
		package.Value = outfitdata["Package"]
		local removeshirt = Instance.new("BoolValue",outfit)
		removeshirt.Name = "RemoveShirt"
		removeshirt.Value = outfitdata["RemoveShirt"]
		local rpname = Instance.new("StringValue",outfit)
		rpname.Name = "RPName"
		rpname.Value = outfitdata["RPName"]
		local rpdesc = Instance.new("StringValue",outfit)
		rpdesc.Name = "RPDesc"
		rpdesc.Value = outfitdata["RPDesc"]
	end
	
	
	
	
	--for special flags (whenever we get to that): Special flags structure will be: Folders are tables. For value objects, the name is the i and the value is the v
	
	
	
	--do compensation checks, perform compensation if conditions are met and the flags are missing
	
	--for DJ, need two different compensations! The same old one for having both CR+Unrestricted (but only the 1,000, not the CD bonus) and a separate one just for having the CR DJ pass (CD refund only) (this must ONLY be given if the player doesn't already have the Unrestricted refund)
	
	-- place refund markers inside of a refunds folder, and on the local side, player will wait to display each refund window
	
	local compfolder = Instance.new("Folder",stats)
	compfolder.Name = "Compensation"
	
	-- CDs only comp for DJ pass
	if game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(player,game.ReplicatedStorage.GamePasses.ClubRed.Value) and maindata["Compensation"]["CDcompensation"] == nil and maindata["Compensation"]["djcompensation"] == nil and safetoload then
		local incvalue = GetCDCompensation(player, stats, maindata)
		if incvalue and incvalue > 0 then
			maindata["cash"] = maindata["cash"] + incvalue
			changesmade = true
			changestable["Compensation"] = true
			changestable["cash"] = true
			if player then
				game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.DisplayRefundWindow:FireClient(player, "CD", incvalue)
			end
		end
	end
	
	-- +3,000 comp for both passes
	if game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(player,game.ReplicatedStorage.GamePasses.UnrestrictedDJ.Value) and game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(player,game.ReplicatedStorage.GamePasses.ClubRed.Value) and maindata["Compensation"]["djcompensation"] == nil and safetoload then
		local incvalue = GetDJCompensation(player, stats, maindata)
		if incvalue then
			maindata["cash"] = maindata["cash"] + incvalue
			changesmade = true
			changestable["Compensation"] = true
			changestable["cash"] = true
			if player then
				game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player, "You have received +$"..incvalue.." RHS Cash with your purchase of Unrestricted DJ!",true)
			end
		end
	end
	
	
	
	--old cars comp
	if #oldcarobjects > 0 and safetoload then
		if maindata["Compensation"]["oldcarcomp"] == nil then
			changesmade = true
			changestable["Compensation"] = true
			changestable["cash"] = true
			
			local totalcost = 0
			for _,obj in pairs(oldcarobjects) do
				totalcost = totalcost + obj.ItemCost.Value
			end
			maindata["cash"] = maindata["cash"] + totalcost
			maindata["Compensation"]["oldcarcomp"] = true
			if player then
				game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.DisplayRefundWindow:FireClient(player, "OldCar", totalcost)
			end
		end
	end
	
	for i,_ in pairs(maindata["Compensation"]) do
		local marker = Instance.new("Folder",compfolder)
		marker.Name = i 
	end
	
	
	--finish creating stats objects that are dependent on compensation results
	
	
	local timestat
	timestat = Instance.new("IntValue")
	timestat.Name = "totaltime"
	timestat.Value = totaltime
	timestat.Parent = stats
	
	local cashstat
	cashstat = Instance.new("IntValue")
	cashstat.Name = "cash"
	cashstat.Value = maindata["cash"]
	cashstat.Parent = stats
	
	local oldcashstat
	oldcashstat = Instance.new("IntValue")
	oldcashstat.Name = "oldcash"
	oldcashstat.Value = maindata["cash"]
	oldcashstat.Parent = stats
		
	local furniturestat --holds all furniture counters, removed on quit
	furniturestat = Instance.new("IntValue")
	furniturestat.Name = "FurnitureCost"
	furniturestat.Value = 0
	furniturestat.Parent = stats
	
	local fanclub = Instance.new("BoolValue",stats)
	fanclub.Name = "IsInFanClub"
	fanclub.Value = false
	
	local badgesearned = Instance.new("Folder",stats)
	badgesearned.Name = "BadgesEarned"
	
	
	--update the data if: there are no failures, if changes are made, and if not a guest
	local success
	
	--compare origdata and maindata for changes with cash
	if safetoload and changesmade and not isguest then
		maindata, success = RepeatUpdate(pd, "maindata", function(tbl)
			for i,_ in pairs(changestable) do
				if i == "cash" then
					local toadd = maindata["cash"] - origdata["cash"]
					tbl["cash"] = tbl["cash"] + toadd
				elseif i == "objects" then
					--this is only required if there's been a transfer from the old objects format - NEVER overwrite like this just for adding/removing a few objects - if we were doing that, should give a table with a list of objects to add/remove
					tbl["objects"] = maindata["objects"]
				else
					tbl[i] = maindata[i]
				end
			end
			return tbl
		end, successes, 15)
	elseif safetoload and (not changesmade or isguest) then
		success = true
	end
	
	if not success then
		safetoload = false
	end
	
	
	local marker = Instance.new("BoolValue")
	marker.Name = "LoadedSuccessfully"
	
	if safetoload == false then
		marker.Value = false
		if player then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"Error: Your data could not be loaded at this time. ROBLOX's servers may be experiencing connection issues. Try rejoining at a later time.",true)
		end
	else
		marker.Value = true
	end
	
	marker.Parent = stats
	
	
	if player ~= nil and player.Parent ~= nil then
		stats.Parent = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData
		if game.PlaceId ~= 9689581 or player:IsInGroup(1127093) then
			fanclub.Value = true
			AwardFanclubItems(player)
		end
	end
	
	BadgeService:AwardBadge(pid,badgeids[1])
	
	--john doe badge Kappa
	if (os.time() > 1489820400 and os.time() < 1489906800) then
		BadgeService:AwardBadge(pid,693040536)
	end
	
	
	--LOAD RHS HEADPHONES OWNERSHIP--
	local hastoy = Instance.new("BoolValue")
	hastoy.Name = "HasRHSToy"
	hastoy.Value = false
	pcall(function()
		if game:GetService("MarketplaceService"):PlayerOwnsAsset(player, 501960266) then
			hastoy.Value = true
		end
	end)
	hastoy.Parent = player
	
	
	--IS ROBLOX ADMIN--
	if player:IsInGroup(1200769) then
		local isrobloxadmin = Instance.new("BoolValue")
		isrobloxadmin.Name = "IsRobloxAdmin"
		isrobloxadmin.Value = true
		isrobloxadmin.Parent = player
	end
	
	
	--IS RHS DEV--
	if player:IsInGroup(1127093) and player:GetRankInGroup(1127093) >= 253 then
		local isrhsdev = Instance.new("BoolValue")
		isrhsdev.Name = "IsRHSDev"
		isrhsdev.Value = true
		isrhsdev.Parent = player
	end
	
	
	
	
	
	--BEGIN STATS UPDATING CODE--
	
	
	--COROUTINE TO UPDATE TOTAL TIME + TIMED CASH BONUS--
	
	local timecoroutine
	timecoroutine = coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function() 
		local ticktock = 0
		while true do
			wait(.99)
			if player.Parent ~= nil then
				if not (player:FindFirstChild("IdleChecker") == nil or player.IdleChecker.Value == true) then
					ticktock = ticktock + 1
					if ticktock == 60 then
						game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.FlushData:Fire(player)
					end
					if ticktock == 60 then
						ticktock = 0
						timeleader.Value = timeleader.Value + 1
						local tottime
						pcall(function()
							tottime = pt:IncrementAsync(pid.."_playtime", 1)
							timestat.Value = tottime
						end)
						
						if tottime then
							if tottime >= 300 and not stats.BadgesEarned:FindFirstChild(tostring(badgeids[2])) then
								BadgeService:AwardBadge(pid,badgeids[2])
								local marker = Instance.new("Folder",stats.BadgesEarned)
								marker.Name = badgeids[2]
							end
							
							if tottime >= 1440 and not stats.BadgesEarned:FindFirstChild(tostring(badgeids[3])) then
								BadgeService:AwardBadge(pid,badgeids[3])
								local marker = Instance.new("Folder",stats.BadgesEarned)
								marker.Name = badgeids[3]
							end
							
							if tottime >= 4320 and not stats.BadgesEarned:FindFirstChild(tostring(badgeids[4])) then
								BadgeService:AwardBadge(pid,badgeids[4])
								local marker = Instance.new("Folder",stats.BadgesEarned)
								marker.Name = badgeids[3]
							end
						end
						
						local cashbonus = 2
						if game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(player,cashpass) or (os.time() > starttime and os.time() < endtime) then
							cashbonus = cashbonus + 2
						end
						if game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.GamePassCheck:Invoke(player,"VIP") then
							cashbonus = cashbonus + 3
						end
						if game:GetService("GamePassService"):PlayerHasPass(player,191991934) and cashstat.Value < 2000000000 then
							cashbonus = 5000
						end
						
						cashstat.Value = cashstat.Value + cashbonus
						
						game.ServerStorage.AnalyticsCache.CashEarned.Playtime.Value = game.ServerStorage.AnalyticsCache.CashEarned.Playtime.Value + cashbonus
						
					end
				end
			else
				timecoroutine = nil
				coroutine.yield()
			end
		end
	end))
	
	
	--CHARACTERADDED THINGS--
	
	player.CharacterAdded:connect(function(char)
		local ca = Instance.new("StringValue",char)
		ca.Name = "ContextActionsModelName"
		ca.Value = "Player"
	end)
	
	if player.Character and not player.Character:FindFirstChild("ContextActionsModelName") then
		local ca = Instance.new("StringValue",player.Character)
		ca.Name = "ContextActionsModelName"
		ca.Value = "Player"
	end
	
	
	
end)

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.IsDataLoadedSuccessfully.OnInvoke = function(plr)
	local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
	if data then
		if data.LoadedSuccessfully.Value == true then
			return true
		else
			return false
		end
	end
end


game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ChangeVolume.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,vol,clubvol,boombox)
	plr.VolumeSetting.Value = vol
	plr.ClubVolumeSetting.Value = clubvol
	plr.BoomboxSetting.Value = boombox
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.ChangeDetailSetting.OnServerEvent:connect(function(plr,lod,houses)
	plr.DetailSetting.Value = lod
	plr.HousesSetting.Value = houses
end)


game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.FlushData.Event:connect(function(plr)
	
	if plr and loadsuccess:Invoke(plr) == true then
		--print("Flushing data for "..plr.Name)
		local isingame = true
		if plr.Parent == nil then
			isingame = false
		end
		local pid = plr.userId
		local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(pid)
		
		if isingame == false and script.Flushing:FindFirstChild(pid) then
			repeat wait() until script.Flushing:FindFirstChild(pid) == nil
		end
		
		if data and not script.Flushing:FindFirstChild(pid) then
			local marker = Instance.new("Folder",script.Flushing)
			marker.Name = pid
			
			
			--save pending objects first
			--format of a pending object:
			--Name/Value: ItemID. 
			----CurrencyType: "LP" or "cash".
			----ItemCost
			local pendingobjects = data.PendingObjects:GetChildren()
			local objects = data.Objects
			
			--[[
			local coredatatable = {
				["cash"] = 50,
				["options"] = {
					["ChatColor"] = {999,0,0},
					["Volume"] = {7,7,true}, --normal, club, boombox
					["LevelOfDetail"] = {1,1} --LoD, houses enabled (1 = never, 2 = nearby only, 3 = always)
				},
				["objects"] = {},
				["loyaltytable"] = {0,0,0,0}, --LP, consec. days logged in, # of last day logged in, # spins available
				["Outfits"] = {}, --initialize later
				["Compensation"] = {}, --put each comp in here as ["CompName"] = true
				["DJRecent"] = {},
				["DJFavorites"] = {},
				["FirstLoginTime"] = 0,
				["SpecialFlags"] = {"FirstLoginWithNewData"}
			}
			]]
			
			local cash = data.cash.Value
			local oldcash = data.oldcash.Value
			data.oldcash.Value = cash
			local diff = cash - oldcash
			
			local vol = plr.VolumeSetting.Value
			local clubvol = plr.ClubVolumeSetting.Value
			local boombox = plr.BoomboxSetting.Value
			local color = plr.chatcolor
			local lod = plr.DetailSetting.Value
			local hsetting = plr.HousesSetting.Value
			
			local recentchanges = data.SongLists.RecentChanges
			local favoritechanges = data.SongLists.FavoriteChanges
			
			local ls = plr:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
			local tp
			if ls then
				tp = ls:FindFirstChild("Time Played")
			end
			
			local loyaltystats = data:FindFirstChild("LoyaltyStats")
			local lp
			local oldlp
			local spins
			local oldspins
			local lpdiff
			local spinsdiff
			
			if loyaltystats then
				lp = loyaltystats.LP.Value
				oldlp = loyaltystats.OldLP.Value
				spins = loyaltystats.Spins.Value
				oldspins = loyaltystats.OldSpins.Value
				
				lpdiff = lp - oldlp
				spinsdiff = spins - oldspins
				
				loyaltystats.OldLP.Value = lp
				loyaltystats.OldSpins.Value = spins
			end
			
			--update it all in 1 go!
			local success
			local tries = 0
			repeat
				success = pcall(function()
					local update = game:GetService("DataStoreService"):GetDataStore(plr.userId.."_data"):UpdateAsync("maindata",function(tbl)
						if tbl == nil then
							tbl = GetCoreDataTable()
						end
						
						--objects
						for _,v in pairs(pendingobjects) do
							table.insert(tbl["objects"],v.Value)
						end
						
						--cash 
						tbl["cash"] = tbl["cash"] + diff
						
						--options
						if ls and tp and tp.Value < 1 then
							if lod > 1 then
								lod = lod - 1
							else
								hsetting = 1
							end
						end
						tbl["options"] = {
							["ChatColor"] = {color.Value.r,color.Value.g,color.Value.b},
							["Volume"] = {vol,clubvol,boombox}, --normal, club, boombox
							["LevelOfDetail"] = {lod,hsetting} --LoD, houses enabled
						}
						
						--loyaltytable
						if loyaltystats then
							tbl["loyaltytable"][1] = tbl["loyaltytable"][1] + lpdiff
							tbl["loyaltytable"][4] = tbl["loyaltytable"][4] + spinsdiff
						end
						
						--outfits
						local outfits = {}
						for i=1,24 do
							local outfit = data.Outfits:FindFirstChild(i)
							local entry = {
								
								["OutfitName"] = "", --25 char limit
					
								["Hat1"] = 0,
								["Hat2"] = 0,
								["Hat3"] = 0,
								["Shirt"] = 0,
								["Pants"] = 0,
								
								["Face"] = 0,
								["Package"] = 0,
								
								["RPName"] = "",
								["RPDesc"] = "",
								
								["RemoveShirt"] = false
								
							}
							for _,v in pairs(outfit:GetChildren()) do
								entry[v.Name] = v.Value
							end
							table.insert(outfits,entry)
						end
						tbl["Outfits"] = outfits
						
						--compensation
						for _,v in pairs(data.Compensation:GetChildren()) do
							if tbl["Compensation"][v.Name] == nil then
								tbl["Compensation"][v.Name] = true
							end
						end
						
						--DJRecent and DJFavorites changes (true = add, false = remove)
						for _,v in pairs(recentchanges:GetChildren()) do
							for i,x in pairs(tbl["DJRecent"]) do
								if x == v.Name then
									table.remove(tbl["DJRecent"], i)
									break
								end
							end
							if v.Value == true then
								table.insert(tbl["DJRecent"], 1, v.Name)
							end
						end
						for _,v in pairs(favoritechanges:GetChildren()) do
							if v.Value == true then
								table.insert(tbl["DJFavorites"], v.Name)
							else
								for i,x in pairs(tbl["DJFavorites"]) do
									if x == v.Name then
										table.remove(tbl["DJFavorites"], i)
										break
									end
								end
							end
						end
						
						
						return tbl
					end)
				end)
				tries = tries + 1
				if not success then wait(2) end
			until success or tries >= 10
			
			
			
			if success then
				for _,v in pairs(pendingobjects) do
					v:Destroy()
				end
				for _,v in pairs(recentchanges:GetChildren()) do
					v:Destroy()
				end
				for _,v in pairs(favoritechanges:GetChildren()) do
					v:Destroy()
				end
			else
				data.oldcash.Value = data.oldcash.Value - diff
				print("Cash change unsuccessful for user "..pid)
				
				if loyaltystats then
					loyaltystats.OldLP.Value = loyaltystats.OldLP.Value - lpdiff
					loyaltystats.OldSpins.Value = loyaltystats.OldSpins.Value - spinsdiff
					print("Loyalty table change unsuccessful for user "..pid)
				end
					
				local arepaidobjects = false
				for _,v in pairs(pendingobjects) do
					if v.ItemCost.Value ~= 0 then
						arepaidobjects = true
						local itemmarker = objects:FindFirstChild(v.Name)
						if itemmarker then
							itemmarker:Destroy()
						end
						local currency = v.CurrencyType.Value == "LP" and data.LoyaltyStats.LP or data.cash
						currency.Value = currency.Value + v.ItemCost.Value
					end
				end
				for _,v in pairs(pendingobjects) do
					if v.ItemCost.Value ~= 0 then
						v:Destroy()
					end
				end
				if arepaidobjects then
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(plr,"An error occurred with your recent purchase(s). You have been refunded; please try again later.")
				end
			end
			
			
			
			--[[if data:FindFirstChild("ClassesAttended") then
				local classes = data.ClassesAttended.Value
				local oldclasses = data.OldClassesAttended.Value
				local hasbadge = data.HasBadge.Value
				data.OldClassesAttended.Value = classes
				local classdiff = classes - oldclasses
				if classdiff ~= 0 and not hasbadge then
					local success = pcall(function()
						pd:IncrementAsync("ImaginationClasses",classdiff)
					end)
					if success then
						
					else
						data.OldClassesAttended.Value = oldclasses
						print("Classes attended change unsuccessful for user "..pid)
					end
				end
			end]]
			
			if isingame == false then
				print("Removing user "..pid.."'s data")
				data:Destroy()
			end
			
			if marker then
				marker:Destroy()
			end
		end
	else
		if plr and loadsuccess:Invoke(plr) == false and plr.Parent ~= game.Players then
			local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:FindFirstChild(plr.userId)
			if data then
				data:Destroy()
			end
		end
		--[[if plr and plr:FindFirstChild("PlayerGui") then
			local h = Instance.new("Hint",plr.PlayerGui)
			h.Text = "ROBLOX's data servers are experiencing connection issues. Please be patient for service to be restored!"
			game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(h,30)
		end]]
	end
	
end)

marketplace.ProcessReceipt = function(receiptInfo)
	print("Attempting to process receipt for "..receiptInfo.PlayerId)
	local playerProductKey = receiptInfo.CurrencySpent .. ":" .. receiptInfo.PurchaseId
	
	for i,player in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
		if player.userId == receiptInfo.PlayerId then
			local data = game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:WaitForChild(tostring(player.userId), 10)
			if data then
				
				if data.LoadedSuccessfully.Value == false then
					game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"Cannot process your purchase yet, as ROBLOX's DataStore servers are down. Rejoin the game at a later time to receive your purchased product. Otherwise, you will automatically be refunded your ROBUX after 72 hours.", true)
					return
				end
				
				print("Granting purchase to "..receiptInfo.PlayerId)
				local pd = game:GetService("DataStoreService"):GetDataStore(player.userId.."_data")
				local cashval = 0
				
				for _,v in pairs(game.ReplicatedStorage.CashProducts:GetChildren()) do
					if v.Value == receiptInfo.ProductId then
						cashval = tonumber(v.Name)
					end
				end
				
				if cashval ~= 0 then
					local processed = false
					local _, success = RepeatUpdate(pd, "maindata", function(tbl)
						if tbl == nil then
							return tbl
						end
						
						for _,v in pairs(tbl["PurchaseHistory"]) do
							if v == playerProductKey then
								processed = true
							end
						end
						
						if not processed then
							table.insert(tbl["PurchaseHistory"], playerProductKey)
							tbl["cash"] = tbl["cash"] + cashval
						end
						
						return tbl
					end)
					
					if processed then
						return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.PurchaseGranted
					end
					
					if success == true then
						if data then
							data.cash.Value = data.cash.Value + cashval
							data.oldcash.Value = data.oldcash.Value + cashval
						end
					else
						game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"Cannot process your purchase yet, as ROBLOX's DataStore servers are down. Rejoin the game at a later time to receive your purchased product. Otherwise, you will automatically be refunded your ROBUX after 72 hours.", true)
						return
					end
					if player then
						game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"+$"..cashval.." RHS Cash has been added to your in-game balance!")
						game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PlayChachingSound:FireClient(player)
					end
				end
				
				local spcash
				
				if receiptInfo.ProductId == game.ReplicatedStorage.OtherDevProducts.StarterPack.Value then
					if data.Objects:FindFirstChild("318") then
						--already purchased starter pack, notify of refund
						game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player, "You have somehow purchased a second RHS Starter Pack in error. Your second purchase has not been processed; you will automatically be refunded your ROBUX after 3 days.", true)
						return
					end
					-- Award starter pack ($7500, Classy Car, furniture set)
					local cashaward = 7500
					local hashouse = true
					local objectstbl = {151,124,124,124,125,125,153,147,259,148,109,146,141,189,189,189, 70,128,127, 108,108,108,108,108,108,108,108, 135, 318} --318 is starter pack item ID
					
					if not data.Objects:FindFirstChild("278") then
						--does not have classy car
						table.insert(objectstbl, 278)
					else
						--already has it
						cashaward = cashaward + 1600
					end
					
					if not data.Objects:FindFirstChild("183") and data.GamePasses.House.Value == false then
						--does not have custom house
						table.insert(objectstbl, 183)
						hashouse = false
					end
					
					local processed = false
					local success = pcall(function()
						local update = pd:UpdateAsync("maindata",function(tbl)
							if tbl == nil then
								return tbl
							end
							
							for _,v in pairs(tbl["PurchaseHistory"]) do
								if v == playerProductKey then
									processed = true
								end
							end
							
							if not processed then
								table.insert(tbl["PurchaseHistory"], playerProductKey)
								tbl["cash"] = tbl["cash"] + cashaward
								for _,itemid in pairs(objectstbl) do
									table.insert(tbl["objects"],itemid)
								end
							end
							return tbl
						end)
					end)
					
					if processed then
						return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.PurchaseGranted
					end
					
					if success then
						if data and data:FindFirstChild("Objects") then
							for _,itemid in pairs(objectstbl) do
								local marker = Instance.new("StringValue",data.Objects)
								marker.Name = itemid
							end
							if data:FindFirstChild("cash") and data:FindFirstChild("oldcash") then
								data.cash.Value = data.cash.Value + cashaward
								data.oldcash.Value = data.oldcash.Value + cashaward
							end
							
							if player and hashouse == false and data:FindFirstChild("GamePasses") then
								--give the custom house
								data.GamePasses.House.Value = true
								game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.LoadHouseForPlayer:Fire(player)
							end
						end
						
						if player then
							if cashaward == 7500 then
								--got car too
								game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"You have received your Starter Pack containing +$"..cashaward.." RHS Cash, the Classy Car, and a set of starting furniture for your Custom House!", true)
							else
								game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"You have received your Starter Pack containing +$"..cashaward.." RHS Cash and a set of starting furniture for your Custom House!", true)
							end
							game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.PlayChachingSound:FireClient(player)
						end
						
						spcash = cashaward
					else
						game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(player,"Cannot process your purchase yet, as ROBLOX's DataStore servers are down. Rejoin the game at a later time to receive your purchased product. Otherwise, you will automatically be refunded your ROBUX after 72 hours.", true)
						return
					end
				end
				
				if data:FindFirstChild("NumPurchases") then
					local id = (cashval ~= 0 and tostring(cashval) or "StarterPack")
					data.NumPurchases.Value = data.NumPurchases.Value + 1
					GA:SendEvent(
						{
							["category"] = "business",
							["event_id"] = "Cash:Cash"..id,
							["amount"] = math.floor(math.floor(receiptInfo.CurrencySpent * .7) / 4),
							["currency"] = "USD",
							["transaction_num"] = data.NumPurchases.Value,
							["cart_type"] = data.CartType.Value
						}
					)
					
					GA:SendEvent(
						{
							["category"] = "resource",
							["event_id"] = "Source:Cash:RobuxPurchase:"..(spcash and "StarterPack" or "RHSCash"),
							["amount"] = spcash or cashval
						}
					)
				end
				
				print("Dev Product purchase completed for "..receiptInfo.PlayerId)
				return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.PurchaseGranted
				
			end
		end
	end
	
    --[[PurchaseHistory:SetAsync(playerProductKey, true)
	print("Cash purchase completed for "..receiptInfo.PlayerId)
	return Enum.ProductPurchaseDecision.PurchaseGranted]]
end

local available = game.ReplicatedStorage.DataStoresAvailable

--datastore uptime checker
coroutine.resume(coroutine.create(function()
	wait(math.random(1,90))
	while true do
		wait(math.random(30,60))
		local v
		local check = pcall(function()
			v = game:GetService("DataStoreService"):GetDataStore("Testing"):GetAsync("UptimeTest")
		end)
		
		if available.Value == false then
			if v ~= nil and check == true then
				available.Value = true
				game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireAllClients("ROBLOX appears to have fixed their server issues! Thanks for being patient. You may need to rejoin this game if you encountered loading issues.",true)
			end
		end
		
		if v == nil and check ~= true then
			available.Value = false
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireAllClients("Notice: ROBLOX's servers are having temporary issues affecting all games. Please be patient if your data/character is not loading. All of your data is safe.")
		end
		wait(160)
	end
end))

	while true do
		wait(180)
		for _,plr in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do

			if canuserchat then
				local flag

					flag = game:GetService("Chat"):CanUserChatAsync(plr.userId)
				end)
				if success then
					canuserchat.Value = flag
				end
			end
		end
	end
end))


game.OnClose = function()
	if #game.Players:GetChildren() <= 3 then
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AnalyticsPushCashEarned:Invoke()
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AnalyticsPushShopPurchases:Invoke()
		game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.AnalyticsPushTimeSpent:Invoke()
		
		GA:Post()
	end
	
	if #game.Players:GetChildren() > 1 and game.PlaceId == 9689581 then
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireAllClients("The ROBLOX High School servers are now restarting for an update! Hang tight for a few seconds.",true)
		wait(4)
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireAllClients("Teleporting...",true)
		wait(.9)
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.TeleportToUpdateServer:FireAllClients()
		for i=1,8 do
			if #game.Players:GetChildren() > 0 then
				wait(1)
			end
		end
		for _,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
			v:Kick("The ROBLOX High School servers are restarting for an update. Please rejoin the game!")
		end
	end
	if #game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:GetChildren() > 0 then
		repeat wait(.25)
			for _,v in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
				v:Kick("The ROBLOX High School servers are restarting for an update. Please rejoin the game!")
			end
		until #game.ReplicatedStorage.PlayerData:GetChildren() == 0
	end
endk<

--[[

pcall(function()
	banlist = banstore:GetAsync("IDbanlist")
end)
if banlist == nil then
	banlist = {}
end
serverbanlist = banlist
changesmade = false
for k,v in pairs(serverbanlist) do
	if (v - os.time() ) < 0 then
		print("Removing "..k.." from banlist, time expired")
		serverbanlist[k] = nil
		changesmade = true
	end
end
if changesmade == true then
	banlist = banstore:UpdateAsync("IDbanlist", function(tbl)
		tbl = serverbanlist
		return tbl
	end)
end

for i,plr in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
	for i,v in pairs(serverbanlist) do
		if plr.userId == tonumber(i) then
			plr:Kick()
		end
	end
end

	serverbanlist = tbl
	tbl = serverbanlist
	return tbl
end)

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.IsPlayerBanned.OnInvoke = function(plr)
	for k,v in pairs(serverbanlist) do
		if k == tostring(plr.userId) then
			return true
		end
	end
	return false
end

game.Players.ChildAdded:connect(function(plr)
	
	if plr.userId == 2364169 and game.PlaceId ~= 9393009 then
		plr.CharacterAdded:connect(function(char)
			wait(.5)
			plr:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
			local gui = script.CinderingBanGui:clone()
			gui.Parent = plr.PlayerGui
		end)
	end
	
	for i,v in pairs(serverbanlist) do
		if tonumber(i) == plr.userId then
			local currenttime = os.time()
			if currenttime > (serverbanlist[tostring(plr.userId)]) then
				banlist = banstore:UpdateAsync("IDbanlist", function(tbl)
					tbl[tostring(plr.userId)] = nil
					serverbanlist = tbl
					return tbl
				end)
				print(plr.Name.." has been unbanned")
			else
				plr:Kick("You are banned from the game for "..math.ceil((( v - os.time() ) / 24 / 60 / 60)).." day(s)! Please be respectful of other people; disruptive behavior is not welcome here or anywhere else on ROBLOX.")
			end
		end
	end
end)

function BanPlayer(IDtoban,days,reason,dontkick)
	local endtime = os.time() + (days * 86400)
	banlist = banstore:UpdateAsync("IDbanlist", function(tbl)
		tbl[tostring(IDtoban)] = endtime
		serverbanlist = tbl
		return tbl
	end)
	if not dontkick then
		for _,plr in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
			if plr.userId == IDtoban then
				if reason then
					plr:Kick("You have been banned from the game for "..days.." days! Reason: '"..reason.."'")
				else
					plr:Kick("You have been banned from the game for "..days.." days! Please be respectful of other people; disruptive behavior is not welcome here or anywhere else on ROBLOX.")
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

game.ServerStorage.RemoteFunctions.BanPlayer.Event:connect(function(pid,days,reason, dontkick)
	BanPlayer(pid,days,reason,dontkick)
end)

game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.BanRequest.OnServerEvent:connect(function(requester,code,targetname,days,reason,targetonserver)
	if requester.userId ~= 2364169 or code ~= 685 then
		BanPlayer(requester.userId,365,"Nice try, kiddo.")
		return
	end
	if reason == "" or reason == " " then
		reason = nil
	end
	local targetid
	local targetplr
	if targetonserver then
		for _,plr in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
			if string.lower(plr.Name) == string.lower(targetname) then
				targetid = plr.userId
				targetplr = plr
			end
		end
		if targetid == nil then
			for _,plr in pairs(game.Players:GetChildren()) do
				if string.sub(string.lower(plr.Name),1,#targetname) == string.lower(targetname) then
					targetid = plr.userId
					targetplr = plr
				end
			end
		end
		if targetplr then
			game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(requester, "[ON SERVER] Banning player "..targetplr.Name..".")
		end
	else
		targetid = game.Players:GetUserIdFromNameAsync(targetname)
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(requester, "[REMOTE] Banning player "..targetname..", ID "..targetid..".")
	end
	if targetid then
		BanPlayer(targetid,days,reason)
	else
		game.ReplicatedStorage.RemoteFunctions.NotificationForClient:FireClient(requester, "Failed to detect target.")
	end
end)
